=================== ./src/app/App.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AppProviders } from './providers';
import { AppRouter } from './router';

export const App: React.FC = () => {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter
      future={{
        v7_startTransition: true,
        v7_relativeSplatPath: true,
      }}
    >
      <AppProviders>
        <AppRouter />
      </AppProviders>
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
};

export default App;

=================== ./src/app/contexts.ts ===================
import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import type { UserSession, UserProfile, Role, ClinicWorkspace, Actor } from '../domain/session';
import type { RequestOtpResult, VerifyOtpResult } from '../services/repositories/IAuthRepository';

export interface AuthContextValue {
  user: UserProfile | null;
  role: Role | null;
  session: UserSession | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  actor: Actor | null;
  requestOtp: (phone: string) => Promise<RequestOtpResult>;
  verifyOtp: (phone: string, code: string) => Promise<VerifyOtpResult>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  updateProfileName: (name: string) => Promise<void>;
}

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

export const useAuth = (): AuthContextValue => {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AppProviders');
  return ctx;
};


export interface ClinicContextValue {
  clinic: ClinicWorkspace;
  clinicId: string;
  setClinic: (clinic: ClinicWorkspace) => void;
}

export const ClinicContext = createContext<ClinicContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

export const useClinic = (): ClinicContextValue => {
  const ctx = useContext(ClinicContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useClinic must be used within AppProviders');
  return ctx;
};

=================== ./src/app/providers.tsx ===================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { UserSession, ClinicWorkspace, Actor } from '../domain/session';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import { services } from '../services';
import { setAuthHandlers } from '../services/api/httpClient';
import {
  AuthContext,
  ClinicContext,
  type AuthContextValue,
  type ClinicContextValue,
} from './contexts';

// Central QueryClient
const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 2, // 2 minutes
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      retry: 1,
    },
  },
});

export const AppProviders: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<UserSession | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [clinic, setClinic] = useState<ClinicWorkspace>(SEED_CLINIC);

  // Connect HTTP interceptor handlers for 401 refresh / logout
  useEffect(() => {
    setAuthHandlers(
      async () => {
        try {
          const refreshed = await services.authRepo.refreshSession();
          if (refreshed?.token) {
            setSession(refreshed);
            return refreshed.token;
          }
        } catch {
          // refresh failed
        }
        return null;
      },
      () => {
        setSession(null);
        queryClient.clear();
      }
    );
  }, []);

  // Bootstrap session from repository on mount
  // Try refreshSession first so in http mode (with HttpOnly refresh cookie) the user stays logged in
  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    const bootAuth = async () => {
      try {
        let currSession: UserSession | null = null;
        try {
          currSession = await services.authRepo.refreshSession();
        } catch {
          // ignore error and try getCurrentUser
        }

        if (!currSession) {
          currSession = await services.authRepo.getCurrentUser();
        }

        if (!isMounted) return;
        if (currSession) {
          setSession(currSession);
          if (currSession.activeClinic) {
            setClinic(currSession.activeClinic);
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // no active session
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };

    void bootAuth();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const requestOtp = useCallback(async (phone: string) => {
    return services.authRepo.requestOtp(phone);
  }, []);

  const verifyOtp = useCallback(async (phone: string, code: string) => {
    const result = await services.authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, code);
    if (result.success && result.session) {
      setSession(result.session);
      if (result.session.activeClinic) {
        setClinic(result.session.activeClinic);
      }
      queryClient.clear();
    }
    return result;
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    await services.authRepo.logout();
    setSession(null);
    queryClient.clear();
  }, []);

  const updateProfileName = useCallback(async (name: string) => {
    if (!session?.user) return;
    const updatedUser = await services.authRepo.updateProfile(session.user.id, { name });
    setSession((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, user: updatedUser } : null));
  }, [session]);

  const actor = useMemo<Actor | null>(() => {
    if (!session || !session.user) return null;
    return {
      userId: session.user.id,
      userName: session.user.name,
      role: session.user.role,
      clinicId: clinic.id,
      doctorId: session.user.doctorId,
      clientId: session.user.clientId,
      phone: session.user.phone,
    };
  }, [session, clinic.id]);

  const authValue = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(
    () => ({
      user: session?.user || null,
      role: session?.user?.role || null,
      session,
      isAuthenticated: !!session?.isAuthenticated,
      isLoading,
      actor,
      requestOtp,
      verifyOtp,
      logout,
      updateProfileName,
    }),
    [session, isLoading, actor, requestOtp, verifyOtp, logout, updateProfileName]
  );

  const clinicValue = useMemo<ClinicContextValue>(
    () => ({
      clinic,
      clinicId: clinic.id,
      setClinic,
    }),
    [clinic]
  );

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <AuthContext.Provider value={authValue}>
        <ClinicContext.Provider value={clinicValue}>
          {children}
        </ClinicContext.Provider>
      </AuthContext.Provider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
};

=================== ./src/app/router.tsx ===================
import React, { Suspense, lazy } from 'react';
import { Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AppLayout } from '../components/layout/AppLayout';

import { RequireAuth } from '../components/auth/RequireAuth';
import { RequirePermission } from '../components/auth/RequirePermission';
import { RequireRole } from '../components/auth/RequireRole';

const LoginView = lazy(() => import('../features/auth/LoginView').then((m) => ({ default: m.LoginView })));
const DashboardView = lazy(() => import('../features/dashboard/DashboardView').then((m) => ({ default: m.DashboardView })));
const AppointmentsView = lazy(() => import('../features/appointments/AppointmentsView').then((m) => ({ default: m.AppointmentsView })));
const ClientsView = lazy(() => import('../features/clients/ClientsView').then((m) => ({ default: m.ClientsView })));
const LeadsView = lazy(() => import('../features/leads/LeadsView').then((m) => ({ default: m.LeadsView })));
const MessagesView = lazy(() => import('../features/messages/MessagesView').then((m) => ({ default: m.MessagesView })));
const SettingsView = lazy(() => import('../features/settings/SettingsView').then((m) => ({ default: m.SettingsView })));
const UsersView = lazy(() => import('../features/settings/UsersView').then((m) => ({ default: m.UsersView })));
const AuditLogsView = lazy(() => import('../features/settings/AuditLogsView').then((m) => ({ default: m.AuditLogsView })));
const MarketingView = lazy(() => import('../features/marketing/MarketingView').then((m) => ({ default: m.MarketingView })));
const ReportsView = lazy(() => import('../features/reports/ReportsView').then((m) => ({ default: m.ReportsView })));
const DoctorView = lazy(() => import('../features/doctor/DoctorView').then((m) => ({ default: m.DoctorView })));
const PatientView = lazy(() => import('../features/patient/PatientView').then((m) => ({ default: m.PatientView })));
const BookingView = lazy(() => import('../features/booking/BookingView').then((m) => ({ default: m.BookingView })));

const ForbiddenPage = lazy(() => import('../pages/ForbiddenPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.ForbiddenPage })));
const NotFoundPage = lazy(() => import('../pages/NotFoundPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.NotFoundPage })));

const RouteLoadingFallback: React.FC = () => (
  <div className="min-h-[50vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
    <div className="w-8 h-8 border-[3px] border-[#0f766e] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
    <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400">در حال بارگذاری صفحه...</span>
  </div>
);

export const AppRouter: React.FC = () => {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<RouteLoadingFallback />}>
      <Routes>
      {/* 1. Public Authentication Route */}
      <Route path="/login" element={<LoginView />} />

      {/* 2. Public Client-Facing Booking Wizard */}
      <Route path="/book/:clinicSlug" element={<BookingView />} />

      {/* 3. Forbidden Error Page */}
      <Route path="/403" element={<ForbiddenPage />} />

      {/* 4. Patient Portal Route */}
      <Route
        path="/me"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequireRole roles={['patient']}>
              <PatientView />
            </RequireRole>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      {/* 5. Doctor Specialized Portal Route */}
      <Route
        path="/doctor"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequireRole roles={['doctor']}>
              <AppLayout>
                <DoctorView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequireRole>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      {/* 6. Clinic Operations & Management Routes */}
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="appointments:read">
              <AppLayout>
                <DashboardView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/appointments"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="appointments:read">
              <AppLayout>
                <AppointmentsView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/clients"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="clients:read">
              <AppLayout>
                <ClientsView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/leads"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="leads:read">
              <AppLayout>
                <LeadsView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/messages"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="messages:read">
              <AppLayout>
                <MessagesView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/marketing"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="marketing:manage">
              <AppLayout>
                <MarketingView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/reports"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="reports:read">
              <AppLayout>
                <ReportsView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/settings"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="settings:manage">
              <AppLayout>
                <SettingsView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/settings/users"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="users:manage">
              <AppLayout>
                <UsersView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/settings/audit-logs"
        element={
          <RequireAuth>
            <RequirePermission permission="audit:read">
              <AppLayout>
                <AuditLogsView />
              </AppLayout>
            </RequirePermission>
          </RequireAuth>
        }
      />

      {/* 7. Fallback Route */}
      <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
    </Routes>
    </Suspense>
  );
};

=================== ./src/app/usePermissions.ts ===================
import { useAuth } from './contexts';
import {
  can as domainCan,
  canAny as domainCanAny,
  canAll as domainCanAll,
} from '../domain/permissions';
import type { Permission } from '../domain/permissions';

export const usePermissions = () => {
  const { role } = useAuth();

  return {
    role,
    can: (permission: Permission) => (role ? domainCan(role, permission) : false),
    canAny: (permissions: Permission[]) => (role ? domainCanAny(role, permissions) : false),
    canAll: (permissions: Permission[]) => (role ? domainCanAll(role, permissions) : false),
  };
};

=================== ./src/components/auth/Can.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { usePermissions } from '../../app/usePermissions';
import type { Permission } from '../../domain/permissions';

export interface CanProps {
  permission?: Permission;
  anyPermissions?: Permission[];
  allPermissions?: Permission[];
  children: React.ReactNode;
  fallback?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Can: React.FC<CanProps> = ({
  permission,
  anyPermissions,
  allPermissions,
  children,
  fallback = null,
}) => {
  const { can, canAny, canAll } = usePermissions();

  let isAllowed = true;

  if (permission && !can(permission)) {
    isAllowed = false;
  }
  if (anyPermissions && !canAny(anyPermissions)) {
    isAllowed = false;
  }
  if (allPermissions && !canAll(allPermissions)) {
    isAllowed = false;
  }

  return isAllowed ? <>{children}</> : <>{fallback}</>;
};

=================== ./src/components/auth/RequireAuth.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../app/contexts';

export const RequireAuth: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { isAuthenticated, isLoading } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#fafafa]">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 border-[3px] border-[#0f766e] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 font-medium">در حال بارگذاری اطلاعات دسترسی...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" state={{ from: location }} replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
};

=================== ./src/components/auth/RequirePermission.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { usePermissions } from '../../app/usePermissions';
import { useAuth } from '../../app/contexts';
import type { Permission } from '../../domain/permissions';

export interface RequirePermissionProps {
  permission: Permission;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const RequirePermission: React.FC<RequirePermissionProps> = ({
  permission,
  children,
}) => {
  const { isLoading } = useAuth();
  const { can } = usePermissions();

  if (isLoading) {
    return null;
  }

  if (!can(permission)) {
    return <Navigate to="/403" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
};

=================== ./src/components/auth/RequireRole.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../app/contexts';
import type { Role } from '../../domain/session';

export interface RequireRoleProps {
  roles: Role[];
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const RequireRole: React.FC<RequireRoleProps> = ({ roles, children }) => {
  const { role, isLoading } = useAuth();

  if (isLoading) {
    return null;
  }

  if (!role || !roles.includes(role)) {
    return <Navigate to="/403" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
};

=================== ./src/components/common/ErrorState.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { AlertCircle, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';

export interface ErrorStateProps {
  title?: string;
  message?: string;
  onRetry?: () => void;
  className?: string;
}

export const ErrorState: React.FC<ErrorStateProps> = ({
  title = 'خطا در بارگذاری اطلاعات',
  message = 'مشکلی در برقراری ارتباط رخ داده است. لطفاً دوباره تلاش نمایید.',
  onRetry,
  className = '',
}) => {
  return (
    <div className={`p-8 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-3 ${className}`}>
      <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center">
        <AlertCircle className="w-6 h-6" />
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-1">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">{title}</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 max-w-sm">{message}</p>
      </div>
      {onRetry && (
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onRetry} className="gap-1.5 text-xs mt-2">
          <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>تلاش مجدد</span>
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Sidebar } from './Sidebar';
import { TopBar } from './TopBar';
import { DemoBanner } from './DemoBanner';

export interface AppLayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const AppLayout: React.FC<AppLayoutProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [isMobileSidebarOpen, setIsMobileSidebarOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafafa] text-[#171717] flex">
      {/* Sidebar (Desktop fixed right + Mobile drawer) */}
      <Sidebar
        isOpenOnMobile={isMobileSidebarOpen}
        onCloseMobile={() => setIsMobileSidebarOpen(false)}
      />

      {/* Main Container */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 lg:mr-64 transition-all duration-200">
        <DemoBanner />
        <TopBar onOpenMobileSidebar={() => setIsMobileSidebarOpen(true)} />
        <main className="flex-1 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto">
          <div className="mf-page-enter">
            {children}
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/layout/DemoBanner.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import { env } from '../../config/env';

const DISMISS_KEY = 'medflow_demo_banner_dismissed';

export const DemoBanner: React.FC = () => {
  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
    return sessionStorage.getItem(DISMISS_KEY) === '1';
  });

  if (env.dataSource !== 'mock' || dismissed) return null;

  return (
    <div className="bg-amber-500/10 border-b border-amber-500/20 text-amber-900 px-4 py-1.5 text-center text-xs font-medium tracking-tight flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
      <span>نسخه‌ی نمایشی — داده‌ها فقط در مرورگر شما ذخیره می‌شوند.</span>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          sessionStorage.setItem(DISMISS_KEY, '1');
          setDismissed(true);
        }}
        className="p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-900/70 hover:text-amber-900"
        aria-label="بستن پیام نسخه نمایشی"
      >
        <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/layout/SidebarServiceStatusCard.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSidebarServiceStatus } from '../../integrations/registry';
import { Radio } from 'lucide-react';
import { Can } from '../auth/Can';

export const SidebarServiceStatusCard: React.FC = () => {
  const status = useSidebarServiceStatus();

  return (
    <div className="bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs space-y-2">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-neutral-500 font-medium">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Radio className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0f766e]" />
          وضعیت سرویس‌ها
        </span>
        <Can permission="settings:manage">
          <Link
            to="/settings"
            className="text-[#0f766e] hover:underline text-[11px]"
          >
            تنظیمات
          </Link>
        </Can>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-neutral-700">{status.bookingStatusText}</span>
          <span
            className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${status.bookingActive ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-rose-500'}`}
            title={status.bookingActive ? 'فعال' : 'غیرفعال'}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-neutral-500">{status.smsStatusText}</span>
          <span
            className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${status.smsConnected ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-amber-400'}`}
            title={status.smsConnected ? 'متصل' : 'غیرفعال'}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom';
import { MAIN_NAVIGATION } from '../../config/navigation';
import { SidebarServiceStatusCard } from './SidebarServiceStatusCard';
import { toPersianDigits } from '../../lib/format';
import { usePermissions } from '../../app/usePermissions';
import { useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { useHotLeads } from '../../features/leads/hooks/useLeads';
import { X, Stethoscope, User } from 'lucide-react';

export interface SidebarProps {
  isOpenOnMobile?: boolean;
  onCloseMobile?: () => void;
}

export const Sidebar: React.FC<SidebarProps> = ({
  isOpenOnMobile = false,
  onCloseMobile,
}) => {
  const { can, role } = usePermissions();
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();
  const { data: hotLeads = [] } = useHotLeads(clinicId);

  const visibleNavItems = MAIN_NAVIGATION.filter((item) => {
    if (!item.permission) return true;
    return can(item.permission);
  });

  return (
    <>
      {/* Mobile Backdrop */}
      {isOpenOnMobile && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm lg:hidden"
          onClick={onCloseMobile}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
      )}

      {/* Sidebar Container */}
      <aside
        className={`fixed top-0 right-0 z-40 h-screen w-64 bg-white border-l border-neutral-200 flex flex-col justify-between transition-transform duration-200 ease-in-out lg:translate-x-0 ${
          isOpenOnMobile ? 'translate-x-0' : 'translate-x-full lg:translate-x-0'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Top: Logo & Brand */}
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-[#0f766e] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-base shadow-sm">
              M
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
                مدفلو
              </h1>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 font-medium">
                سیستم رشد و عملیات کلینیک
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile close button */}
          {onCloseMobile && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onCloseMobile}
              className="p-1 rounded-lg text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 lg:hidden"
              aria-label="بستن منو"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Center: Navigation Menu */}
        <nav className="flex-1 px-3 py-4 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {/* Doctor Panel Direct Nav Link */}
          {role === 'doctor' && (
            <NavLink
              to="/doctor"
              onClick={onCloseMobile}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors mb-2 ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] font-semibold ring-1 ring-[#0f766e]'
                    : 'text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50'
                }`
              }
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Stethoscope className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
                <span>نوبت‌های تخصصی من</span>
              </div>
            </NavLink>
          )}

          {/* Patient Portal Direct Nav Link */}
          {role === 'patient' && (
            <NavLink
              to="/me"
              onClick={onCloseMobile}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors mb-2 ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] font-semibold ring-1 ring-[#0f766e]'
                    : 'text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50'
                }`
              }
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <User className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
                <span>نوبت‌های من</span>
              </div>
            </NavLink>
          )}

          {visibleNavItems.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            const isLeadsItem = item.id === 'leads';
            const badgeCount = isLeadsItem ? hotLeads.length : item.badge;
            const showBadge = badgeCount !== undefined && Number(badgeCount) > 0 && can(item.permission || 'appointments:read');

            return (
              <NavLink
                key={item.id}
                to={item.path}
                onClick={onCloseMobile}
                className={({ isActive }) =>
                  `flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] font-semibold'
                      : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-50'
                  }`
                }
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <Icon className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{item.title}</span>
                </div>

                {showBadge && (
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-800">
                    {toPersianDigits(badgeCount!)}
                  </span>
                )}
              </NavLink>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Bottom: Service Status Card */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-neutral-200">
          <SidebarServiceStatusCard />
        </div>
      </aside>
    </>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/layout/TopBar.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth, useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { useAppointments } from '../../features/appointments/hooks/useAppointments';
import { useOutboxMessages } from '../../features/messages/hooks/useMessages';
import { toPersianDigits } from '../../lib/format';
import {
  Search,
  Bell,
  ChevronDown,
  Menu,
  Building2,
  Check,
  LogOut,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ROLE_LABELS } from '../../domain/session';

export interface TopBarProps {
  onOpenMobileSidebar?: () => void;
}

export const TopBar: React.FC<TopBarProps> = ({ onOpenMobileSidebar }) => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, session, logout } = useAuth();
  const { clinic, clinicId, setClinic } = useClinic();
  const [isClinicMenuOpen, setIsClinicMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isNotifOpen, setIsNotifOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const { data: appointments = [] } = useAppointments(clinicId);
  const { data: messages = [] } = useOutboxMessages(clinicId);

  const pendingAppointmentsCount = appointments.filter(
    (a) => a.status === 'pending_confirmation'
  ).length;
  const failedMessagesCount = messages.filter((m) => m.status === 'failed').length;
  const totalNotifications = pendingAppointmentsCount + failedMessagesCount;

  const availableClinics = session?.availableClinics || [clinic];
  const hasMultipleClinics = availableClinics.length > 1;

  const handleSubmitSearch = () => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim();
    if (!q) return;
    navigate(`/appointments?search=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
  };

  const handleSearchKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSubmitSearch();
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 h-16 bg-white border-b border-neutral-200 px-4 sm:px-6 flex items-center justify-between">
      {/* Right side (in RTL): Mobile Hamburger + Workspace Switcher */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {onOpenMobileSidebar && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenMobileSidebar}
            className="p-2 rounded-lg text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-100 lg:hidden"
            aria-label="باز کردن منو"
          >
            <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        )}

        {/* Workspace Switcher or Static Title */}
        {hasMultipleClinics ? (
          <div className="relative">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsClinicMenuOpen(!isClinicMenuOpen)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-white hover:bg-neutral-50 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900 transition-colors"
              aria-expanded={isClinicMenuOpen}
            >
              <Building2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
              <span>{clinic.name}</span>
              <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400" />
            </button>

            {isClinicMenuOpen && (
              <div
                className="absolute right-0 mt-1.5 w-60 bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-lg py-1.5 z-50 animate-in fade-in"
                onClick={() => setIsClinicMenuOpen(false)}
              >
                <div className="px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-400">
                  فضاهای کاری در دسترس
                </div>
                {availableClinics.map((c) => (
                  <button
                    key={c.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setClinic(c)}
                    className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-xs text-right text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#0f766e]" />
                      <span className="font-medium">{c.name}</span>
                    </div>
                    {c.id === clinic.id && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0f766e]" />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900">
            <Building2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
            <span>{clinic.name}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Left side (in RTL): Search, Notification Bell, User Profile, Logout */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4">
        {/* Search Input */}
        <div className="relative hidden md:block w-48 lg:w-64">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSubmitSearch}
            className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3 flex items-center text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 transition-colors"
            aria-label="جستجو"
          >
            <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={handleSearchKeyDown}
            placeholder="جستجوی مراجع، نوبت..."
            className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f766e]"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Notification Bell with Dynamic Popover */}
        <div className="relative">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsNotifOpen(!isNotifOpen)}
            className="relative p-2 rounded-lg text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors"
            title="اعلان‌ها"
            aria-label="اعلان‌ها"
          >
            <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
            {totalNotifications > 0 && (
              <span className="absolute top-1 left-1 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-rose-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                {toPersianDigits(totalNotifications)}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>

          {isNotifOpen && (
            <div
              className="absolute left-0 mt-2 w-64 bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-lg py-2 z-50 text-right animate-in fade-in"
              onClick={() => setIsNotifOpen(false)}
            >
              <div className="px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-400 border-b border-neutral-100">
                اعلان‌های سیستم
              </div>
              {totalNotifications === 0 ? (
                <div className="px-3 py-3 text-xs text-neutral-500 text-center">
                  اعلان جدیدی وجود ندارد
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
                  {pendingAppointmentsCount > 0 && (
                    <Link
                      to="/appointments?status=pending_confirmation"
                      className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-xs text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50"
                    >
                      <span>نوبت‌های در انتظار تأیید</span>
                      <span className="font-bold text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px]">
                        {toPersianDigits(pendingAppointmentsCount)}
                      </span>
                    </Link>
                  )}
                  {failedMessagesCount > 0 && (
                    <Link
                      to="/messages"
                      className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-xs text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50"
                    >
                      <span>پیام‌های ناموفق در ارسال</span>
                      <span className="font-bold text-rose-600 bg-rose-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px]">
                        {toPersianDigits(failedMessagesCount)}
                      </span>
                    </Link>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* User Avatar + Profile */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pl-1 border-r border-neutral-200 pr-3">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-teal-100 text-[#0f766e] font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center">
            {user?.avatarInitials || 'ک'}
          </div>
          <div className="hidden sm:block text-right">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-900">
              {user?.name || 'کاربر سیستم'}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500">
              {user?.roleTitle || (user?.role ? ROLE_LABELS[user.role] : '')}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Logout Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={logout}
            className="p-1.5 mr-1 text-neutral-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            title="خروج از حساب کاربری"
            aria-label="خروج از حساب کاربری"
          >
            <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/ui/ButtonLink.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { Link, type LinkProps } from 'react-router-dom';

export interface ButtonLinkProps extends LinkProps {
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'outline' | 'ghost';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const ButtonLink: React.FC<ButtonLinkProps> = ({
  variant = 'primary',
  size = 'md',
  className = '',
  children,
  ...props
}) => {
  const baseStyles =
    'inline-flex items-center justify-center font-medium rounded-lg transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-1';

  const sizeStyles = {
    sm: 'text-xs px-3 py-1.5 gap-1.5',
    md: 'text-sm px-4 py-2 gap-2',
    lg: 'text-base px-5 py-2.5 gap-2.5',
  };

  const variantStyles = {
    primary:
      'bg-[#0f766e] text-white hover:bg-[#0d635d] focus:ring-[#0f766e]',
    secondary:
      'bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-800 hover:bg-neutral-200 focus:ring-neutral-400',
    outline:
      'border border-neutral-300 bg-white text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50 focus:ring-neutral-300',
    ghost:
      'bg-transparent text-neutral-600 hover:bg-neutral-100 hover:text-neutral-900 focus:ring-neutral-300',
  };

  return (
    <Link
      className={`${baseStyles} ${sizeStyles[size]} ${variantStyles[variant]} ${className}`}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/ui/Button.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';

export interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'danger';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  isLoading?: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Button: React.FC<ButtonProps> = ({
  variant = 'primary',
  size = 'md',
  className = '',
  children,
  disabled,
  isLoading,
  ...props
}) => {
  const baseStyles =
    'inline-flex items-center justify-center font-medium rounded-lg transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-1 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed';

  const sizeStyles = {
    sm: 'text-xs px-3 py-1.5 gap-1.5',
    md: 'text-sm px-4 py-2 gap-2',
    lg: 'text-base px-5 py-2.5 gap-2.5',
  };

  const variantStyles = {
    primary:
      'bg-[#0f766e] text-white hover:bg-[#0d635d] focus:ring-[#0f766e]',
    secondary:
      'bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-800 hover:bg-neutral-200 focus:ring-neutral-400',
    outline:
      'border border-neutral-300 bg-white text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50 focus:ring-neutral-300',
    ghost:
      'bg-transparent text-neutral-600 hover:bg-neutral-100 hover:text-neutral-900 focus:ring-neutral-300',
    danger:
      'bg-rose-600 text-white hover:bg-rose-700 focus:ring-rose-500',
  };

  return (
    <button
      className={`${baseStyles} ${sizeStyles[size]} ${variantStyles[variant]} ${className}`}
      disabled={disabled || isLoading}
      {...props}
    >
      {isLoading ? (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <svg className="animate-spin h-3.5 w-3.5 text-current" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" />
            <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z" />
          </svg>
          {children}
        </span>
      ) : (
        children
      )}
    </button>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/ui/captchaProvider.ts ===================
import type React from 'react';
import { env } from '../../config/env';

export interface CaptchaProps {
  verified: boolean;
  onVerify: (token: string) => void;
  error?: string;
  className?: string;
}

export interface ICaptchaProvider {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  Component: React.FC<CaptchaProps>;
}

export type CaptchaRegistry = Record<string, ICaptchaProvider>;

const providers: CaptchaRegistry = {};

export const registerCaptchaProvider = (provider: ICaptchaProvider): void => {
  providers[provider.id] = provider;
};

export const getCaptchaProvider = (providerName: string = env.captchaProvider): ICaptchaProvider | undefined => {
  return providers[providerName] || providers['mock'];
};

=================== ./src/components/ui/Captcha.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Shield, Check, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import type { CaptchaProps } from './captchaProvider';
import { registerCaptchaProvider, getCaptchaProvider } from './captchaProvider';

export type { CaptchaProps } from './captchaProvider';

const MockCaptchaComponent: React.FC<CaptchaProps> = ({
  verified,
  onVerify,
  error,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const [isVerifying, setIsVerifying] = useState(false);

  const handleClick = () => {
    if (verified || isVerifying) return;
    setIsVerifying(true);
    // Simulate brief turnstile challenge verification
    setTimeout(() => {
      setIsVerifying(false);
      const mockToken = `mock-turnstile-${Date.now()}`;
      onVerify(mockToken);
    }, 350);
  };

  return (
    <div className={`space-y-1 ${className}`}>
      <div
        onClick={handleClick}
        role="button"
        tabIndex={0}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
            e.preventDefault();
            handleClick();
          }
        }}
        className={`p-3 bg-neutral-50 border rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between text-xs cursor-pointer select-none transition-all ${
          verified
            ? 'border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50/40 ring-1 ring-emerald-200'
            : error
            ? 'border-rose-300 bg-rose-50/30'
            : 'border-neutral-200 hover:border-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-100/50'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div
            className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-md border flex items-center justify-center transition-colors ${
              verified
                ? 'bg-emerald-600 border-emerald-600 text-white'
                : 'bg-white border-neutral-300'
            }`}
          >
            {isVerifying ? (
              <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-[#0f766e]" />
            ) : verified ? (
              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[3]" />
            ) : null}
          </div>
          <span className="text-neutral-800 font-medium">
            {verified ? 'تأیید هویت امنیتی با موفقیت انجام شد' : 'من ربات نیستم (تأیید امنیتی مراجع)'}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
          <Shield className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${verified ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-[#0f766e]'}`} />
          <span>Cloudflare Turnstile</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      {error && <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-600 mt-1">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
};

// Register default mock provider
registerCaptchaProvider({
  id: 'mock',
  name: 'Mock Turnstile Captcha',
  Component: MockCaptchaComponent,
});

export const Captcha: React.FC<CaptchaProps> = (props) => {
  const provider = getCaptchaProvider();
  const Component = provider?.Component || MockCaptchaComponent;
  return <Component {...props} />;
};

=================== ./src/components/ui/Modal.tsx ===================
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

export interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  footer?: React.ReactNode;
  maxWidth?: string;
}

export const Modal: React.FC<ModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  children,
  footer,
  maxWidth = 'max-w-lg',
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm mf-overlay-in"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="modal-title"
    >
      <div
        className={`w-full ${maxWidth} bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] mf-modal-in`}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">
            {title}
          </h2>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors"
            aria-label="بستن پنجره"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1">{children}</div>

        {footer && (
          <div className="px-6 py-3.5 bg-neutral-50 border-t border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
            {footer}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/components/ui/StatCard.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { Card } from './Card';
import {
  Calendar,
  Flame,
  AlertTriangle,
  MessageCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';

const ICON_MAP: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  calendar: Calendar,
  flame: Flame,
  'alert-triangle': AlertTriangle,
  'message-circle': MessageCircle,
};

export interface StatCardProps {
  metric: {
    id: string;
    label: string;
    value: string | number;
    delta?: string;
    hint?: string;
    iconName?: 'calendar' | 'flame' | 'alert-triangle' | 'message-circle';
  };
  className?: string;
}

export const StatCard: React.FC<StatCardProps> = ({ metric, className = '' }) => {
  const IconComponent = (metric.iconName && ICON_MAP[metric.iconName]) || Calendar;

  return (
    <Card className={`flex flex-col justify-between ${className}`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-neutral-500">
          {metric.label}
        </span>
        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-teal-50/70 text-[#0f766e] flex items-center justify-center">
          <IconComponent className="w-4 h-4" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight">
          {metric.value}
        </span>
      </div>

      {(metric.delta || metric.hint) && (
        <div className="mt-2 text-xs">
          {metric.delta && (
            <span className="text-[#0f766e] font-medium">
              {metric.delta}
            </span>
          )}
          {metric.hint && (
            <span className="text-neutral-500">
              {metric.hint}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
};

=================== ./src/domain/appointmentStatus.ts ===================
export type AppointmentStatus =
  | 'pending_confirmation'
  | 'confirmed'
  | 'pending_payment'
  | 'in_clinic'
  | 'completed'
  | 'cancelled';

export const AppointmentStatus = {
  PENDING_CONFIRMATION: 'pending_confirmation',
  CONFIRMED: 'confirmed',
  PENDING_PAYMENT: 'pending_payment',
  IN_CLINIC: 'in_clinic',
  COMPLETED: 'completed',
  CANCELLED: 'cancelled',
} as const;

export interface StatusMeta {
  label: string;
  bgClass: string;
  textClass: string;
  dotClass: string;
}

export const STATUS_META: Record<AppointmentStatus, StatusMeta> = {
  pending_confirmation: {
    label: 'در انتظار تأیید',
    bgClass: 'bg-neutral-100',
    textClass: 'text-neutral-700',
    dotClass: 'bg-neutral-400',
  },
  confirmed: {
    label: 'تأیید شده',
    bgClass: 'bg-teal-50',
    textClass: 'text-teal-800',
    dotClass: 'bg-[#0f766e]',
  },
  pending_payment: {
    label: 'در انتظار پرداخت',
    bgClass: 'bg-amber-50',
    textClass: 'text-amber-800',
    dotClass: 'bg-amber-500',
  },
  in_clinic: {
    label: 'در کلینیک',
    bgClass: 'bg-sky-50',
    textClass: 'text-sky-800',
    dotClass: 'bg-sky-500',
  },
  completed: {
    label: 'تکمیل شده',
    bgClass: 'bg-emerald-50',
    textClass: 'text-emerald-800',
    dotClass: 'bg-emerald-600',
  },
  cancelled: {
    label: 'لغو شده',
    bgClass: 'bg-rose-50',
    textClass: 'text-rose-800',
    dotClass: 'bg-rose-500',
  },
};

/**
 * State Transition Matrix for Appointments.
 */
const ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Record<AppointmentStatus, AppointmentStatus[]> = {
  pending_confirmation: ['confirmed', 'cancelled'],
  confirmed: ['in_clinic', 'pending_payment', 'cancelled'],
  pending_payment: ['in_clinic', 'confirmed', 'completed', 'cancelled'],
  in_clinic: ['completed', 'pending_payment', 'cancelled'],
  completed: [],
  cancelled: [],
};

/**
 * Role-specific allowed transitions.
 */
import type { Role } from './session';

const ROLE_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Record<Role, Partial<Record<AppointmentStatus, AppointmentStatus[]>>> = {
  owner: ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS,
  admin: ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS,
  reception: {
    pending_confirmation: ['confirmed', 'cancelled'],
    confirmed: ['in_clinic', 'pending_payment', 'cancelled'],
    pending_payment: ['in_clinic', 'confirmed', 'completed', 'cancelled'],
    in_clinic: ['completed', 'pending_payment', 'cancelled'],
    completed: [],
    cancelled: [],
  },
  doctor: {
    confirmed: ['in_clinic'],
    in_clinic: ['completed'],
    pending_confirmation: [],
    pending_payment: [],
    completed: [],
    cancelled: [],
  },
  patient: {
    pending_confirmation: ['cancelled'],
    confirmed: ['cancelled'],
    pending_payment: ['cancelled'],
    in_clinic: [],
    completed: [],
    cancelled: [],
  },
};

/**
 * Checks whether transitioning from one status to another is permitted generally.
 */
export const canTransition = (
  from: AppointmentStatus,
  to: AppointmentStatus,
  role?: Role
): boolean => {
  if (role) {
    return canTransitionByRole(from, to, role);
  }
  if (from === to) return false;
  const allowed = ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[from];
  return allowed ? allowed.includes(to) : false;
};

/**
 * Checks whether transitioning from one status to another is permitted for a specific role.
 */
export const canTransitionByRole = (
  from: AppointmentStatus,
  to: AppointmentStatus,
  role: Role
): boolean => {
  if (from === to) return false;
  const roleTransitions = ROLE_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[role] || ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS;
  const allowed = roleTransitions[from];
  return allowed ? allowed.includes(to) : false;
};

/**
 * Returns the list of permitted next statuses for an appointment.
 */
export const getAvailableTransitions = (
  status: AppointmentStatus
): AppointmentStatus[] => {
  return ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[status] || [];
};

/**
 * Returns the list of permitted next statuses for a specific role.
 */
export const getAvailableTransitionsByRole = (
  status: AppointmentStatus,
  role: Role
): AppointmentStatus[] => {
  const roleTransitions = ROLE_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[role] || ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS;
  return roleTransitions[status] || [];
};

=================== ./src/domain/permissions.ts ===================
import type { Role } from './session';

export type Permission =
  | 'appointments:read'
  | 'appointments:read_own'
  | 'appointments:create'
  | 'appointments:update_status'
  | 'clients:read'
  | 'clients:read_own'
  | 'leads:read'
  | 'leads:manage'
  | 'messages:read'
  | 'messages:send'
  | 'marketing:manage'
  | 'reports:read'
  | 'reports:financial'
  | 'settings:manage'
  | 'users:manage'
  | 'medical_notes:read'
  | 'medical_notes:write'
  | 'audit:read';

export const ROLE_PERMISSIONS: Record<Role, readonly Permission[]> = {
  owner: [
    'appointments:read',
    'appointments:read_own',
    'appointments:create',
    'appointments:update_status',
    'clients:read',
    'clients:read_own',
    'leads:read',
    'leads:manage',
    'messages:read',
    'messages:send',
    'marketing:manage',
    'reports:read',
    'reports:financial',
    'settings:manage',
    'users:manage',
    'medical_notes:read',
    'medical_notes:write',
    'audit:read',
  ],
  admin: [
    'appointments:read',
    'appointments:read_own',
    'appointments:create',
    'appointments:update_status',
    'clients:read',
    'clients:read_own',
    'leads:read',
    'leads:manage',
    'messages:read',
    'messages:send',
    'marketing:manage',
    'reports:read',
    'settings:manage',
    'users:manage',
  ],
  reception: [
    'appointments:read',
    'appointments:create',
    'appointments:update_status',
    'clients:read',
    'leads:read',
    'leads:manage',
    'messages:read',
    'messages:send',
  ],
  doctor: [
    'appointments:read_own',
    'appointments:update_status',
    'clients:read_own',
    'medical_notes:read',
    'medical_notes:write',
  ],
  patient: [
    'appointments:read_own',
    'appointments:update_status',
    'clients:read_own',
  ],
};

/**
 * Checks if a given role has a specific permission.
 */
export function can(role: Role, permission: Permission): boolean {
  const permissions = ROLE_PERMISSIONS[role];
  return permissions ? permissions.includes(permission) : false;
}

/**
 * Checks if a role has at least one of the specified permissions.
 */
export function canAny(role: Role, permissions: Permission[]): boolean {
  return permissions.some((perm) => can(role, perm));
}

/**
 * Checks if a role has all of the specified permissions.
 */
export function canAll(role: Role, permissions: Permission[]): boolean {
  return permissions.every((perm) => can(role, perm));
}
