=================== ./src/domain/slot.ts ===================
import type { Doctor } from './doctor';
import type { Appointment } from './appointment';
import { toPersianDigits, toEnglishDigits } from '../lib/format';

export interface TimeSlot {
  id: string;
  time: string; // Persian formatted: "۰۹:۰۰"
  rawTime: string; // "09:00"
  available: boolean;
  reason?: string;
}

export interface CalculateSlotsParams {
  doctor: Doctor;
  serviceDurationMinutes: number;
  existingAppointments: Appointment[];
  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
}

/**
 * Calculates available time slots for a doctor on a specific date based on working hours,
 * service duration, and existing appointments (excluding cancelled ones).
 */
export const calculateAvailableSlots = (params: CalculateSlotsParams): TimeSlot[] => {
  const { doctor, serviceDurationMinutes, existingAppointments, date } = params;
  const slots: TimeSlot[] = [];

  // Filter existing active appointments on this date for this doctor
  const activeBookings = existingAppointments.filter((apt) => {
    return (
      apt.doctorId === doctor.id &&
      apt.date === date &&
      apt.status !== 'cancelled'
    );
  });

  const startMinutes = doctor.startHour * 60; // e.g. 9 * 60 = 540
  const endMinutes = doctor.endHour * 60; // e.g. 20 * 60 = 1200
  
  // Step forward using a dynamic common step so candidate start times are properly granular
  const stepMinutes = Math.min(30, doctor.slotIntervalMinutes || 30);

  let currentMinute = startMinutes;

  while (currentMinute + serviceDurationMinutes <= endMinutes) {
    const hours = Math.floor(currentMinute / 60);
    const mins = currentMinute % 60;
    const rawTime = `${String(hours).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const persianTime = toPersianDigits(rawTime);

    const slotStart = currentMinute;
    const slotEnd = currentMinute + serviceDurationMinutes;

    // Check collision with any active appointment
    const hasConflict = activeBookings.some((apt) => {
      // Normalize apt.time from Persian or English
      const aptRawTime = toEnglishDigits(apt.time).trim();
      const [aptH, aptM] = aptRawTime.split(':').map(Number);
      const aptStart = aptH * 60 + aptM;
      const aptEnd = aptStart + (apt.durationMinutes || 30);

      // Overlap condition: slotStart < aptEnd and slotEnd > aptStart
      return slotStart < aptEnd && slotEnd > aptStart;
    });

    slots.push({
      id: `slot-${doctor.id}-${rawTime.replace(':', '')}`,
      time: persianTime,
      rawTime,
      available: !hasConflict,
      reason: hasConflict ? 'رزرو شده' : undefined,
    });

    currentMinute += stepMinutes;
  }

  return slots;
};

=================== ./src/features/appointments/AppointmentsView.tsx ===================
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { usePermissions } from '../../app/usePermissions';
import { useAppointments, useUpdateAppointmentStatus } from './hooks/useAppointments';
import type { AppointmentStatus } from '../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import { STATUS_META, getAvailableTransitionsByRole } from '../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import { getInitials, formatTimeFa, formatTrackingCodeFa, formatPhoneNumber, toPersianDigits } from '../../lib/format';
import { formatIsoToJalali, getTodayDateString, getTomorrowDateString } from '../../lib/jalali';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Skeleton } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { ErrorState } from '../../components/common/ErrorState';
import { Search, Filter, MoreVertical, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft } from 'lucide-react';

import { useDebounce } from '../../lib/useDebounce';
import { useClickOutside } from '../../lib/useClickOutside';

type DateFilterTab = 'all' | 'today' | 'tomorrow';

export const AppointmentsView: React.FC = () => {
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();
  const { can, role } = usePermissions();
  const canUpdateStatus = can('appointments:update_status');
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const [search, setSearch] = useState(() => searchParams.get('search') || '');
  const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(search, 300);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<AppointmentStatus | undefined>(
    () => (searchParams.get('status') as AppointmentStatus) || undefined
  );
  const [dateFilter, setDateFilter] = useState<DateFilterTab>(() => {
    const urlDate = searchParams.get('date');
    return urlDate === 'tomorrow' ? 'tomorrow' : urlDate === 'today' ? 'today' : 'all';
  });
  const [activeMenuId, setActiveMenuId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useClickOutside(menuRef, () => setActiveMenuId(null), activeMenuId !== null);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const pageSize = 10;

  const { data: appointments = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useAppointments(clinicId, {
    search: debouncedSearch,
    status: statusFilter,
  });

  const prevStatusesRef = React.useRef<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [highlightedRowIds, setHighlightedRowIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (appointments.length === 0) return;
    const newHighlights: string[] = [];
    const prev = prevStatusesRef.current;
    const nextStatuses: Record<string, string> = {};

    appointments.forEach((apt) => {
      nextStatuses[apt.id] = apt.status;
      if (prev[apt.id] && prev[apt.id] !== apt.status) {
        newHighlights.push(apt.id);
      }
    });

    prevStatusesRef.current = nextStatuses;

    if (newHighlights.length > 0) {
      setHighlightedRowIds((current) => {
        const updated = { ...current };
        newHighlights.forEach((id) => {
          updated[id] = true;
        });
        return updated;
      });

      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        setHighlightedRowIds((current) => {
          const updated = { ...current };
          newHighlights.forEach((id) => {
            delete updated[id];
          });
          return updated;
        });
      }, 900);

      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [appointments]);

  const updateStatusMutation = useUpdateAppointmentStatus(clinicId);

  const todayDateStr = getTodayDateString();
  const tomorrowDateStr = getTomorrowDateString();

  const filteredAppointments = appointments.filter((apt) => {
    if (dateFilter === 'today' && apt.date !== todayDateStr) return false;
    if (dateFilter === 'tomorrow' && apt.date !== tomorrowDateStr) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(filteredAppointments.length / pageSize) || 1;
  const paginatedAppointments = filteredAppointments.slice(
    (currentPage - 1) * pageSize,
    currentPage * pageSize
  );

  if (isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetch()} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight">
            مدیریت نوبت‌ها
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-1">
            مشاهده، فیلتر و مدیریت وضعیت تمامی نوبت‌های ثبت‌شده
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters Bar */}
      <Card className="p-4 flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-3 w-full lg:w-auto">
          {/* Search Input */}
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-72">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto text-neutral-400 pointer-events-none" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setSearch(e.target.value);
                setCurrentPage(1);
              }}
              placeholder="جستجوی نام یا تلفن مراجع..."
              className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f766e]"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Date Filter Tabs */}
          <div className="flex items-center rounded-lg bg-neutral-100 p-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-600 w-full sm:w-auto justify-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setDateFilter('today');
                setCurrentPage(1);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md transition-colors ${dateFilter === 'today'
                ? 'bg-white text-neutral-900 shadow-sm font-semibold'
                : 'hover:text-neutral-900'
                }`}
            >
              امروز
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setDateFilter('tomorrow');
                setCurrentPage(1);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md transition-colors ${dateFilter === 'tomorrow'
                ? 'bg-white text-neutral-900 shadow-sm font-semibold'
                : 'hover:text-neutral-900'
                }`}
            >
              فردا
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setDateFilter('all');
                setCurrentPage(1);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md transition-colors ${dateFilter === 'all'
                ? 'bg-white text-neutral-900 shadow-sm font-semibold'
                : 'hover:text-neutral-900'
                }`}
            >
              همه
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Status Dropdown */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full lg:w-auto justify-end">
          <Filter className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 shrink-0" />
          <select
            value={statusFilter || ''}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setStatusFilter((e.target.value as AppointmentStatus) || undefined);
              setCurrentPage(1);
            }}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-200 rounded-lg bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
          >
            <option value="">همه وضعیت‌ها</option>
            {Object.entries(STATUS_META).map(([key, meta]) => (
              <option key={key} value={key}>
                {meta.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Appointments Table */}
      <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden">
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="p-6 space-y-3">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 w-full" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-right border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/60 text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">تاریخ و ساعت</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">مراجع</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">خدمت</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">متخصص</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">کد پیگیری</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">وضعیت</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-center">عملیات</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200 text-sm text-neutral-900">
                {paginatedAppointments.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={7} className="py-12 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500">
                      هیچ نوبتی با این مشخصات یافت نشد.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  paginatedAppointments.map((apt) => {
                    const meta = STATUS_META[apt.status];
                    const transitions = role ? getAvailableTransitionsByRole(apt.status, role) : [];
                    const isMenuOpen = activeMenuId === apt.id;
                    const isHighlighted = !!highlightedRowIds[apt.id];

                    return (
                      <tr
                        key={apt.id}
                        className={`h-14 hover:bg-neutral-50/60 transition-colors ${isHighlighted ? 'mf-row-highlight' : ''
                          }`}
                      >
                        <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap text-xs sm:text-sm">
                          <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 block">{formatTimeFa(apt.time)}</span>
                          <span className="text-neutral-500 text-[11px]">{formatIsoToJalali(apt.date)}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                              {getInitials(apt.clientName)}
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <div className="font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm">{apt.clientName}</div>
                              <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 font-mono">{formatPhoneNumber(apt.clientPhone)}</div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 text-neutral-700 text-xs sm:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">
                          {apt.serviceName}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 text-neutral-700 text-xs sm:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">
                          {apt.doctorName}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {formatTrackingCodeFa(apt.trackingCode)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${meta.bgClass} ${meta.textClass}`}>
                            <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${meta.dotClass}`} />
                            <span>{meta.label}</span>
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center relative">
                          {canUpdateStatus && apt.status === 'in_clinic' && (role === 'reception' || role === 'owner' || role === 'admin') ? (
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                              <Button
                                size="sm"
                                variant="primary"
                                onClick={() => updateStatusMutation.mutate({ id: apt.id, newStatus: 'completed' })}
                                className="text-xs px-2 py-1 h-7 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700"
                                title="تکمیل و خروج مراجع"
                              >
                                <span>تکمیل و تسویه</span>
                              </Button>
                              {transitions.length > 0 && (
                                <div
                                  ref={isMenuOpen ? menuRef : undefined}
                                  className="relative inline-block text-right"
                                >
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setActiveMenuId(isMenuOpen ? null : apt.id)}
                                    className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors"
                                  >
                                    <MoreVertical className="w-4 h-4" />
                                  </button>
                                  {isMenuOpen && (
                                    <div
                                      className="absolute left-0 mt-1 w-36 bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-lg py-1 z-50 text-xs animate-in fade-in"
                                      onClick={() => setActiveMenuId(null)}
                                    >
                                      {transitions.map((nextStatus) => (
                                        <button
                                          key={nextStatus}
                                          type="button"
                                          onClick={() => updateStatusMutation.mutate({ id: apt.id, newStatus: nextStatus })}
                                          className="w-full text-right px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-700"
                                        >
                                          تغییر به: {STATUS_META[nextStatus].label}
                                        </button>
                                      ))}
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : canUpdateStatus && transitions.length > 0 ? (
                            <div
                              ref={isMenuOpen ? menuRef : undefined}
                              className="relative inline-block text-right"
                            >
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setActiveMenuId(isMenuOpen ? null : apt.id)}
                                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors"
                              >
                                <MoreVertical className="w-4 h-4" />
                              </button>
                              {isMenuOpen && (
                                <div
                                  className="absolute flex flex-col left-0 mt-1 w-36 bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-lg py-1 z-50 text-xs animate-in fade-in"
                                  onClick={() => setActiveMenuId(null)}
                                >
                                  {transitions.map((nextStatus) => (
                                    <button
                                      key={nextStatus}
                                      type="button"
                                      onClick={() => updateStatusMutation.mutate({ id: apt.id, newStatus: nextStatus })}
                                      className="w-full text-right px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-700"
                                    >
                                      تغییر به: {STATUS_META[nextStatus].label}
                                    </button>
                                  ))}
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-neutral-300 text-xs">—</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Client-Side Pagination */}
        {filteredAppointments.length > pageSize && (
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-neutral-600 bg-neutral-50/50">
            <div>
              نمایش {toPersianDigits((currentPage - 1) * pageSize + 1)} تا{' '}
              {toPersianDigits(Math.min(currentPage * pageSize, filteredAppointments.length))} از{' '}
              {toPersianDigits(filteredAppointments.length)} نوبت
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => setCurrentPage((p) => Math.max(p - 1, 1))}
                disabled={currentPage === 1}
                className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs gap-1"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>قبلی</span>
              </Button>
              <span className="font-medium text-neutral-800">
                صفحه {toPersianDigits(currentPage)} از {toPersianDigits(totalPages)}
              </span>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => setCurrentPage((p) => Math.min(p + 1, totalPages))}
                disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs gap-1"
              >
                <span>بعدی</span>
                <ChevronLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/appointments/hooks/useAppointments.ts ===================
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient, type QueryKey } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../../services/queryKeys';
import type { Appointment, CreateAppointmentInput } from '../../../domain/appointment';
import type { AppointmentStatus } from '../../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import type { AppointmentFilters } from '../../../services/repositories/IAppointmentRepository';
import { useAuth } from '../../../app/contexts';

export const useAppointments = (clinicId: string, filters?: AppointmentFilters) => {
  const { actor } = useAuth();

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.list(clinicId, filters as Record<string, unknown>),
    queryFn: () => services.appointmentRepo.getAppointments(clinicId, filters, actor || undefined),
  });
};

export const useTodayQueue = (clinicId: string, limit: number = 5) => {
  const { actor } = useAuth();

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.todayQueue(clinicId, actor?.userId),
    queryFn: () => services.appointmentRepo.getTodayQueue(clinicId, limit, actor || undefined),
  });
};

export const useUpdateAppointmentStatus = (clinicId: string) => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { actor } = useAuth();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, newStatus }: { id: string; newStatus: AppointmentStatus }) =>
      services.appointmentRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, id, newStatus, actor || undefined),

    // Optimistic Update
    onMutate: async ({ id, newStatus }) => {
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId) });

      const snapshots: Array<{ key: QueryKey; data: Appointment[] }> = [];
      const cache = queryClient.getQueryCache().findAll({
        queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId),
      });

      for (const query of cache) {
        const data = query.state.data;
        if (Array.isArray(data)) {
          snapshots.push({ key: query.queryKey, data: data as Appointment[] });
          queryClient.setQueryData<Appointment[]>(
            query.queryKey,
            (data as Appointment[]).map((apt) =>
              apt.id === id ? { ...apt, status: newStatus } : apt
            )
          );
        }
      }

      return { snapshots };
    },

    // Rollback on error
    onError: (_err, _vars, context) => {
      context?.snapshots.forEach(({ key, data }) => {
        queryClient.setQueryData(key, data);
      });
    },

    // Re-sync server/mock state
    onSettled: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.dashboard.summary(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.messages.outbox(clinicId) });
    },
  });
};

export const useCreateAppointment = (clinicId: string) => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { actor } = useAuth();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: CreateAppointmentInput) =>
      services.appointmentRepo.createAppointment(clinicId, input, actor || undefined),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.dashboard.summary(clinicId) });
    },
  });
};

=================== ./src/features/auth/LoginView.tsx ===================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useLocation, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { Phone, KeyRound, ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, RefreshCw, AlertCircle, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { useAuth } from '../../app/contexts';
import { isValidIranianMobile, normalizePhoneNumber } from '../../lib/phone';
import { toPersianDigits } from '../../lib/format';
import { SEED_USERS } from '../../mocks/seed';

import { can } from '../../domain/permissions';
import type { Role } from '../../domain/session';
import { env } from '../../config/env';

const phoneSchema = z.string().refine((val) => isValidIranianMobile(val), {
  message: 'شماره تلفن همراه معتبر نمی‌باشد (فرمت ۰۹۱۲...).',
});

const otpSchema = z.string().regex(/^\d{6}$/, {
  message: 'کد تأیید باید یک عدد ۶ رقمی باشد.',
});

function isRouteAllowedForRole(pathname: string, userRole: Role): boolean {
  if (pathname === '/me') return userRole === 'patient';
  if (pathname === '/doctor') return userRole === 'doctor';
  if (pathname === '/settings/audit-logs') return can(userRole, 'audit:read');
  if (pathname === '/settings/users') return can(userRole, 'users:manage');
  if (pathname.startsWith('/settings')) return can(userRole, 'settings:manage');
  if (pathname.startsWith('/reports')) return can(userRole, 'reports:read');
  if (pathname.startsWith('/marketing')) return can(userRole, 'marketing:manage');
  if (pathname.startsWith('/messages')) return can(userRole, 'messages:read');
  if (pathname.startsWith('/leads')) return can(userRole, 'leads:read');
  if (pathname.startsWith('/clients')) return can(userRole, 'clients:read');
  if (pathname.startsWith('/appointments') || pathname === '/') {
    return can(userRole, 'appointments:read');
  }
  return true;
}

function getDefaultLandingForRole(userRole: Role): string {
  if (userRole === 'doctor') return '/doctor';
  if (userRole === 'patient') return '/me';
  return '/';
}

export const LoginView: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const { requestOtp, verifyOtp, isAuthenticated, role } = useAuth();

  const [step, setStep] = useState<'phone' | 'otp'>('phone');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [otpCode, setOtpCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(120);

  // If already authenticated, redirect
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAuthenticated && role) {
      const from = (location.state as { from?: { pathname?: string } })?.from?.pathname;
      const targetPath =
        from && from !== '/login' && isRouteAllowedForRole(from, role)
          ? from
          : getDefaultLandingForRole(role);
      navigate(targetPath, { replace: true });
    }
  }, [isAuthenticated, role, navigate, location.state]);

  // Timer countdown
  useEffect(() => {
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;
    if (step === 'otp' && countdown > 0) {
      timer = setInterval(() => {
        setCountdown((prev) => prev - 1);
      }, 1000);
    }
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [step, countdown]);

  const handlePhoneSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    const parseResult = phoneSchema.safeParse(phone);
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      setError(parseResult.error.issues[0].message);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await requestOtp(phone);
      if (res.success) {
        setStep('otp');
        setCountdown(res.expiresInSeconds || 120);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ارسال کد تأیید.';
      setError(msg);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOtpSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    const parseResult = otpSchema.safeParse(otpCode.trim());
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      setError(parseResult.error.issues[0].message);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await verifyOtp(phone, otpCode.trim());
      if (!res.success) {
        setError(res.error || 'کد تأیید اشتباه است.');
      } else if (res.session?.user) {
        const userRole = res.session.user.role;
        const from = (location.state as { from?: { pathname?: string } })?.from?.pathname;
        const targetPath =
          from && from !== '/login' && isRouteAllowedForRole(from, userRole)
            ? from
            : getDefaultLandingForRole(userRole);
        navigate(targetPath, { replace: true });
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در اعتبارسنجی کد.';
      setError(msg);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleResend = async () => {
    if (countdown > 0) return;
    setError(null);
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await requestOtp(phone);
      if (res.success) {
        setCountdown(res.expiresInSeconds || 120);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ارسال مجدد کد.';
      setError(msg);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const fillSampleUser = (samplePhone: string) => {
    setPhone(samplePhone);
    setError(null);
  };

  const formatCountdown = (seconds: number) => {
    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const secs = seconds % 60;
    return `${toPersianDigits(mins)}:${toPersianDigits(String(secs).padStart(2, '0'))}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafafa] flex flex-col justify-center py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md text-center mb-6">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-[#0f766e] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xl mx-auto shadow-md shadow-teal-900/10 mb-3">
          M
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900">
          ورود به سامانه‌ی مدفلو
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-500">
          سیستم رشد و مدیریت عملیات کلینیک زیبایی آریانا
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md px-4">
        <Card className="p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6 shadow-sm border-neutral-200">
          {error && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-start gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-rose-600" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {step === 'phone' ? (
            <form onSubmit={handlePhoneSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="phoneInput"
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1.5"
                >
                  شماره تلفن همراه <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Phone className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto pointer-events-none text-neutral-400" />
                  <input
                    id="phoneInput"
                    type="tel"
                    dir="ltr"
                    required
                    autoFocus
                    value={phone}
                    onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="۰۹۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"
                    className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm text-right border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0f766e] focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white transition-colors"
                  />
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 mt-1.5">
                  کد تأیید ۶ رقمی یکبار مصرف به این شماره پیامک خواهد شد.
                </p>
              </div>

              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="primary"
                size="md"
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              >
                {isLoading ? (
                  <span>در حال ارسال کد...</span>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span>دریافت کد ورود</span>
                    <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </form>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleOtpSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs pb-2 border-b border-neutral-100">
                <span className="text-neutral-500">
                  ارسال شده به: <strong className="text-neutral-800 font-mono" dir="ltr">{toPersianDigits(normalizePhoneNumber(phone))}</strong>
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setStep('phone');
                    setError(null);
                  }}
                  className="text-[#0f766e] hover:underline font-medium text-[11px]"
                >
                  ویرایش شماره
                </button>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                  <label
                    htmlFor="otpInput"
                    className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800"
                  >
                    کد تأیید ۶ رقمی <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                  </label>
                  {Boolean(import.meta.env.DEV && env.dataSource === 'mock') && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-teal-200">
                      کد تستی: ۱۲۳۴۵۶
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="relative">
                  <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto pointer-events-none text-neutral-400" />
                  <input
                    id="otpInput"
                    type="text"
                    dir="ltr"
                    required
                    autoFocus
                    maxLength={6}
                    value={otpCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setOtpCode(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="۱۲۳۴۵۶"
                    className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2.5 text-center tracking-[0.3em] font-mono text-sm sm:text-base border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0f766e] focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white transition-colors"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs pt-1">
                {countdown > 0 ? (
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">
                    امکان ارسال مجدد تا {formatCountdown(countdown)} دیگر
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleResend}
                    disabled={isLoading}
                    className="text-[#0f766e] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 hover:underline"
                  >
                    <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>ارسال مجدد کد تأیید</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="primary"
                size="md"
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              >
                {isLoading ? (
                  <span>در حال اعتبارسنجی...</span>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span>ورود به سامانه</span>
                    <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </form>
          )}

          {/* Quick Account Switcher for Demo / Dev */}
          {Boolean(import.meta.env.DEV && env.dataSource === 'mock') && (
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-neutral-200 space-y-2.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0f766e]" />
                <span>انتخاب سریع حساب‌های تستی (شبیه‌ساز نقش‌ها):</span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
                {SEED_USERS.map((u) => (
                  <button
                    key={u.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => fillSampleUser(u.phone)}
                    className="text-right p-2 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 hover:bg-neutral-100 hover:border-teal-300 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800 block truncate">
                      {u.name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500 block truncate">
                      {u.roleTitle} ({toPersianDigits(u.phone)})
                    </span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>

        {/* Public Booking Link */}
        <div className="mt-4 text-center">
          <Link
            to="/book/ariana"
            className="text-xs text-neutral-600 hover:text-[#0f766e] inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-medium transition-colors"
          >
            <span>صفحه عمومی ثبت نوبت آنلاین مراجعان</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/automations/hooks/useAutomations.ts ===================
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../../services/queryKeys';

export const useAutomations = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.automations.all(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.automationRepo.getAutomations(clinicId),
  });
};

export const useAutomationStats = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.automations.stats(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.automationRepo.getStats(clinicId),
  });
};

export const useSetAutomationEnabled = (clinicId: string) => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, enabled }: { id: string; enabled: boolean }) =>
      services.automationRepo.setEnabled(clinicId, id, enabled),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.automations.all(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.automations.stats(clinicId) });
    },
  });
};

=================== ./src/features/booking/BookingView.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../services/queryKeys';
import { BookingIntro } from './components/BookingIntro';
import { BookingWizard } from './BookingWizard';
import { ShieldCheck, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { DemoBanner } from '../../components/layout/DemoBanner';

export const BookingView: React.FC = () => {
  const { clinicSlug = 'ariana' } = useParams<{ clinicSlug: string }>();

  const { data: clinic, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.catalog.clinicBySlug(clinicSlug),
    queryFn: () => services.catalogRepo.getClinicBySlug(clinicSlug),
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#fafafa]">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 border-[3px] border-[#0f766e] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!clinic) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafafa] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
          <AlertCircle className="w-8 h-8" />
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-neutral-900 mb-2">کلینیک یافت نشد</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500 mb-6 max-w-sm">
          آدرس اینترنتی کلینیک مورد نظر اشتباه است یا این کلینیک در سامانه غیرفعال می‌باشد.
        </p>
        <Link
          to="/"
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#0f766e] text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-teal-800 transition-colors"
        >
          بازگشت به صفحه اصلی
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafafa] text-[#171717] flex flex-col">
      <DemoBanner />
      {/* Top Simple Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-white">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <span className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#0f766e] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm shadow-sm">
              M
            </span>
            <div>
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 block">
                {clinic.name}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 block">
                ثبت آنلاین درخواست نوبت
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <Link
              to="/me"
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0f766e] hover:text-teal-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-teal-50 transition-colors border border-teal-200"
            >
              <span>پیگیری نوبت من</span>
            </Link>

            <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-500">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
              <span>نوبت‌دهی رسمی کلینیک</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Two-Column Content (Intro + Wizard) */}
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-6xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-8 sm:py-10">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 lg:gap-12 items-start">
          {/* Column 1: Introduction & Clinic Value Props (5 cols) */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 order-2 lg:order-1">
            <BookingIntro clinic={clinic} />
          </div>

          {/* Column 2: 4-Step Booking Wizard (7 cols) */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 order-1 lg:order-2">
            <BookingWizard clinicId={clinic.id} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-neutral-200 bg-white py-6">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 text-center text-xs text-neutral-400">
          {clinic.name} • سامانه هوشمند رزرو نوبت مدفلو • تلفن کلینیک: {clinic.phone}
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/booking/BookingWizard.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useServices, useDoctors } from '../catalog/hooks/useCatalog';
import { useSubmitBooking } from './hooks/useBooking';
import { StepService } from './steps/StepService';
import { StepDoctor } from './steps/StepDoctor';
import { StepTime } from './steps/StepTime';
import { StepDetails } from './steps/StepDetails';
import type { BookingFormData } from './steps/StepDetails';
import { BookingConfirmation } from './BookingConfirmation';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { getTodayDateString } from '../../lib/jalali';
import type { Appointment } from '../../domain/appointment';
import { ArrowRight, ArrowLeft, Check, Lock } from 'lucide-react';

export interface BookingWizardProps {
  clinicId: string;
}

const STEPS = [
  { id: 1, title: 'خدمت' },
  { id: 2, title: 'پزشک' },
  { id: 3, title: 'زمان' },
  { id: 4, title: 'مشخصات' },
];

export const BookingWizard: React.FC<BookingWizardProps> = ({ clinicId }) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [serviceId, setServiceId] = useState('');
  const [doctorId, setDoctorId] = useState('');
  const [date, setDate] = useState(() => getTodayDateString());
  const [time, setTime] = useState('');
  const [verifiedPhone, setVerifiedPhone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [verificationToken, setVerificationToken] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [confirmedData, setConfirmedData] = useState<{
    appointment: Appointment;
    trackingCode: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const [conflictError, setConflictError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submissionError, setSubmissionError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { data: services = [] } = useServices(clinicId);
  const { data: doctors = [] } = useDoctors(clinicId);
  const submitBookingMutation = useSubmitBooking(clinicId);

  const selectedService = services.find((s) => s.id === serviceId);
  const selectedDoctor = doctors.find((d) => d.id === doctorId);

  const canProceed = (() => {
    if (step === 1) return Boolean(serviceId);
    if (step === 2) return Boolean(doctorId);
    if (step === 3) return Boolean(date && time);
    return true;
  })();

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (step < 4 && canProceed) {
      setStep((prev) => prev + 1);
    }
  };

  const handlePrev = () => {
    if (step > 1) {
      setStep((prev) => prev - 1);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitForm = async (
    formData: BookingFormData & { verificationToken: string; captchaToken: string }
  ) => {
    setSubmissionError(null);
    setConflictError(null);

    try {
      const res = await submitBookingMutation.mutateAsync({
        clinicId,
        serviceId,
        doctorId,
        date,
        time,
        clientName: formData.clientName,
        clientPhone: formData.clientPhone,
        notes: formData.notes,
        medicalNotes: formData.medicalNotes,
        verificationToken: formData.verificationToken,
        captchaToken: formData.captchaToken,
      });

      setConfirmedData(res);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ثبت نوبت.';
      if (errMsg.includes('رزرو شد') || errMsg.includes('تداخل')) {
        setConflictError(errMsg);
        setStep(3); // Send user back to time selection without resetting token
      } else {
        setSubmissionError(errMsg);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleBookAnother = () => {
    setConfirmedData(null);
    setStep(1);
    setServiceId('');
    setDoctorId('');
    setTime('');
    setDate(getTodayDateString());
    setVerifiedPhone(null);
    setVerificationToken(null);
    setConflictError(null);
    setSubmissionError(null);
  };

  if (confirmedData) {
    return (
      <BookingConfirmation
        appointment={confirmedData.appointment}
        trackingCode={confirmedData.trackingCode}
        onBookAnother={handleBookAnother}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Card className="p-6 space-y-6">
        {/* Stepper Header */}
        <div className="pb-5 border-b border-neutral-200">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            {STEPS.map((s, idx) => {
              const isCompleted = step > s.id;
              const isCurrent = step === s.id;

              return (
                <React.Fragment key={s.id}>
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center flex-1">
                    <div
                      className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
                        isCompleted
                          ? 'bg-[#0f766e] text-white'
                          : isCurrent
                          ? 'bg-[#0f766e] text-white ring-4 ring-teal-50'
                          : 'bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isCompleted ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[3]" /> : s.id}
                    </div>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[11px] sm:text-xs mt-1.5 font-medium ${
                        isCurrent
                          ? 'text-neutral-900 font-bold'
                          : isCompleted
                          ? 'text-[#0f766e]'
                          : 'text-neutral-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {s.title}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {idx < STEPS.length - 1 && (
                    <div
                      className={`h-[2px] flex-1 -mt-5 transition-colors duration-300 ease-out ${
                        step > s.id ? 'bg-[#0f766e]' : 'bg-neutral-200'
                      }`}
                    />
                  )}
                </React.Fragment>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Step Contents */}
        <div className="min-h-[290px]">
          {step === 1 && (
            <StepService
              clinicId={clinicId}
              selectedServiceId={serviceId}
              onSelectService={setServiceId}
            />
          )}

          {step === 2 && (
            <StepDoctor
              clinicId={clinicId}
              selectedDoctorId={doctorId}
              onSelectDoctor={setDoctorId}
            />
          )}

          {step === 3 && (
            <StepTime
              clinicId={clinicId}
              doctorId={doctorId}
              serviceId={serviceId}
              selectedDate={date}
              selectedTime={time}
              onSelectDate={setDate}
              onSelectTime={setTime}
              conflictError={conflictError}
            />
          )}

          {step === 4 && (
            <StepDetails
              service={selectedService}
              doctor={selectedDoctor}
              time={time}
              verifiedPhone={verifiedPhone}
              verificationToken={verificationToken}
              onTokenVerified={(phone, token) => {
                setVerifiedPhone(phone);
                setVerificationToken(token);
              }}
              onSubmit={handleSubmitForm}
              isSubmitting={submitBookingMutation.isPending}
              submissionError={submissionError}
            />
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Navigation Buttons */}
        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            {step > 1 && (
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                size="md"
                onClick={handlePrev}
                className="gap-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm"
              >
                <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>مرحله قبلی</span>
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            {step < 4 ? (
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="primary"
                size="md"
                onClick={handleNext}
                disabled={!canProceed}
                className="gap-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm"
              >
                <span>مرحله بعدی</span>
                <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button
                type="submit"
                form="booking-details-form"
                variant="primary"
                size="md"
                disabled={submitBookingMutation.isPending}
                className="text-xs sm:text-sm px-6"
              >
                {submitBookingMutation.isPending ? 'در حال ثبت نوبت...' : 'ثبت درخواست نوبت'}
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Privacy Notice below wizard */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-400 py-2">
        <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0f766e]" />
        <span>اطلاعات شما فقط برای هماهنگی همین درخواست استفاده می‌شود.</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
