=================== ./src/features/booking/hooks/useBooking.ts ===================
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../../services/queryKeys';
import type { BookingSubmissionInput } from '../../../services/repositories/IBookingRepository';

export const useAvailableSlots = (params: {
  clinicId: string;
  doctorId: string;
  serviceId: string;
  date: string;
}) => {
  const { clinicId, doctorId, serviceId, date } = params;

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.booking.slots(clinicId, doctorId, serviceId, date),
    queryFn: () => services.bookingRepo.getAvailableSlots(params),
    enabled: Boolean(clinicId && doctorId && serviceId && date),
  });
};

export const useSubmitBooking = (clinicId: string) => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: BookingSubmissionInput) =>
      services.bookingRepo.submitBooking(input),
    onSuccess: () => {
      // Immediate invalidation so new appointment appears on the reception dashboard
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.todayQueue(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.dashboard.summary(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.messages.outbox(clinicId) });
    },
  });
};

=================== ./src/features/booking/steps/StepDetails.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { isValidIranianMobile } from '../../../lib/phone';
import { formatCurrency, toPersianDigits } from '../../../lib/format';
import type { Service } from '../../../domain/service';
import type { Doctor } from '../../../domain/doctor';
import {
  User,
  Phone,
  FileText,
  CheckCircle2,
  ShieldCheck,
  AlertCircle,
  KeyRound,
  Check,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Modal } from '../../../components/ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/Button';
import { Captcha } from '../../../components/ui/Captcha';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { useAuth } from '../../../app/contexts';
import { env } from '../../../config/env';

const bookingFormSchema = z.object({
  clientName: z.string().min(3, 'نام و نام خانوادگی باید حداقل ۳ حرف باشد.'),
  clientPhone: z.string().refine((val) => isValidIranianMobile(val), {
    message: 'شماره تلفن همراه معتبر نمی‌باشد (فرمت ۰۹۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹).',
  }),
  medicalNotes: z.string().optional(),
  notes: z.string().optional(),
});

export type BookingFormData = z.infer<typeof bookingFormSchema>;

export interface StepDetailsProps {
  service?: Service;
  doctor?: Doctor;
  time: string;
  verifiedPhone?: string | null;
  verificationToken?: string | null;
  onTokenVerified?: (phone: string, token: string) => void;
  onSubmit: (data: BookingFormData & { verificationToken: string; captchaToken: string }) => Promise<void>;
  isSubmitting?: boolean;
  submissionError?: string | null;
}

export const StepDetails: React.FC<StepDetailsProps> = ({
  service,
  doctor,
  time,
  verifiedPhone,
  verificationToken: initialToken,
  onTokenVerified,
  onSubmit,
  isSubmitting = false,
  submissionError,
}) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [clientName, setClientName] = useState(user?.name || '');
  const [clientPhone, setClientPhone] = useState(user?.phone || '');
  const [medicalNotes, setMedicalNotes] = useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [isLocalSubmitting, setIsLocalSubmitting] = useState(false);

  // OTP verification state (short-lived phone ownership verification)
  const [isOtpModalOpen, setIsOtpModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [otpCode, setOtpCode] = useState('');
  const [otpError, setOtpError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isOtpLoading, setIsOtpLoading] = useState(false);
  const [pendingFormData, setPendingFormData] = useState<BookingFormData | null>(null);
  const [internalToken, setInternalToken] = useState<string | null>(initialToken || null);

  // Captcha state
  const [captchaToken, setCaptchaToken] = useState<string>('');
  const [isCaptchaVerified, setIsCaptchaVerified] = useState(false);

  // Determine active verification token for current clientPhone
  const activeVerificationToken =
    verifiedPhone && verifiedPhone === clientPhone.trim() ? initialToken : internalToken;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (isSubmitting || isLocalSubmitting) return; // Prevent double submission
    setErrors({});

    const result = bookingFormSchema.safeParse({
      clientName: clientName.trim(),
      clientPhone: clientPhone.trim(),
      medicalNotes: medicalNotes.trim() || undefined,
      notes: undefined,
    });

    if (!result.success) {
      const fieldErrors: Record<string, string> = {};
      result.error.issues.forEach((issue) => {
        const field = issue.path[0] as string;
        fieldErrors[field] = issue.message;
      });
      setErrors(fieldErrors);
      return;
    }

    if (!isCaptchaVerified || !captchaToken) {
      setErrors({ captcha: 'لطفاً آزمون امنیتی (کپچا) را علامت بزنید.' });
      return;
    }

    // If phone has not yet been verified with a token, request OTP
    if (!activeVerificationToken) {
      setPendingFormData(result.data);
      setIsOtpLoading(true);
      setOtpError(null);
      try {
        await services.authRepo.requestOtp(result.data.clientPhone);
        setIsOtpModalOpen(true);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ارسال کد تأیید.';
        setErrors({ clientPhone: msg });
      } finally {
        setIsOtpLoading(false);
      }
      return;
    }

    try {
      setIsLocalSubmitting(true);
      await onSubmit({ ...result.data, verificationToken: activeVerificationToken, captchaToken });
    } finally {
      setIsLocalSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerifyOtpAndProceed = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!pendingFormData || isSubmitting || isLocalSubmitting || isOtpLoading) return;
    setIsOtpLoading(true);
    setOtpError(null);

    try {
      const res = await services.authRepo.verifyPhoneOwnership(
        pendingFormData.clientPhone,
        otpCode.trim()
      );
      if (!res.success || !res.verificationToken) {
        setOtpError(res.error || 'کد تأیید اشتباه است.');
        return;
      }

      const token = res.verificationToken;
      setInternalToken(token);
      onTokenVerified?.(pendingFormData.clientPhone, token);
      setIsOtpModalOpen(false);
      setIsLocalSubmitting(true);
      await onSubmit({ ...pendingFormData, verificationToken: token, captchaToken });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در اعتبارسنجی کد تأیید.';
      setOtpError(msg);
    } finally {
      setIsOtpLoading(false);
      setIsLocalSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <form id="booking-details-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
        {submissionError && (
          <div className="p-3.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-start gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{submissionError}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Booking Summary Box */}
        <div className="bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-lg p-4 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
            <span>خلاصه نوبت انتخابی</span>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2 text-xs">
            <div>
              <span className="text-neutral-500 block">خدمت:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">{service?.name || '—'}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-neutral-500 block">پزشک:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">{doctor?.name || '—'}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-neutral-500 block">ساعت:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">{time || '—'}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-neutral-500 block">مبلغ:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-[#0f766e]">
                {service ? formatCurrency(service.price) : '—'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Form Inputs */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Name */}
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="clientName" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1.5">
              نام و نام خانوادگی مراجع <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <User className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto pointer-events-none text-neutral-400" />
              <input
                id="clientName"
                type="text"
                required
                value={clientName}
                onChange={(e) => setClientName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="مثال: سارا محمدی"
                className={`w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0f766e] bg-white ${
                  errors.clientName ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-neutral-300 focus:border-[#0f766e]'
                }`}
              />
            </div>
            {errors.clientName && (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-600 mt-1">{errors.clientName}</p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Phone */}
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
              <label htmlFor="clientPhone" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800">
                شماره تلفن همراه <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              {activeVerificationToken && (
                <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded flex items-center gap-1 border border-emerald-200">
                  <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
                  شماره تأیید شده
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="relative">
              <Phone className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto pointer-events-none text-neutral-400" />
              <input
                id="clientPhone"
                type="tel"
                required
                dir="ltr"
                value={clientPhone}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setClientPhone(e.target.value);
                  setInternalToken(null);
                }}
                placeholder="۰۹۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"
                className={`w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-right border rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0f766e] bg-white ${
                  errors.clientPhone ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-neutral-300 focus:border-[#0f766e]'
                }`}
              />
            </div>
            {errors.clientPhone ? (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-600 mt-1">{errors.clientPhone}</p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
                جهت جلوگیری از ثبت نوبت با شماره دیگران، کد تأیید پیامکی برای این شماره ارسال خواهد شد.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Sensitive Medical Notes (Optional) */}
          {/* TODO: Implement server-side at-rest encryption for medical notes in backend */}
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
              <label htmlFor="medicalNotes" className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800">
                سوابق و حساسیت‌های پزشکی (اختیاری)
              </label>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500 flex items-center gap-1 bg-teal-50/70 text-[#0f766e] px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-teal-200/50">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                محرمانه — فقط پزشک معالج و مدیران مجاز کلینیک
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="relative">
              <FileText className="w-4 h-4 absolute top-2.5 right-3 pointer-events-none text-neutral-400" />
              <textarea
                id="medicalNotes"
                rows={2}
                value={medicalNotes}
                onChange={(e) => setMedicalNotes(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="در صورت داشتن حساسیت دارویی، سابقه تزریق قبلی یا بیماری زمینه‌ای بنویسید..."
                className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0f766e] focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
              />
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
              اطلاعات و سوابق پزشکی شما محرمانه بوده و تنها برای پزشک معالج و مدیران مجاز کلینیک نمایش داده می‌شود (پرسنل پذیرش به این اطلاعات دسترسی ندارند).
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Captcha Security Verification */}
          <Captcha
            verified={isCaptchaVerified}
            onVerify={(token: string) => {
              setCaptchaToken(token);
              setIsCaptchaVerified(true);
              setErrors((prev) => {
                const next = { ...prev };
                delete next.captcha;
                return next;
              });
            }}
            error={errors.captcha}
          />
        </div>
      </form>

      {/* OTP Verification Modal */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={isOtpModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsOtpModalOpen(false)}
        title="تأیید شماره تلفن همراه با کد یکبارمصرف"
        maxWidth="max-w-md"
      >
        <form onSubmit={handleVerifyOtpAndProceed} className="space-y-4">
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
            کد تأیید ۶ رقمی به شماره{' '}
            <strong className="font-mono text-neutral-900" dir="ltr">
              {toPersianDigits(pendingFormData?.clientPhone || clientPhone)}
            </strong>{' '}
            ارسال شد. لطفاً کد را وارد فرمایید:
          </p>

          {Boolean(import.meta.env.DEV && env.dataSource === 'mock') && (
            <div className="p-2 bg-teal-50 border border-teal-200 text-teal-800 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono text-center">
              کد آزمایشی (Dev Mock): ۱۲۳۴۵۶
            </div>
          )}

          {otpError && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{otpError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="otpCodeBooking" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
              کد تأیید ۶ رقمی <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto pointer-events-none text-neutral-400" />
              <input
                id="otpCodeBooking"
                type="text"
                dir="ltr"
                required
                autoFocus
                maxLength={6}
                value={otpCode}
                onChange={(e) => setOtpCode(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="۱۲۳۴۵۶"
                className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-center tracking-[0.25em] font-mono text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              size="md"
              onClick={() => setIsOtpModalOpen(false)}
            >
              انصراف
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              variant="primary"
              size="md"
              disabled={isOtpLoading || otpCode.length < 6}
            >
              {isOtpLoading ? 'در حال بررسی...' : 'تأیید و ثبت نوبت'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/booking/steps/StepDoctor.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { useDoctors } from '../../catalog/hooks/useCatalog';
import { User, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { ErrorState } from '../../../components/common/ErrorState';

export interface StepDoctorProps {
  clinicId: string;
  selectedDoctorId: string;
  onSelectDoctor: (id: string) => void;
}

export const StepDoctor: React.FC<StepDoctorProps> = ({
  clinicId,
  selectedDoctorId,
  onSelectDoctor,
}) => {
  const { data: doctors = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useDoctors(clinicId);

  if (isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetch()} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">
          انتخاب پزشک متخصص
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          پزشک مورد نظر خود را برای انجام مشاوره و درمان مشخص نمایید.
        </p>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {[1, 2].map((i) => (
            <Skeleton key={i} className="h-20 w-full" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {doctors.map((doctor) => {
            const isSelected = selectedDoctorId === doctor.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={doctor.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectDoctor(doctor.id)}
                aria-pressed={isSelected}
                className={`p-4 rounded-lg border text-right transition-all flex items-center justify-between ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'border-[#0f766e] bg-teal-50/40 ring-1 ring-[#0f766e]'
                    : 'border-neutral-200 bg-white hover:border-neutral-300'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-neutral-100 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-600">
                    <User className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 text-sm">
                      {doctor.name}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                      {doctor.specialty}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {isSelected && (
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-[#0f766e] text-white flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Check className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[3]" />
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/booking/steps/StepService.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { useServices } from '../../catalog/hooks/useCatalog';
import { formatCurrency, formatDuration } from '../../../lib/format';
import { Clock, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { ErrorState } from '../../../components/common/ErrorState';

export interface StepServiceProps {
  clinicId: string;
  selectedServiceId: string;
  onSelectService: (id: string) => void;
}

export const StepService: React.FC<StepServiceProps> = ({
  clinicId,
  selectedServiceId,
  onSelectService,
}) => {
  const { data: services = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useServices(clinicId);

  if (isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetch()} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">
          انتخاب نوع خدمت
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          لطفاً خدمت مورد نظر خود را جهت دریافت نوبت انتخاب فرمایید.
        </p>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
            <Skeleton key={i} className="h-24 w-full" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {services.map((service) => {
            const isSelected = selectedServiceId === service.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={service.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectService(service.id)}
                aria-pressed={isSelected}
                className={`p-4 rounded-lg border text-right transition-all flex flex-col justify-between ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'border-[#0f766e] bg-teal-50/40 ring-1 ring-[#0f766e]'
                    : 'border-neutral-200 bg-white hover:border-neutral-300'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between w-full">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 text-sm">
                    {service.name}
                  </span>
                  {isSelected && (
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-[#0f766e] text-white flex items-center justify-center">
                      <Check className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[3]" />
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-xs w-full pt-2 border-t border-neutral-100">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-neutral-500">
                    <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    {formatDuration(service.duration)}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-neutral-900">
                    {formatCurrency(service.price)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/booking/steps/StepTime.tsx ===================
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { getUpcomingWorkingDays } from '../../../lib/jalali';
import { useAvailableSlots } from '../hooks/useBooking';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { ErrorState } from '../../../components/common/ErrorState';
import { Clock, Calendar, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export interface StepTimeProps {
  clinicId: string;
  doctorId: string;
  serviceId: string;
  selectedDate: string;
  selectedTime: string;
  onSelectDate: (date: string) => void;
  onSelectTime: (time: string) => void;
  conflictError?: string | null;
}

export const StepTime: React.FC<StepTimeProps> = ({
  clinicId,
  doctorId,
  serviceId,
  selectedDate,
  selectedTime,
  onSelectDate,
  onSelectTime,
  conflictError,
}) => {
  const workingDays = useMemo(() => getUpcomingWorkingDays(14), []);

  const {
    data: slots = [],
    isLoading,
    isError,
    refetch,
  } = useAvailableSlots({
    clinicId,
    doctorId,
    serviceId,
    date: selectedDate,
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {conflictError && (
        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-start gap-2">
          <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <span>{conflictError}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 1. Date Selection (14 Working Days) */}
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 mb-2.5">
          <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
          <h3>انتخاب روز مراجعه (تقویم کاری کلینیک)</h3>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 md:grid-cols-7 gap-2">
          {workingDays.map((day) => {
            const isSelected = selectedDate === day.dateString;
            return (
              <button
                key={day.dateString}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  onSelectDate(day.dateString);
                  onSelectTime('');
                }}
                aria-pressed={isSelected}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border text-center transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'border-[#0f766e] bg-teal-50/70 text-[#0f766e] font-bold ring-1 ring-[#0f766e]'
                    : 'border-neutral-200 bg-white hover:border-neutral-300 text-neutral-700'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 font-medium">
                  {day.dayName}
                </span>
                <span className="text-sm font-bold my-0.5">
                  {day.dayNumber}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400">
                  {day.monthName}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. Available Time Slots */}
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">
            <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
            <h3>انتخاب ساعت مراجعه</h3>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full border border-[#0f766e] bg-teal-50" />
              قابل رزرو
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-neutral-200" />
              تکمیل
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map((i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 w-full" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : isError ? (
          <div className="p-4 bg-neutral-50 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200">
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetch()} />
          </div>
        ) : slots.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-6 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500 bg-neutral-50 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200">
            هیچ ساعت خالی برای این تاریخ یافت نشد. لطفاً روز دیگری را انتخاب فرمایید.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
            {slots.map((slot) => {
              const isSelected = selectedTime === slot.rawTime || selectedTime === slot.time;

              if (!slot.available) {
                return (
                  <div
                    key={slot.id}
                    className="p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-100/70 text-neutral-400 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-not-allowed select-none"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium line-through">
                      {slot.time}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 mt-0.5">
                      رزرو شده
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              }

              return (
                <button
                  key={slot.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onSelectTime(slot.rawTime)}
                  aria-pressed={isSelected}
                  className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border text-center transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'border-[#0f766e] bg-[#0f766e] text-white font-bold ring-2 ring-teal-100'
                      : 'border-neutral-200 bg-white hover:border-neutral-300 text-neutral-800 font-medium'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold">
                    {slot.time}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`text-[10px] mt-0.5 ${
                      isSelected ? 'text-teal-100' : 'text-[#0f766e]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    خالی
                  </span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/catalog/hooks/useCatalog.ts ===================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../../services/queryKeys';

export const useServices = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.catalog.services(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.catalogRepo.getServices(clinicId),
  });
};

export const useDoctors = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.catalog.doctors(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.catalogRepo.getDoctors(clinicId),
  });
};

=================== ./src/features/clients/ClientsView.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { useClients } from './hooks/useClients';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Skeleton } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { ErrorState } from '../../components/common/ErrorState';
import { formatCurrency, toPersianDigits, getInitials, formatPhoneNumber } from '../../lib/format';
import { Search, UserCheck, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

import { useDebounce } from '../../lib/useDebounce';

export const ClientsView: React.FC = () => {
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const statusParam = searchParams.get('status');

  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(search, 300);
  const { data: clients = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useClients(clinicId, debouncedSearch);

  const displayedClients = statusParam
    ? clients.filter((c) => c.status === statusParam)
    : clients;

  if (isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetch()} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight">
          مدیریت مراجعان و پرونده‌ها
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          پرونده‌های فعال، مراجعان وفادار و پایش مشتریان در خطر ریزش
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-4">
        <div className="relative w-full sm:w-80">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto text-neutral-400 pointer-events-none" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="جستجوی نام یا تلفن مراجع..."
            className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f766e]"
          />
        </div>
      </Card>

      <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden">
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="p-6 space-y-3">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 w-full" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-right border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/60 text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">مراجع</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">شماره تماس</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">تعداد مراجعه</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">مجموع پرداختی</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">وضعیت</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">ارزش بازیابی</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200 text-sm text-neutral-900">
                {displayedClients.map((client) => {
                  const isAtRisk = client.status === 'at_risk';
                  return (
                    <tr key={client.id} className="h-14 hover:bg-neutral-50/60 transition-colors">
                      <td className="px-4 py-2">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                          <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                            isAtRisk ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' : 'bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e]'
                          }`}>
                            {getInitials(client.name)}
                          </div>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-900">
                            {client.name}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 text-xs font-mono text-neutral-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {formatPhoneNumber(client.phone)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {toPersianDigits(client.totalVisits)} بار
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {formatCurrency(client.totalSpent)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {isAtRisk ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
                            <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-600" />
                            در خطر ریزش
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200">
                            <UserCheck className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
                            فعال
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 text-xs font-bold text-[#0f766e] whitespace-nowrap">
                        {client.recoveryValue ? formatCurrency(client.recoveryValue) : '—'}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/clients/hooks/useClients.ts ===================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../../services/queryKeys';

export const useClients = (clinicId: string, search?: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.clients.all(clinicId, search),
    queryFn: () => services.clientRepo.getClients(clinicId, search),
  });
};

export const useAtRiskClients = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.clients.atRisk(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.clientRepo.getAtRiskClients(clinicId),
  });
};

=================== ./src/features/dashboard/components/AutomationsStatusPanel.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import type { Automation, AutomationStats } from '../../../domain/automation';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { toPersianDigits } from '../../../lib/format';
import { Zap, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { Can } from '../../../components/auth/Can';

export interface AutomationsStatusPanelProps {
  automations?: Automation[];
  stats?: AutomationStats;
  isLoading?: boolean;
  onToggleEnabled?: (id: string, enabled: boolean) => void;
  isToggling?: boolean;
}

export const AutomationsStatusPanel: React.FC<AutomationsStatusPanelProps> = React.memo(({
  automations = [],
  stats,
  onToggleEnabled,
  isToggling = false,
}) => {
  const isAllHealthy = automations.length > 0 && automations.every((a) => a.healthy);
  const totalActions = stats?.totalActions ?? automations.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.totalTriggered || 0), 0);
  const hoursSaved = stats?.hoursSaved ?? Math.round((totalActions * 4) / 60);

  return (
    <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-between">
      <div>
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="p-5 sm:px-6 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">
              وضعیت اتوماسیونها
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
              فعالیتهایی که بدون دخالت منشی انجام میشوند
            </p>
          </div>

          {isAllHealthy ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e]">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#0f766e]" />
              <span>سالم</span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-amber-50 text-amber-800">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500" />
              <span>نیازمند بررسی</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Rows */}
        <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
          {automations.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="p-4 sm:px-6 flex items-center justify-between hover:bg-neutral-50/50 transition-colors"
            >
              <div>
                <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900">
                  {item.title}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                  کانال: {item.channel}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium">
                  {item.healthy ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-[#0f766e] bg-teal-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#0f766e]" />
                      <span>سالم</span>
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-rose-700 bg-rose-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500" />
                      <span>خطا</span>
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <Can permission="settings:manage">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    role="switch"
                    aria-checked={item.enabled}
                    onClick={() => onToggleEnabled?.(item.id, !item.enabled)}
                    disabled={isToggling}
                    title={item.enabled ? 'غیرفعال‌سازی اتوماسیون' : 'فعال‌سازی اتوماسیون'}
                    className={`relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-full p-0.5 transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out focus:outline-none disabled:opacity-50 ${
                      item.enabled ? 'bg-[#0f766e] justify-start' : 'bg-neutral-300 justify-end'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full bg-white shadow-sm transition-all" />
                  </button>
                </Can>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Summary Footer */}
      <div className="p-4 sm:px-6 bg-neutral-50 border-t border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-neutral-600">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0f766e]" />
          <span>
            <strong className="font-bold text-neutral-900">
              {toPersianDigits(totalActions)}
            </strong>{' '}
            اقدام خودکار
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0f766e]" />
          <span>
            <strong className="font-bold text-neutral-900">
              {toPersianDigits(hoursSaved)}
            </strong>{' '}
            ساعت زمان ذخیرهشده
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
});
AutomationsStatusPanel.displayName = 'AutomationsStatusPanel';


=================== ./src/features/dashboard/components/DashboardMetricsBar.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { StatCard } from '../../../components/ui/StatCard';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import type { DashboardMetric } from '../../../domain/dashboard';

export interface DashboardMetricsBarProps {
  metrics?: DashboardMetric[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
}

export const DashboardMetricsBar: React.FC<DashboardMetricsBarProps> = React.memo(({
  metrics,
  isLoading,
}) => {
  if (isLoading || !metrics) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
          <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 p-6 space-y-3">
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-24" />
            <Skeleton className="h-8 w-16" />
            <Skeleton className="h-3 w-32" />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section aria-label="شاخص‌های کلیدی عملیات" className="w-full">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {metrics.map((metric) => (
          <StatCard key={metric.id} metric={metric} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
});
