=================== ./src/features/dashboard/components/ManualAppointmentModal.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Modal } from '../../../components/ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/Button';
import { useServices, useDoctors } from '../../catalog/hooks/useCatalog';
import { useCreateAppointment } from '../../appointments/hooks/useAppointments';
import { useAvailableSlots } from '../../booking/hooks/useBooking';
import { getUpcomingWorkingDays } from '../../../lib/jalali';
import { toPersianDigits, formatTimeFa } from '../../../lib/format';
import { isValidIranianMobile } from '../../../lib/phone';

export interface ManualAppointmentModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  clinicId: string;
}

export const ManualAppointmentModal: React.FC<ManualAppointmentModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  clinicId,
}) => {
  const { data: servicesList = [] } = useServices(clinicId);
  const { data: doctors = [] } = useDoctors(clinicId);
  const createMutation = useCreateAppointment(clinicId);

  const upcomingDays = React.useMemo(() => getUpcomingWorkingDays(14), []);

  const [clientName, setClientName] = useState('');
  const [clientPhone, setClientPhone] = useState('');
  const [serviceId, setServiceId] = useState('');
  const [doctorId, setDoctorId] = useState('');
  const [date, setDate] = useState(upcomingDays[0]?.dateString || '');
  const [time, setTime] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { data: availableSlots = [], isLoading: isLoadingSlots } = useAvailableSlots({
    clinicId,
    doctorId,
    serviceId,
    date,
  });

  const [isSuccessPulsing, setIsSuccessPulsing] = useState(false);
  const pulseTimerRef = React.useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (pulseTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(pulseTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (!clientName.trim() || !clientPhone.trim() || !serviceId || !doctorId || !date || !time) {
      setError('لطفاً تمامی فیلدهای الزامی از جمله تاریخ و ساعت را تکمیل فرمایید.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidIranianMobile(clientPhone)) {
      setError('شماره موبایل وارد شده معتبر نمی‌باشد (فرمت ۰۹۱۲...).');
      return;
    }

    try {
      await createMutation.mutateAsync({
        clinicId,
        date,
        time,
        clientName: clientName.trim(),
        clientPhone: clientPhone.trim(),
        serviceId,
        doctorId,
        source: 'reception_manual',
      });
      setIsSuccessPulsing(true);
      pulseTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        setIsSuccessPulsing(false);
        onClose();
        // Reset form
        setClientName('');
        setClientPhone('');
        setServiceId('');
        setDoctorId('');
        setTime('');
      }, 450);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ثبت نوبت.';
      setError(message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="ثبت نوبت دستی مراجع (پذیرش)"
      maxWidth="max-w-md"
    >
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        {error && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs text-rose-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
            نام مراجع <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            required
            value={clientName}
            onChange={(e) => setClientName(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="مثال: سارا محمدی"
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
            شماره تماس <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <input
            type="tel"
            required
            dir="ltr"
            value={clientPhone}
            onChange={(e) => setClientPhone(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="۰۹۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-right border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
            نوع خدمت <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            value={serviceId}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setServiceId(e.target.value);
              setTime('');
            }}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
          >
            <option value="">انتخاب نوع خدمت...</option>
            {servicesList.map((s) => (
              <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                {s.name} ({toPersianDigits(s.duration)} دقیقه)
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
            پزشک معالج <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            value={doctorId}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setDoctorId(e.target.value);
              setTime('');
            }}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
          >
            <option value="">انتخاب پزشک معالج...</option>
            {doctors.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                {d.name} ({d.specialty})
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
            تاریخ نوبت <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            value={date}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setDate(e.target.value);
              setTime('');
            }}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
          >
            {upcomingDays.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.dateString} value={d.dateString}>
                {d.formatted}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
            ساعت مراجعه <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            value={time}
            onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)}
            disabled={!doctorId || !serviceId || isLoadingSlots}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white disabled:bg-neutral-100 disabled:text-neutral-400"
          >
            <option value="">
              {!doctorId || !serviceId
                ? 'ابتدا خدمت و پزشک را انتخاب کنید...'
                : isLoadingSlots
                ? 'در حال دریافت ساعت‌های خالی...'
                : availableSlots.length === 0
                ? 'هیچ ساعت خالی یافت نشد'
                : 'انتخاب ساعت مراجعه...'}
            </option>
            {availableSlots.map((s) => (
              <option key={s.time} value={s.time}>
                ساعت {formatTimeFa(s.time)}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onClose}>
            انصراف
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="submit"
            variant="primary"
            size="sm"
            disabled={createMutation.isPending || !time}
            className={isSuccessPulsing ? 'mf-confirm-pulse' : ''}
          >
            {createMutation.isPending ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ثبت نوبت'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/dashboard/components/NextActionsList.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import type { DashboardNextAction } from '../../../domain/dashboard';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { ButtonLink } from '../../../components/ui/ButtonLink';
import { Sparkles, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';

export interface NextActionsListProps {
  actions?: DashboardNextAction[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
}

const PRIORITY_BADGE = {
  high: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200',
  medium: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200',
  low: 'bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-700 border-neutral-200',
};

const PRIORITY_LABEL = {
  high: 'اولویت بالا',
  medium: 'اولویت متوسط',
  low: 'اولویت عادی',
};

export const NextActionsList: React.FC<NextActionsListProps> = React.memo(({ actions = [] }) => {
  const getActionHref = (action: DashboardNextAction): string => {
    if (action.actionType === 'call_clients' || action.id === 'a1') {
      return '/clients?status=at_risk';
    }
    if (action.actionType === 'reply_leads' || action.id === 'a2') {
      return '/leads';
    }
    if (action.actionType === 'confirm_tomorrow' || action.id === 'a3') {
      return '/appointments?date=tomorrow&status=pending_confirmation';
    }
    return '/';
  };

  return (
    <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="p-5 sm:px-6 border-b border-neutral-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">
          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
          <h2>اقدام پیشنهادی بعدی</h2>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
          بر اساس احتمال تبدیل و ارزش مالی
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Action Cards */}
      <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
        {actions.map((action) => {
          const badgeClass = PRIORITY_BADGE[action.priority];
          const badgeLabel = PRIORITY_LABEL[action.priority];
          const href = getActionHref(action);

          return (
            <div
              key={action.id}
              className="p-5 sm:px-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 hover:bg-neutral-50/50 transition-colors"
            >
              <div className="space-y-1.5 flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span
                    className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium border ${badgeClass}`}
                  >
                    {badgeLabel}
                  </span>
                  <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">
                    {action.title}
                  </h3>
                </div>

                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                  {action.description}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center sm:flex-col sm:items-end justify-between border-t sm:border-t-0 pt-3 sm:pt-0 border-neutral-100 gap-2 shrink-0">
                <div className="text-right">
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 block">
                    {action.valueLabel}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#0f766e]">
                    {action.value}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <ButtonLink
                  to={href}
                  size="sm"
                  variant="outline"
                  className="text-xs px-3 py-1.5 hover:border-[#0f766e] hover:text-[#0f766e]"
                >
                  <span>شروع اقدام</span>
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1" />
                </ButtonLink>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
});
NextActionsList.displayName = 'NextActionsList';

=================== ./src/features/dashboard/components/TodayAppointmentsQueue.tsx ===================
import React, { useState, useRef } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import type { Appointment } from "../../../domain/appointment";
import type { AppointmentStatus } from "../../../domain/appointmentStatus";
import {
  STATUS_META,
  getAvailableTransitionsByRole,
} from "../../../domain/appointmentStatus";
import { usePermissions } from "../../../app/usePermissions";
import {
  getInitials,
  formatTimeFa,
  formatPhoneNumber,
} from "../../../lib/format";
import { Card } from "../../../components/ui/Card";
import { Button } from "../../../components/ui/Button";
import { Skeleton } from "../../../components/ui/Skeleton";
import { ArrowLeft, MoreVertical, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { useClickOutside } from "../../../lib/useClickOutside";

export interface TodayAppointmentsQueueProps {
  appointments?: Appointment[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
  onUpdateStatus: (id: string, newStatus: AppointmentStatus) => void;
  isUpdating?: boolean;
}

export const TodayAppointmentsQueue: React.FC<TodayAppointmentsQueueProps> =
  React.memo(({ appointments = [], isLoading, onUpdateStatus }) => {
    const [activeMenuId, setActiveMenuId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    useClickOutside(
      menuRef,
      () => setActiveMenuId(null),
      activeMenuId !== null,
    );
    const prevStatusesRef = React.useRef<Record<string, string>>({});
    const [highlightedRowIds, setHighlightedRowIds] = useState<
      Record<string, boolean>
    >({});
    const [pulsingBtnKey, setPulsingBtnKey] = useState<string | null>(null);

    React.useEffect(() => {
      if (appointments.length === 0) return;
      const newHighlights: string[] = [];
      const prev = prevStatusesRef.current;
      const nextStatuses: Record<string, string> = {};

      appointments.forEach((apt) => {
        nextStatuses[apt.id] = apt.status;
        if (prev[apt.id] && prev[apt.id] !== apt.status) {
          newHighlights.push(apt.id);
        }
      });

      prevStatusesRef.current = nextStatuses;

      if (newHighlights.length > 0) {
        setHighlightedRowIds((current) => {
          const updated = { ...current };
          newHighlights.forEach((id) => {
            updated[id] = true;
          });
          return updated;
        });

        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
          setHighlightedRowIds((current) => {
            const updated = { ...current };
            newHighlights.forEach((id) => {
              delete updated[id];
            });
            return updated;
          });
        }, 900);

        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }, [appointments]);

    const { can, role } = usePermissions();
    const canUpdateStatus = can("appointments:update_status");

    const handleStatusClick = (
      id: string,
      newStatus: AppointmentStatus,
      btnKey: string,
    ) => {
      setPulsingBtnKey(btnKey);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setPulsingBtnKey((curr) => (curr === btnKey ? null : curr));
      }, 450);
      onUpdateStatus(id, newStatus);
    };

    if (isLoading) {
      return (
        <Card className="p-6 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
            <Skeleton className="h-6 w-40" />
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-20" />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 w-full" />
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>
      );
    }

    return (
      <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="p-5 sm:px-6 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">
              صف نوبتهای امروز
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
              وضعیت لحظهای نوبتهای بعدی
            </p>
          </div>

          <Link
            to="/appointments"
            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[#0f766e] hover:text-[#0b5c55] transition-colors"
          >
            <span>مشاهده همه</span>
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* Table */}
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-right border-collapse">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/60 text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">
                <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">
                  ساعت
                </th>
                <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">
                  مراجع
                </th>
                <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">
                  خدمت
                </th>
                <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">
                  متخصص
                </th>
                <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">
                  وضعیت
                </th>
                <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-center">
                  عملیات
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200 text-sm text-neutral-900">
              {appointments.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={6}
                    className="py-12 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500"
                  >
                    هیچ نوبتی برای امروز ثبت نشده است.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                appointments.map((apt) => {
                  const meta = STATUS_META[apt.status];
                  const transitions = role
                    ? getAvailableTransitionsByRole(apt.status, role)
                    : [];
                  const isMenuOpen = activeMenuId === apt.id;
                  const isHighlighted = !!highlightedRowIds[apt.id];

                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={apt.id}
                      className={`h-14 hover:bg-neutral-50/60 transition-colors ${
                        isHighlighted ? "mf-row-highlight" : ""
                      }`}
                    >
                      {/* Time */}
                      <td className="px-4 font-semibold text-neutral-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {formatTimeFa(apt.time)}
                      </td>

                      {/* Client (Avatar with initial + Name + Phone) */}
                      <td className="px-4 py-2">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                            {getInitials(apt.clientName)}
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <div className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 text-xs sm:text-sm">
                              {apt.clientName}
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 font-mono tracking-wider">
                              {formatPhoneNumber(apt.clientPhone)}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* Service */}
                      <td className="px-4 text-neutral-700 text-xs sm:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">
                        {apt.serviceName}
                      </td>

                      {/* Doctor */}
                      <td className="px-4 text-neutral-700 text-xs sm:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">
                        {apt.doctorName}
                      </td>

                      {/* Status Badge */}
                      <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <span
                          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${meta.bgClass} ${meta.textClass}`}
                        >
                          <span
                            className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${meta.dotClass}`}
                          />
                          <span>{meta.label}</span>
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* Action */}
                      <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center relative">
                        <div className="w-full ">
                          {!canUpdateStatus ? (
                            <span className="text-end text-neutral-300 text-xs">—</span>
                          ) : apt.status === "pending_confirmation" ? (
                            <Button
                              size="sm"
                              variant="primary"
                              onClick={() =>
                                handleStatusClick(
                                  apt.id,
                                  "confirmed",
                                  `${apt.id}-confirmed`,
                                )
                              }
                              className={`text-xs  px-2.5 py-1 h-7 ${
                                pulsingBtnKey === `${apt.id}-confirmed`
                                  ? "mf-confirm-pulse"
                                  : ""
                              }`}
                            >
                              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 ml-1" />
                              <span>تأیید</span>
                            </Button>
                            
                          ) : apt.status === "in_clinic" &&
                            (role === "reception" ||
                              role === "owner" ||
                              role === "admin") ? (
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                              <Button
                                size="sm"
                                variant="primary"
                                onClick={() =>
                                  handleStatusClick(
                                    apt.id,
                                    "completed",
                                    `${apt.id}-completed`,
                                  )
                                }
                                className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 h-7 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 ${
                                  pulsingBtnKey === `${apt.id}-completed`
                                    ? "mf-confirm-pulse"
                                    : ""
                                }`}
                                title="تکمیل و خروج مراجع"
                              >
                                <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 ml-1" />
                                <span>تکمیل</span>
                              </Button>
                              {transitions.length > 0 && (
                                <div
                                  ref={isMenuOpen ? menuRef : undefined}
                                  className="relative inline-block text-right"
                                >
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() =>
                                      setActiveMenuId(
                                        isMenuOpen ? null : apt.id,
                                      )
                                    }
                                    className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors"
                                    aria-label="منوی عملیات نوبت"
                                    aria-expanded={isMenuOpen}
                                  >
                                    <MoreVertical className="w-4 h-4" />
                                  </button>
                                  {isMenuOpen && (
                                    <div
                                      className="absolute left-0 mt-1 w-36 bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-lg py-1 z-50 text-xs animate-in fade-in"
                                      onClick={() => setActiveMenuId(null)}
                                    >
                                      {transitions.map((nextStatus) => (
                                        <button
                                          key={nextStatus}
                                          type="button"
                                          onClick={() =>
                                            handleStatusClick(
                                              apt.id,
                                              nextStatus,
                                              `${apt.id}-${nextStatus}`,
                                            )
                                          }
                                          className="w-full text-right px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-700 flex items-center justify-between"
                                        >
                                          <span>
                                            تغییر به:{" "}
                                            {STATUS_META[nextStatus].label}
                                          </span>
                                        </button>
                                      ))}
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : transitions.length > 0 ? (
                            <div
                              ref={isMenuOpen ? menuRef : undefined}
                              className="relative inline-block text-right"
                            >
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() =>
                                  setActiveMenuId(isMenuOpen ? null : apt.id)
                                }
                                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors"
                                aria-label="منوی عملیات نوبت"
                                aria-expanded={isMenuOpen}
                              >
                                <MoreVertical className="w-4 h-4" />
                              </button>
                              {isMenuOpen && (
                                <div
                                  className="absolute left-0 mt-1 w-36 bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-lg py-1 z-50 text-xs animate-in fade-in"
                                  onClick={() => setActiveMenuId(null)}
                                >
                                  {transitions.map((nextStatus) => (
                                    <button
                                      key={nextStatus}
                                      type="button"
                                      onClick={() =>
                                        handleStatusClick(
                                          apt.id,
                                          nextStatus,
                                          `${apt.id}-${nextStatus}`,
                                        )
                                      }
                                      className="w-full text-right px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-700 flex items-center justify-between"
                                    >
                                      <span>
                                        تغییر به:{" "}
                                        {STATUS_META[nextStatus].label}
                                      </span>
                                    </button>
                                  ))}
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-neutral-300 text-xs">—</span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Card>
    );
  });
TodayAppointmentsQueue.displayName = "TodayAppointmentsQueue";

=================== ./src/features/dashboard/DashboardView.tsx ===================
import React, { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { useDashboardSummary, useNextActions } from './hooks/useDashboard';
import { useTodayQueue, useUpdateAppointmentStatus } from '../appointments/hooks/useAppointments';
import { useAutomations, useAutomationStats, useSetAutomationEnabled } from '../automations/hooks/useAutomations';

import { DashboardMetricsBar } from './components/DashboardMetricsBar';
import { TodayAppointmentsQueue } from './components/TodayAppointmentsQueue';
import { AutomationsStatusPanel } from './components/AutomationsStatusPanel';
import { NextActionsList } from './components/NextActionsList';
import { ManualAppointmentModal } from './components/ManualAppointmentModal';

import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { ButtonLink } from '../../components/ui/ButtonLink';
import { ErrorState } from '../../components/common/ErrorState';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Can } from '../../components/auth/Can';
import { ExternalLink, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import type { AppointmentStatus } from '../../domain/appointmentStatus';

export const DashboardView: React.FC = () => {
  const { clinic, clinicId } = useClinic();
  const [isManualModalOpen, setIsManualModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const {
    data: summary,
    isLoading: isSummaryLoading,
    isError: isSummaryError,
    refetch: refetchSummary,
  } = useDashboardSummary(clinicId);
  const {
    data: queue = [],
    isLoading: isQueueLoading,
    isError: isQueueError,
    refetch: refetchQueue,
  } = useTodayQueue(clinicId, 5);
  const {
    data: automations = [],
    isLoading: isAutomationsLoading,
    isError: isAutomationsError,
    refetch: refetchAutomations,
  } = useAutomations(clinicId);
  const { data: stats } = useAutomationStats(clinicId);
  const {
    data: nextActions = [],
    isLoading: isActionsLoading,
    isError: isActionsError,
    refetch: refetchActions,
  } = useNextActions(clinicId);

  const updateStatusMutation = useUpdateAppointmentStatus(clinicId);
  const toggleAutomationMutation = useSetAutomationEnabled(clinicId);

  const handleUpdateStatus = useCallback(
    (id: string, newStatus: AppointmentStatus) => {
      updateStatusMutation.mutate({ id, newStatus });
    },
    [updateStatusMutation]
  );

  const handleToggleAutomation = useCallback(
    (id: string, enabled: boolean) => {
      toggleAutomationMutation.mutate({ id, enabled });
    },
    [toggleAutomationMutation]
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      {/* Top Header Section */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0f766e] tracking-wide block">
            فضای عملیاتی کلینیک
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight mt-0.5">
            داشبورد
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-1">
            تصویر زنده فروش، نوبت و پیگیری
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Header Action Buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <ButtonLink
            to={`/book/${clinic.slug}`}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            variant="outline"
            size="md"
            className="text-xs sm:text-sm gap-1.5"
          >
            <span>لینک رزرو مشتری</span>
            <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </ButtonLink>

          <Can permission="appointments:create">
            <Button
              variant="primary"
              size="md"
              onClick={() => setIsManualModalOpen(true)}
              className="text-xs sm:text-sm gap-1.5"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>ثبت نوبت</span>
            </Button>
          </Can>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 1. Metrics Bar (4 StatCards) */}
      {isSummaryError ? (
        <Card className="p-4">
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetchSummary()} />
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <DashboardMetricsBar
          metrics={summary?.metrics}
          isLoading={isSummaryLoading}
        />
      )}

      {/* 2. Today's Appointments Queue Table (5 upcoming appointments) */}
      {isQueueError ? (
        <Card className="p-4">
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetchQueue()} />
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <TodayAppointmentsQueue
          appointments={queue}
          isLoading={isQueueLoading}
          onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
          isUpdating={updateStatusMutation.isPending}
        />
      )}

      {/* 3 & 4. Automations and Next Actions Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 items-start">
        {isAutomationsError ? (
          <Card className="p-4">
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetchAutomations()} />
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <AutomationsStatusPanel
            automations={automations}
            stats={stats}
            isLoading={isAutomationsLoading}
            onToggleEnabled={handleToggleAutomation}
            isToggling={toggleAutomationMutation.isPending}
          />
        )}

        {isActionsError ? (
          <Card className="p-4">
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetchActions()} />
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <NextActionsList
            actions={nextActions}
            isLoading={isActionsLoading}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Manual Appointment Booking Modal */}
      {isManualModalOpen && (
        <ManualAppointmentModal
          isOpen={isManualModalOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsManualModalOpen(false)}
          clinicId={clinicId}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/dashboard/hooks/useDashboard.ts ===================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../../services/queryKeys';

export const useDashboardSummary = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.dashboard.summary(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.dashboardRepo.getSummary(clinicId),
  });
};

export const useNextActions = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.dashboard.nextActions(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.dashboardRepo.getNextActions(clinicId),
  });
};

=================== ./src/features/doctor/DoctorView.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  Stethoscope,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Phone,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  Edit3,
  ShieldCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Badge } from '../../components/ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { Modal } from '../../components/ui/Modal';
import { useAuth, useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { services } from '../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../services/queryKeys';
import type { Appointment } from '../../domain/appointment';
import type { AppointmentStatus } from '../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import { STATUS_META, canTransitionByRole } from '../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import { getTodayDateString } from '../../lib/jalali';
import { toPersianDigits, formatTimeFa, formatTrackingCodeFa, formatPhoneNumber } from '../../lib/format';
import { Can } from '../../components/auth/Can';

export const DoctorView: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, actor, role } = useAuth();
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const [dateFilter, setDateFilter] = useState<'today' | 'all'>('today');
  const [selectedAptForNotes, setSelectedAptForNotes] = useState<Appointment | null>(null);
  const [noteContent, setNoteContent] = useState('');
  const [isNoteSubmitting, setIsNoteSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [noteError, setNoteError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const today = getTodayDateString();

  // Query appointments for doctor
  const {
    data: appointments = [],
    isLoading,
    error,
  } = useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.list(clinicId, {
      doctorId: actor?.doctorId,
      date: dateFilter === 'today' ? today : undefined,
    }),
    queryFn: () =>
      services.appointmentRepo.getAppointments(
        clinicId,
        {
          doctorId: actor?.doctorId,
          date: dateFilter === 'today' ? today : undefined,
        },
        actor || undefined
      ),
    enabled: !!actor,
  });

  // Mutation for status update
  const statusMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, newStatus }: { id: string; newStatus: AppointmentStatus }) =>
      services.appointmentRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, id, newStatus, actor || undefined),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.dashboard.summary(clinicId) });
    },
  });

  const handleOpenNoteModal = (apt: Appointment) => {
    setSelectedAptForNotes(apt);
    setNoteContent(apt.medicalNotes || '');
    setNoteError(null);
  };

  const handleSaveMedicalNote = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedAptForNotes || !actor) return;
    setIsNoteSubmitting(true);
    setNoteError(null);

    try {
      await services.appointmentRepo.updateMedicalNotes(
        clinicId,
        selectedAptForNotes.id,
        noteContent.trim(),
        actor
      );
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId) });
      setSelectedAptForNotes(null);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ثبت یادداشت پزشکی.';
      setNoteError(msg);
    } finally {
      setIsNoteSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const todayAppointments = React.useMemo(
    () => appointments.filter((a) => a.date === today),
    [appointments, today]
  );
  const inClinicAppointments = React.useMemo(
    () => appointments.filter((a) => a.status === 'in_clinic'),
    [appointments]
  );
  const completedAppointments = React.useMemo(
    () => appointments.filter((a) => a.status === 'completed'),
    [appointments]
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-l from-teal-800 to-[#0f766e] text-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center text-teal-100 border border-white/20">
              <Stethoscope className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold">{user?.name || 'پزشک محترم'}</h1>
                <Badge className="bg-white/15 text-white border border-white/30 text-[10px]">
                  {user?.roleTitle || 'پزشک معالج'}
                </Badge>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-teal-100/90 mt-1">
                پنل تخصصی ویزیت بیماران، سوابق پزشکی و مدیریت نوبت‌ها
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => setDateFilter('today')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-all ${
                dateFilter === 'today'
                  ? 'bg-white text-[#0f766e] shadow-sm font-bold'
                  : 'bg-white/10 text-white hover:bg-white/20'
              }`}
            >
              نوبت‌های امروز
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setDateFilter('all')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-all ${
                dateFilter === 'all'
                  ? 'bg-white text-[#0f766e] shadow-sm font-bold'
                  : 'bg-white/10 text-white hover:bg-white/20'
              }`}
            >
              همه نوبت‌ها
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Metrics Row */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <Card className="p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] flex items-center justify-center">
            <Calendar className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500 block">نوبت‌های امروز</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-neutral-900">
              {toPersianDigits(todayAppointments.length)} نوبت
            </span>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card className="p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Clock className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500 block">در انتظار ویزیت / در کلینیک</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-sky-800">
              {toPersianDigits(inClinicAppointments.length)} نفر
            </span>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card className="p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 flex items-center justify-center">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500 block">ویزیت تکمیل‌شده</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-emerald-800">
              {toPersianDigits(completedAppointments.length)} ویزیت
            </span>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Appointments List */}
      <Card className="p-5 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-neutral-100">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">
              {dateFilter === 'today' ? 'صف بیماران و نوبت‌های امروز' : 'فهرست نوبت‌های تخصصی شما'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400 mt-0.5">
              مشاهده سوابق و حساسیت‌های دارویی، ثبت نتیجه‌ی ویزیت و تغییر وضعیت حضور بیمار
            </p>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500 font-medium">
            تعداد: {toPersianDigits(appointments.length)}
          </span>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="py-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 border-[3px] border-[#0f766e] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400">در حال دریافت لیست بیماران...</p>
          </div>
        ) : error ? (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs text-rose-700">
            خطا در دریافت لیست نوبت‌ها.
          </div>
        ) : appointments.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="py-12 text-center text-xs text-neutral-400 space-y-2">
            <Calendar className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-neutral-300" />
            <p>نوبتی برای بازه انتخابی ثبت نشده است.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {appointments.map((apt) => {
              const meta = STATUS_META[apt.status];
              const canMoveToInClinic = role && canTransitionByRole(apt.status, 'in_clinic', role);
              const canComplete = role && canTransitionByRole(apt.status, 'completed', role);

              return (
                <div
                  key={apt.id}
                  className="p-4 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 hover:border-neutral-300 bg-white transition-all space-y-3"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3">
                      <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-neutral-100 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[#0f766e] text-sm shrink-0">
                        {apt.clientName.charAt(0)}
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="font-bold text-sm text-neutral-900">
                            {apt.clientName}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-400 bg-neutral-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-neutral-200">
                            {formatTrackingCodeFa(apt.trackingCode)}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono" dir="ltr">
                            <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400" />
                            {formatPhoneNumber(apt.clientPhone)}
                          </span>
                          <span>•</span>
                          <span>{apt.serviceName}</span>
                          <span>•</span>
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono">
                            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400" />
                            {formatTimeFa(apt.time)}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-end sm:self-auto">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${meta.bgClass} ${meta.textClass}`}
                      >
                        <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${meta.dotClass}`} />
                        {meta.label}
                      </span>

                      {canMoveToInClinic && (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="outline"
                          disabled={statusMutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() =>
                            statusMutation.mutate({ id: apt.id, newStatus: 'in_clinic' })
                          }
                          className="text-xs"
                        >
                          ورود به اتاق ویزیت
                        </Button>
                      )}

                      {canComplete && (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="primary"
                          disabled={statusMutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() =>
                            statusMutation.mutate({ id: apt.id, newStatus: 'completed' })
                          }
                          className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1"
                        >
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>تکمیل ویزیت</span>
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Medical Notes Banner */}
                  <div className="bg-amber-50/60 border border-amber-200/80 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                      <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                      <div>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800 ml-1.5">
                          یادداشت و پرونده محرمانه پزشکی:
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-neutral-600">
                          {apt.medicalNotes ? apt.medicalNotes : 'هنوز یادداشت یا حساسیت خاصی ثبت نشده است.'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <Can permission="medical_notes:write">
                      <Button
                        size="sm"
                        variant="ghost"
                        onClick={() => handleOpenNoteModal(apt)}
                        className="text-[#0f766e] hover:bg-teal-50 shrink-0 text-xs flex items-center gap-1 self-end sm:self-auto"
                      >
                        <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>{apt.medicalNotes ? 'ویرایش پرونده' : 'ثبت یادداشت پزشک'}</span>
                      </Button>
                    </Can>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Medical Notes Modal */}
      {/* TODO: Implement server-side at-rest encryption for medical notes in backend */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={!!selectedAptForNotes}
        onClose={() => setSelectedAptForNotes(null)}
        title="ثبت / ویرایش پرونده محرمانه پزشکی بیمار"
        maxWidth="max-w-lg"
      >
        {selectedAptForNotes && (
          <form onSubmit={handleSaveMedicalNote} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="p-3 bg-neutral-50 rounded-lg text-xs space-y-1">
              <div className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">
                بیمار: {selectedAptForNotes.clientName} ({formatPhoneNumber(selectedAptForNotes.clientPhone)})
              </div>
              <div className="text-neutral-500">
                خدمت: {selectedAptForNotes.serviceName} | نوبت: {selectedAptForNotes.date} ساعت{' '}
                {formatTimeFa(selectedAptForNotes.time)}
              </div>
            </div>

            {noteError && (
              <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                <span>{noteError}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="medicalNoteText" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1.5">
                توضیحات و دستورات پزشکی پزشک معالج <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <textarea
                id="medicalNoteText"
                rows={4}
                required
                value={noteContent}
                onChange={(e) => setNoteContent(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="سوابق بیماری، حساسیت دارویی، نوع مواد مصرفی، توصیه‌های مراقبتی یا خلاصه اقدامات انجام شده..."
                className="w-full p-3 text-xs sm:text-sm border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0f766e] focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white leading-relaxed"
              />
              <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
                این یادداشت برای پزشک معالج و مدیر مجاز نمایش داده می‌شود و پرسنل پذیرش به آن دسترسی ندارند.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                size="md"
                onClick={() => setSelectedAptForNotes(null)}
              >
                انصراف
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="primary"
                size="md"
                disabled={isNoteSubmitting}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                {isNoteSubmitting ? (
                  <span>در حال ذخیره...</span>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>ذخیره در پرونده بیمار</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
};

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { services } from '../../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../../services/queryKeys';

export const useLeads = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.leads.all(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.leadRepo.getLeads(clinicId),
  });
};

export const useHotLeads = (clinicId: string) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.leads.hot(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.leadRepo.getHotLeads(clinicId),
  });
};

=================== ./src/features/leads/LeadsView.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { useLeads } from './hooks/useLeads';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Skeleton } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { ErrorState } from '../../components/common/ErrorState';
import { toPersianDigits, formatPhoneNumber } from '../../lib/format';
import type { LeadStatus } from '../../domain/lead';
import { Flame } from 'lucide-react';

const LEAD_STATUS_CONFIG: Record<
  LeadStatus,
  { label: string; bg: string; text: string; border: string }
> = {
  hot: { label: 'سرنخ داغ', bg: 'bg-rose-50', text: 'text-rose-700', border: 'border-rose-200' },
  new: { label: 'سرنخ جدید', bg: 'bg-blue-50', text: 'text-blue-700', border: 'border-blue-200' },
  contacted: { label: 'تماس گرفته‌شده', bg: 'bg-amber-50', text: 'text-amber-800', border: 'border-amber-200' },
  converted: { label: 'تبدیل‌شده', bg: 'bg-emerald-50', text: 'text-emerald-700', border: 'border-emerald-200' },
  dropped: { label: 'انصراف', bg: 'bg-neutral-100', text: 'text-neutral-600', border: 'border-neutral-200' },
};

export const LeadsView: React.FC = () => {
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();
  const { data: leads = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useLeads(clinicId);

  if (isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetch()} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight">
          سرنخ‌های فروش و متقاضیان
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          پیگیری مشتریان بالقوه ورودی از کمپین‌ها، اینستاگرام و سایت
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden">
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="p-6 space-y-3">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 w-full" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-right border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/60 text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">نام متقاضی</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">شماره تماس</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">خدمت مورد علاقه</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">منبع جذب</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">امتیاز تبدیل</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="h-11 px-4 text-right">وضعیت</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200 text-sm text-neutral-900">
                {leads.map((lead) => {
                  const statusConfig = LEAD_STATUS_CONFIG[lead.status] || LEAD_STATUS_CONFIG.new;

                  return (
                    <tr key={lead.id} className="h-14 hover:bg-neutral-50/60 transition-colors">
                      <td className="px-4 font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {lead.name}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 text-xs font-mono text-neutral-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {formatPhoneNumber(lead.phone)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {lead.interestedService}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 text-xs text-neutral-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {lead.source}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <span className="font-bold text-xs text-[#0f766e]">
                          {toPersianDigits(lead.score)}٪
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span
                            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold border ${statusConfig.bg} ${statusConfig.text} ${statusConfig.border}`}
                          >
                            {lead.status === 'hot' && <Flame className="w-3 h-3 text-rose-500" />}
                            {statusConfig.label}
                          </span>
                          {lead.isDecisionReady && (
                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium bg-amber-50 text-amber-800">
                              آماده تصمیم‌گیری
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
};
