=================== ./src/features/messages/MessagesView.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { useOutboxMessages } from './hooks/useMessages';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Skeleton } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { ErrorState } from '../../components/common/ErrorState';
import { CheckCheck, Clock, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatPhoneNumber } from '../../lib/format';
import { formatIsoToJalali } from '../../lib/jalali';

export const MessagesView: React.FC = () => {
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();
  const { data: messages = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useOutboxMessages(clinicId);

  if (isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => refetch()} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight">
          صندوق پیام‌های ارسالی (Outbox)
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          گزارش و رهگیری پیامک‌ها و اعلان‌های واتساپ ارسال‌شده توسط موتور اتوماسیون
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden">
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="p-6 space-y-3">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-16 w-full" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : messages.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500">
            هنوز پیامی ارسال نشده است.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
            {messages.map((msg) => (
              <div
                key={msg.id}
                className="p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 hover:bg-neutral-50/50 transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="space-y-1.5 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span
                      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold ${
                        msg.channel === 'whatsapp'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200'
                          : 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-800 border border-sky-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {msg.channel === 'whatsapp' ? 'واتساپ' : 'پیامک'}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-900">
                      گیرنده: {msg.recipientName} ({formatPhoneNumber(msg.recipientPhone)})
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed bg-neutral-50/80 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-100">
                    {msg.content}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center sm:flex-col sm:items-end justify-between text-xs gap-1 shrink-0">
                  {msg.status === 'sent' && (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-emerald-200 font-medium text-[11px]">
                      <CheckCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                      ارسال موفق
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {msg.status === 'pending' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-200 font-medium text-[11px]">
                        <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
                        در انتظار ارسال
                      </span>
                      {msg.scheduledFor && (
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-800">
                          زمان‌بندی‌شده برای: {formatIsoToJalali(msg.scheduledFor)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {msg.status === 'failed' && (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-rose-700 bg-rose-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-rose-200 font-medium text-[11px]">
                      <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-600" />
                      خطا در ارسال
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400">{msg.createdAt}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/patient/PatientView.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Phone,
  Search,
  Edit2,
  Check,
  Building,
  LogOut,
  AlertTriangle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { Modal } from '../../components/ui/Modal';
import { useAuth, useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { services } from '../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../services/queryKeys';
import { STATUS_META, type AppointmentStatus } from '../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import type { Appointment } from '../../domain/appointment';
import { toPersianDigits, formatTimeFa, formatTrackingCodeFa, formatPhoneNumber } from '../../lib/format';

export const PatientView: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, actor, logout, updateProfileName } = useAuth();
  const { clinic, clinicId } = useClinic();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'upcoming' | 'history'>('upcoming');
  const [trackingFilter, setTrackingFilter] = useState('');

  // Cancel Appointment state
  const [aptToCancel, setAptToCancel] = useState<Appointment | null>(null);
  const [cancelError, setCancelError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Edit Name state
  const [isEditingName, setIsEditingName] = useState(false);
  const [nameInput, setNameInput] = useState(user?.name || '');
  const [nameSuccess, setNameSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Fetch patient appointments
  const { data: appointments = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.myAppointments(clinicId, user?.phone || ''),
    queryFn: () => services.appointmentRepo.getAppointments(clinicId, undefined, actor || undefined),
    enabled: !!actor,
  });

  // Cancel mutation
  const cancelMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (appointmentId: string) =>
      services.appointmentRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, appointmentId, 'cancelled', actor || undefined),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.myAppointments(clinicId, user?.phone || '') });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.appointments.all(clinicId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.dashboard.summary(clinicId) });
      setAptToCancel(null);
      setCancelError(null);
    },
    onError: (err: unknown) => {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در لغو نوبت. لطفاً دوباره تلاش کنید.';
      setCancelError(msg);
    },
  });

  const handleConfirmCancel = () => {
    if (!aptToCancel) return;
    setCancelError(null);
    cancelMutation.mutate(aptToCancel.id);
  };

  const handleUpdateName = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!nameInput.trim() || !user) return;
    try {
      await updateProfileName(nameInput.trim());
      setIsEditingName(false);
      setNameSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setNameSuccess(false), 3000);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  const filteredAppointments = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!trackingFilter.trim()) return appointments;
    const q = trackingFilter.trim().toLowerCase();
    return appointments.filter(
      (apt) => apt.trackingCode.toLowerCase().includes(q) || apt.serviceName.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [appointments, trackingFilter]);

  const upcomingAppointments = React.useMemo(
    () =>
      filteredAppointments.filter(
        (apt) =>
          apt.status === 'pending_confirmation' ||
          apt.status === 'confirmed' ||
          apt.status === 'in_clinic' ||
          apt.status === 'pending_payment'
      ),
    [filteredAppointments]
  );

  const historyAppointments = React.useMemo(
    () =>
      filteredAppointments.filter(
        (apt) => apt.status === 'completed' || apt.status === 'cancelled'
      ),
    [filteredAppointments]
  );

  const displayedAppointments = activeTab === 'upcoming' ? upcomingAppointments : historyAppointments;

  const isCancellable = (status: AppointmentStatus) =>
    status === 'pending_confirmation' || status === 'confirmed' || status === 'pending_payment';

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto py-8 px-4 sm:px-6 space-y-6">
      {/* Top Patient Profile Header */}
      <Card className="p-6 bg-gradient-to-l from-teal-900 to-[#0f766e] text-white rounded-2xl border-none shadow-md">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-sm border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-bold text-teal-100">
              {user?.avatarInitials || 'م'}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {isEditingName ? (
                  <form onSubmit={handleUpdateName} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={nameInput}
                      onChange={(e) => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1 text-sm bg-white/20 border border-white/40 rounded text-white focus:outline-none focus:bg-white/30"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="p-1 rounded bg-white text-[#0f766e] hover:bg-teal-50"
                      title="ذخیره نام"
                    >
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </form>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <h1 className="text-xl font-bold">{user?.name}</h1>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        setNameInput(user?.name || '');
                        setIsEditingName(true);
                      }}
                      className="p-1 text-teal-200 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                      title="ویرایش نام"
                    >
                      <Edit2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-teal-100/90 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono" dir="ltr">
                <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>{formatPhoneNumber(user?.phone || '')}</span>
              </p>
              {nameSuccess && (
                <span className="text-[11px] text-emerald-200 mt-1 block">نام شما با موفقیت بروزرسانی شد.</span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 self-end sm:self-auto">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={logout}
              className="bg-white/10 text-white border-white/20 hover:bg-white/20 text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>خروج از حساب</span>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Clinic Info Box */}
      <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-200 text-xs text-neutral-600 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Building className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e] shrink-0" />
          <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800">{clinic.name}</span>
          <span>•</span>
          <span>{clinic.address}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="text-neutral-500 font-mono">
          ساعت کاری: {clinic.workingHours}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Search and Tabs */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3 pt-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-neutral-100 p-1 rounded-xl w-fit">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('upcoming')}
            className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'upcoming'
                ? 'bg-white text-[#0f766e] shadow-sm'
                : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
            }`}
          >
            نوبت‌های آینده ({toPersianDigits(upcomingAppointments.length)})
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('history')}
            className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'history'
                ? 'bg-white text-[#0f766e] shadow-sm'
                : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
            }`}
          >
            تاریخچه نوبت‌ها ({toPersianDigits(historyAppointments.length)})
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tracking Code Filter */}
        <div className="relative w-full sm:w-64">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute inset-y-0 right-3 my-auto text-neutral-400 pointer-events-none" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={trackingFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setTrackingFilter(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="رهگیری با کد یا نام خدمت..."
            className="w-full pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Info Notice Band */}
      <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
        شما می‌توانید نوبت‌های آتی خود را مشاهده کرده یا در صورت لزوم لغو نمایید. برای رزرو نوبت جدید، با پذیرش کلینیک تماس بگیرید.
      </div>

      {/* Appointments List */}
      <Card className="p-6 space-y-4">
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="py-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 border-[3px] border-[#0f766e] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400">در حال دریافت اطلاعات نوبت‌ها...</p>
          </div>
        ) : displayedAppointments.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="py-12 text-center text-xs text-neutral-400 space-y-2">
            <Calendar className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-neutral-300" />
            <p>هیچ نوبتی در این بخش یافت نشد.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {displayedAppointments.map((apt) => {
              const meta = STATUS_META[apt.status];
              const canCancel = activeTab === 'upcoming' && isCancellable(apt.status);

              return (
                <div
                  key={apt.id}
                  className="p-4 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white hover:border-neutral-300 transition-all space-y-3"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-bold text-sm text-neutral-900">
                          {apt.serviceName}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500 bg-neutral-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-neutral-200">
                          کد رهگیری: {formatTrackingCodeFa(apt.trackingCode)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1">
                        <span>پزشک: {apt.doctorName}</span>
                        <span>•</span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400" />
                          {apt.date}
                        </span>
                        <span>•</span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono">
                          <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400" />
                          ساعت {formatTimeFa(apt.time)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-end sm:self-auto">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${meta.bgClass} ${meta.textClass}`}
                      >
                        <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${meta.dotClass}`} />
                        {meta.label}
                      </span>

                      {canCancel && (
                        <Button
                          variant="outline"
                          size="sm"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setCancelError(null);
                            setAptToCancel(apt);
                          }}
                          className="text-xs text-rose-600 border-rose-200 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:border-rose-300"
                        >
                          لغو نوبت
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Cancel Confirmation Modal */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={Boolean(aptToCancel)}
        onClose={() => {
          if (!cancelMutation.isPending) {
            setAptToCancel(null);
            setCancelError(null);
          }
        }}
        title="تأیید لغو نوبت"
        footer={
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => {
                setAptToCancel(null);
                setCancelError(null);
              }}
              disabled={cancelMutation.isPending}
            >
              انصراف
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="danger"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleConfirmCancel}
              isLoading={cancelMutation.isPending}
            >
              بله، لغو شود
            </Button>
          </div>
        }
      >
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 text-xs">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold">آیا از لغو نوبت خود اطمینان دارید؟</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-amber-700 leading-relaxed">
                با لغو نوبت، زمان رزرو شده برای سایر بیماران آزاد خواهد شد و برای رزرو مجدد باید مجدداً هماهنگی انجام شود.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {aptToCancel && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 space-y-2 text-xs">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-neutral-500">خدمت:</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800">{aptToCancel.serviceName}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-neutral-500">پزشک معالج:</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800">{aptToCancel.doctorName}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-neutral-500">تاریخ و ساعت:</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800 font-mono">
                  {aptToCancel.date} - ساعت {formatTimeFa(aptToCancel.time)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-neutral-500">کد رهگیری:</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-neutral-800">
                  {formatTrackingCodeFa(aptToCancel.trackingCode)}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {cancelError && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 text-xs">
              {cancelError}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/settings/AuditLogsView.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Shield } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { services } from '../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../services/queryKeys';
import { ROLE_LABELS } from '../../domain/session';
import type { AuditAction } from '../../domain/audit';

export const AuditLogsView: React.FC = () => {
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();

  const { data: logs = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.audit.logs(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.auditRepo.getLogs(clinicId),
  });

  const getActionBadgeColor = (action: AuditAction) => {
    switch (action) {
      case 'auth.login_success':
        return 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200';
      case 'auth.login_failed':
        return 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-200';
      case 'auth.logout':
        return 'bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-700 border-neutral-200';
      case 'medical_notes.view':
        return 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200';
      case 'user.role_change':
      case 'user.deactivated':
        return 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-800 border-purple-200';
      default:
        return 'bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 border-teal-200';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="pb-4 border-b border-neutral-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Shield className="w-5 h-5 text-[#0f766e]" />
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-neutral-900">گزارش‌های امنیتی و رویدادها (Audit Log)</h1>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          ثبت تغییرات حیاتی سیستم، ورود و خروج کاربران، مشاهده پرونده‌های حساس پزشکی و تغییرات وضعیت نوبت‌ها
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-right text-xs">
            <thead className="bg-neutral-50 border-b border-neutral-200 text-neutral-500 font-semibold">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">زمان ثبت</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">کاربر / اقدام‌کننده</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">نقش</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">نوع رویداد</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">توضیحات و جزئیات</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
              {isLoading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-8 text-center text-neutral-400">
                    در حال دریافت گزارش‌های امنیتی...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-8 text-center text-neutral-400">
                    هیچ رویدادی ثبت نشده است.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                logs.map((log) => (
                  <tr key={log.id} className="hover:bg-neutral-50/50 transition-colors">
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-500 font-mono text-[11px]" dir="ltr">
                      {new Date(log.createdAt).toLocaleString('fa-IR')}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-neutral-900">
                      {log.userName}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
                        {ROLE_LABELS[log.role] || log.role}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border font-medium ${getActionBadgeColor(
                          log.action
                        )}`}
                      >
                        {log.actionLabel}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-600 max-w-md truncate">
                      {log.details || '—'}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/settings/SettingsView.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { integrationRegistry, useIntegrationStatuses } from '../../integrations/registry';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { Radio } from 'lucide-react';

export const SettingsView: React.FC = () => {
  const statuses = useIntegrationStatuses();

  const handleToggle = (id: string, current: string) => {
    const next = current === 'connected' ? 'not_connected' : 'connected';
    integrationRegistry.updateStatus(id, next);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-tight">
          تنظیمات و اتصال سرویس‌ها
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          مدیریت اتصال به ارائه‌دهندگان بیرونی (پیامک، واتساپ، درگاه پرداخت و رزرو آنلاین)
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Radio className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0f766e]" />
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">
              رجیستری ارتباط با ارائه‌دهندگان سرویس (Integration Registry)
            </h2>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400">وضعیت زنده پورت‌ها</span>
        </div>

        <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
          {statuses.map((item) => {
            const isConnected = item.status === 'connected';

            return (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 hover:bg-neutral-50/50 transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <span
                      className={`w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${
                        isConnected ? 'bg-emerald-500 ring-2 ring-emerald-100' : 'bg-amber-400 ring-2 ring-amber-100'
                      }`}
                    />
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">
                      {item.title}
                    </h3>
                    <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400">
                      ({item.providerName})
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                    {item.hint}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
                  <span
                    className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${
                      isConnected
                        ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800'
                        : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {item.statusLabel}
                  </span>

                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    variant="outline"
                    onClick={() => handleToggle(item.id, item.status)}
                    className="text-xs"
                  >
                    {isConnected ? 'قطع اتصال' : 'اتصال سرویس'}
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/features/settings/UsersView.tsx ===================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Users, UserPlus, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card } from '../../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { Modal } from '../../components/ui/Modal';
import { Badge } from '../../components/ui/Badge';
import { useAuth, useClinic } from '../../app/contexts';
import { services } from '../../services';
import { queryKeys } from '../../services/queryKeys';
import { useDoctors } from '../catalog/hooks/useCatalog';
import type { Role, UserProfile } from '../../domain/session';
import { ROLE_LABELS } from '../../domain/session';
import { isValidIranianMobile } from '../../lib/phone';
import { toPersianDigits } from '../../lib/format';

export const UsersView: React.FC = () => {
  const { role: currentUserRole, user: currentUser, actor } = useAuth();
  const { clinicId } = useClinic();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const [isAddModalOpen, setIsAddModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role>('reception');
  const [roleTitle, setRoleTitle] = useState('کارشناس پذیرش');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [selectedDoctorId, setSelectedDoctorId] = useState('');
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [tableError, setTableError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modal for assigning doctor when changing role to doctor
  const [roleChangeModal, setRoleChangeModal] = useState<{
    user: UserProfile;
    newRole: Role;
  } | null>(null);
  const [roleChangeDoctorId, setRoleChangeDoctorId] = useState('');

  // Fetch doctors for clinic
  const { data: doctors = [] } = useDoctors(clinicId);

  // Fetch users
  const { data: users = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.auth.users(clinicId),
    queryFn: () => services.authRepo.getUsers(clinicId),
  });

  // Toggle user status mutation
  const toggleStatusMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (userId: string) =>
      services.authRepo.toggleUserStatus(clinicId, userId, actor || undefined),
    onSuccess: () => {
      setTableError(null);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.auth.users(clinicId) });
    },
    onError: (err: unknown) => {
      setTableError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در تغییر وضعیت کاربر.');
    },
  });

  // Change role mutation
  const changeRoleMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({
      userId,
      newRole,
      doctorId,
    }: {
      userId: string;
      newRole: Role;
      doctorId?: string;
    }) =>
      services.authRepo.updateUserRole(
        clinicId,
        userId,
        newRole,
        actor || undefined,
        doctorId
      ),
    onSuccess: () => {
      setTableError(null);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.auth.users(clinicId) });
      setRoleChangeModal(null);
      setRoleChangeDoctorId('');
    },
    onError: (err: unknown) => {
      setTableError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در تغییر نقش کاربر.');
    },
  });

  // Create user mutation
  const createUserMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      services.authRepo.createUser(
        clinicId,
        {
          name: name.trim(),
          phone: phone.trim(),
          role,
          roleTitle: roleTitle.trim() || ROLE_LABELS[role],
          email: email.trim() || undefined,
          doctorId: role === 'doctor' ? selectedDoctorId : undefined,
        },
        actor || undefined
      ),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.auth.users(clinicId) });
      setIsAddModalOpen(false);
      setName('');
      setPhone('');
      setEmail('');
      setSelectedDoctorId('');
      setFormError(null);
    },
    onError: (err: unknown) => {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ایجاد کاربر جدید.';
      setFormError(msg);
    },
  });

  const handleAddSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);

    if (!isValidIranianMobile(phone)) {
      setFormError('شماره تلفن همراه معتبر نمی‌باشد (فرمت ۰۹۱۲...).');
      return;
    }

    // Doctor role must have doctorId
    if (role === 'doctor' && !selectedDoctorId) {
      setFormError('انتخاب پزشک معالج برای نقش پزشک متخصص الزامی است.');
      return;
    }

    // Role creation constraints: admin cannot create owner or admin
    if (currentUserRole === 'admin' && (role === 'owner' || role === 'admin')) {
      setFormError('مدیر عملیات تنها مجاز به ایجاد کاربران با نقش پذیرش، پزشک یا بیمار می‌باشد.');
      return;
    }

    createUserMutation.mutate();
  };

  const handleRoleSelectChange = (user: UserProfile, newRole: Role) => {
    setTableError(null);
    if (newRole === 'doctor' && !user.doctorId) {
      setRoleChangeModal({ user, newRole });
      setRoleChangeDoctorId('');
      return;
    }
    changeRoleMutation.mutate({ userId: user.id, newRole, doctorId: user.doctorId });
  };

  // Available roles for selection depending on current user role
  const availableRoles: Role[] =
    currentUserRole === 'owner'
      ? ['admin', 'reception', 'doctor', 'patient']
      : ['reception', 'doctor', 'patient'];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-neutral-200">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Users className="w-5 h-5 text-[#0f766e]" />
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-neutral-900">مدیریت کاربران و دسترسی‌ها</h1>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1">
            مشاهده، افزودن، تغییر سطح دسترسی و فعال/غیرفعال‌سازی حساب‌های پرسنل و مراجعان
          </p>
        </div>

        <Button
          variant="primary"
          size="md"
          onClick={() => {
            setFormError(null);
            setIsAddModalOpen(true);
          }}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>افزودن کاربر جدید</span>
        </Button>
      </div>

      {tableError && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 shrink-0" />
            <span>{tableError}</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setTableError(null)}
            className="text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-600"
          >
            بستن
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Users Table */}
      <Card className="overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-right text-xs">
            <thead className="bg-neutral-50 border-b border-neutral-200 text-neutral-500 font-semibold">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">کاربر</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">شماره همراه</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">نقش سیستمی</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">عنوان سازمانی</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">وضعیت حساب</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">عملیات</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
              {isLoading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={6} className="py-8 text-center text-neutral-400">
                    در حال دریافت فهرست کاربران...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : users.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={6} className="py-8 text-center text-neutral-400">
                    هیچ کاربری یافت نشد.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                users.map((u) => {
                  const isSelf = u.id === currentUser?.id;
                  const isOwnerUser = u.role === 'owner';
                  const canManageThisUser =
                    !isSelf &&
                    (currentUserRole === 'owner' ||
                      (currentUserRole === 'admin' && !isOwnerUser && u.role !== 'admin'));

                  return (
                    <tr key={u.id} className="hover:bg-neutral-50/50 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-teal-50 text-[#0f766e] flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0">
                            {u.avatarInitials || u.name.charAt(0)}
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 block">{u.name}</span>
                            {u.email && (
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 block">{u.email}</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-neutral-600" dir="ltr">
                        {toPersianDigits(u.phone)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        {canManageThisUser ? (
                          <select
                            value={u.role}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                              handleRoleSelectChange(u, e.target.value as Role)
                            }
                            className="px-2 py-1 border border-neutral-300 rounded text-xs bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
                          >
                            {availableRoles.map((r) => (
                              <option key={r} value={r}>
                                {ROLE_LABELS[r]}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                          </select>
                        ) : (
                          <Badge className="text-neutral-700 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200">
                            {ROLE_LABELS[u.role] || u.role}
                          </Badge>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-600">{u.roleTitle}</td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <span
                          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium ${
                            u.isActive
                              ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
                              : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <span
                            className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${
                              u.isActive ? 'bg-emerald-600' : 'bg-rose-600'
                            }`}
                          />
                          {u.isActive ? 'فعال' : 'غیرفعال'}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                        {canManageThisUser ? (
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="outline"
                            onClick={() => toggleStatusMutation.mutate(u.id)}
                            disabled={toggleStatusMutation.isPending}
                            className={`text-xs ${
                              u.isActive
                                ? 'text-rose-600 border-rose-200 hover:bg-rose-50'
                                : 'text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-50'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {u.isActive ? 'غیرفعال‌سازی' : 'فعال‌سازی'}
                          </Button>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
                            {isSelf ? 'حساب جاری' : 'غیرقابل ویرایش'}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Add User Modal */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={isAddModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAddModalOpen(false)}
        title="افزودن کاربر و تخصیص نقش"
        maxWidth="max-w-md"
      >
        <form onSubmit={handleAddSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          {formError && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-start gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-rose-600" />
              <span>{formError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
              نام و نام خانوادگی <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="مثال: دکتر مهدی کاظمی"
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
              شماره تلفن همراه <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="tel"
              required
              dir="ltr"
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="۰۹۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs text-right border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
            />
            <p className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
              ورود کاربر با رمز یکبارمصرف پیامکی به این شماره انجام خواهد شد.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
              نقش دسترسی در سامانه <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <select
              value={role}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const newR = e.target.value as Role;
                setRole(newR);
                setRoleTitle(ROLE_LABELS[newR] || '');
              }}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
            >
              {availableRoles.map((r) => (
                <option key={r} value={r}>
                  {ROLE_LABELS[r]}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {role === 'doctor' && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
                انتخاب پزشک معالج <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <select
                required
                value={selectedDoctorId}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedDoctorId(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
              >
                <option value="">انتخاب پزشک از سیستم...</option>
                {doctors.map((d) => (
                  <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                    {d.name} ({d.specialty})
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
                دسترسی پزشک فقط به نوبت‌ها و مراجعان پرونده خودش محدود خواهد شد.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
              عنوان شغلی / سازمانی <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={roleTitle}
              onChange={(e) => setRoleTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="مثال: پزشک متخصص پوست / کارشناس پذیرش"
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
              پست الکترونیکی (اختیاری)
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              dir="ltr"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="user@ariana.clinic"
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e]"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="pt-3 border-t border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              size="md"
              onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(false)}
            >
              انصراف
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              variant="primary"
              size="md"
              disabled={createUserMutation.isPending}
            >
              {createUserMutation.isPending ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ثبت و فعال‌سازی کاربر'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>

      {/* Assign Doctor Modal for Role Update */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={Boolean(roleChangeModal)}
        onClose={() => setRoleChangeModal(null)}
        title="تخصیص شناسه پزشک معالج به کاربر"
        maxWidth="max-w-md"
      >
        {roleChangeModal && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
              برای تغییر نقش کاربر <strong>{roleChangeModal.user.name}</strong> به پزشک متخصص، انتخاب پرونده پزشک متناظر در کلینیک الزامی است:
            </p>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">
                پزشک متناظر <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <select
                value={roleChangeDoctorId}
                onChange={(e) => setRoleChangeDoctorId(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f766e] bg-white"
              >
                <option value="">انتخاب پزشک معالج...</option>
                {doctors.map((d) => (
                  <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                    {d.name} ({d.specialty})
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                size="md"
                onClick={() => setRoleChangeModal(null)}
              >
                انصراف
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="primary"
                size="md"
                disabled={!roleChangeDoctorId || changeRoleMutation.isPending}
                onClick={() =>
                  changeRoleMutation.mutate({
                    userId: roleChangeModal.user.id,
                    newRole: 'doctor',
                    doctorId: roleChangeDoctorId,
                  })
                }
              >
                {changeRoleMutation.isPending ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'تأیید و ذخیره'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/integrations/mock/MockPaymentProvider.ts ===================
import type { PaymentProvider, CreateInvoiceResult } from '../ports/PaymentProvider';

export class MockPaymentProvider implements PaymentProvider {
  name = 'زرین‌پال (درگاه پرداخت آنلاین)';

  async createInvoice(_amount: number, _description: string, _callbackUrl: string): Promise<CreateInvoiceResult> {
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
    const invoiceId = `inv-${Date.now()}`;
    return {
      success: true,
      invoiceId,
      paymentUrl: `https://mock-payment.zarinpal.com/pg/StartPay/${invoiceId}`,
    };
  }

  async verifyPayment(_invoiceId: string, _authority: string): Promise<boolean> {
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 80));
    return true;
  }

  async checkHealth(): Promise<boolean> {
    return true;
  }
}

=================== ./src/integrations/mock/MockSmsProvider.ts ===================
import type { SmsProvider, SendSmsResult } from '../ports/SmsProvider';

export class MockSmsProvider implements SmsProvider {
  name = 'کاوهنگار (شبیه‌ساز)';

  async send(_to: string, _message: string, _templateCode?: string): Promise<SendSmsResult> {
    // Simulated network delay
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 80));

    const messageId = `sms-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
    return {
      success: true,
      messageId,
    };
  }

  async checkHealth(): Promise<boolean> {
    return false; // Initially marked as not yet connected as required by sidebar card: «پیامک هنوز متصل نیست»
  }
}

  checkHealth(): Promise<boolean>;
}

=================== ./src/integrations/providerFactory.ts ===================
import type { SmsProvider } from './ports/SmsProvider';
import type { PaymentProvider } from './ports/PaymentProvider';
import type { WhatsAppProvider } from './ports/WhatsAppProvider';
import { MockSmsProvider } from './mock/MockSmsProvider';
import { MockPaymentProvider } from './mock/MockPaymentProvider';
import { MockWhatsAppProvider } from './mock/MockWhatsAppProvider';
import { env } from '../config/env';

export const createSmsProvider = (_type: string = env.smsProvider): SmsProvider => {
  return new MockSmsProvider();
};

export const createPaymentProvider = (_type: string = env.paymentProvider): PaymentProvider => {
  return new MockPaymentProvider();
};

export const createWhatsAppProvider = (): WhatsAppProvider => {
  return new MockWhatsAppProvider();
};

=================== ./src/integrations/registry.ts ===================
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import type { IntegrationStatus, SidebarServiceStatus } from '../domain/integration';
import {
  createSmsProvider,
  createPaymentProvider,
  createWhatsAppProvider,
} from './providerFactory';

export class IntegrationRegistry {
  private static instance: IntegrationRegistry;
  public smsProvider = createSmsProvider();
  public whatsappProvider = createWhatsAppProvider();
  public paymentProvider = createPaymentProvider();

  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  // Observable connection status
  private statuses: IntegrationStatus[] = [
    {
      id: 'online_booking',
      title: 'سامانه رزرو آنلاین نوبت',
      providerName: 'MedFlow Booking Engine',
      status: 'connected',
      statusLabel: 'فعال و برخط',
      hint: 'لینک عمومی رزرو در دسترس مراجعان است.',
      lastCheckedAt: 'هم‌اکنون',
    },
    {
      id: 'sms',
      title: 'ارسال پیامک اطلاع‌رسانی',
      providerName: 'کاوهنگار',
      status: 'not_connected',
      statusLabel: 'هنوز متصل نیست',
      hint: 'کلید API پنل پیامک را در تنظیمات وارد فرمایید.',
      lastCheckedAt: 'امروز ۰۹:۰۰',
    },
    {
      id: 'whatsapp',
      title: 'واتساپ تجاری خودکار',
      providerName: 'Cloud WhatsApp API',
      status: 'connected',
      statusLabel: 'متصل',
      hint: 'پیام‌های یادآوری و خوش‌آمدگویی فعال هستند.',
      lastCheckedAt: 'امروز ۱۰:۳۰',
    },
    {
      id: 'payment',
      title: 'درگاه پرداخت بیعانه آنلاین',
      providerName: 'زرین‌پال',
      status: 'connected',
      statusLabel: 'متصل',
      hint: 'تسویه حساب روزانه روی شماره شبا ثبت‌شده انجام می‌شود.',
      lastCheckedAt: 'امروز ۰۸:۰۰',
    },
  ];

  private cachedStatuses: IntegrationStatus[] = [];
  private cachedSidebarStatus: SidebarServiceStatus = {
    bookingActive: true,
    smsConnected: false,
    bookingStatusText: 'رزرو آنلاین فعال است',
    smsStatusText: 'پیامک هنوز متصل نیست',
  };

  private constructor() {
    this.updateCaches();
  }

  public static getInstance(): IntegrationRegistry {
    if (!IntegrationRegistry.instance) {
      IntegrationRegistry.instance = new IntegrationRegistry();
    }
    return IntegrationRegistry.instance;
  }

  private updateCaches(): void {
    this.cachedStatuses = [...this.statuses];
    const booking = this.statuses.find((s) => s.id === 'online_booking');
    const sms = this.statuses.find((s) => s.id === 'sms');

    const bookingActive = booking?.status === 'connected';
    const smsConnected = sms?.status === 'connected';

    this.cachedSidebarStatus = {
      bookingActive,
      smsConnected,
      bookingStatusText: bookingActive ? 'رزرو آنلاین فعال است' : 'رزرو آنلاین غیرفعال است',
      smsStatusText: smsConnected ? 'پیامک متصل است' : 'پیامک هنوز متصل نیست',
    };
  }

  public subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  };

  private notify(): void {
    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
  }

  public getAllStatuses = (): IntegrationStatus[] => {
    return this.cachedStatuses;
  };

  public getSidebarStatus = (): SidebarServiceStatus => {
    return this.cachedSidebarStatus;
  };

  public updateStatus(id: string, status: 'connected' | 'not_connected' | 'disconnected'): void {
    const item = this.statuses.find((s) => s.id === id);
    if (item) {
      const canonicalStatus = status === 'connected' ? 'connected' : 'not_connected';
      item.status = canonicalStatus;
      item.statusLabel = canonicalStatus === 'connected' ? 'متصل' : 'قطع شده';
      this.updateCaches();
      this.notify();
    }
  }

  public isConnected(id: string): boolean {
    const item = this.statuses.find((s) => s.id === id);
    return item?.status === 'connected';
  }
}

export const integrationRegistry = IntegrationRegistry.getInstance();

export const useIntegrationStatuses = (): IntegrationStatus[] => {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    integrationRegistry.subscribe,
    integrationRegistry.getAllStatuses,
    integrationRegistry.getAllStatuses
  );
};

export const useSidebarServiceStatus = (): SidebarServiceStatus => {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    integrationRegistry.subscribe,
    integrationRegistry.getSidebarStatus,
    integrationRegistry.getSidebarStatus
  );
};

=================== ./src/lib/format.ts ===================
const PERSIAN_DIGITS = ['۰', '۱', '۲', '۳', '۴', '۵', '۶', '۷', '۸', '۹'];
const ARABIC_DIGITS  = ['٠', '١', '٢', '٣', '٤', '٥', '٦', '٧', '٨', '٩'];
const ARABIC_TO_PERSIAN: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries(
  ARABIC_DIGITS.map((d, i) => [d, PERSIAN_DIGITS[i]])
);

/**
 * Converts English and Arabic digits to Persian digits.
 */
export const toPersianDigits = (value: number | string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value)
    .replace(/[0-9]/g, (ch) => PERSIAN_DIGITS[ch.charCodeAt(0) - 48])
    .replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (ch) => ARABIC_TO_PERSIAN[ch] ?? ch);
};

/**
 * Converts Persian and Arabic digits to standard English ASCII digits.
 */
export const toEnglishDigits = (value: string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!value) return '';
  let str = value;
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    str = str.replace(new RegExp(PERSIAN_DIGITS[i], 'g'), i.toString());
    str = str.replace(new RegExp(ARABIC_DIGITS[i], 'g'), i.toString());
  }
  return str;
};

const currencyFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR', {
  maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  useGrouping: true,
});

/**
 * Formats a monetary amount in Tomans with Persian thousands separator (٬).
 * e.g. 12800000 -> "۱۲٬۸۰۰٬۰۰۰ تومان"
 */
export const formatCurrency = (amount: number): string =>
  `${currencyFormatter.format(Math.round(amount)).replace(/,/g, '٬')} تومان`;

/**
 * Formats duration in minutes to Persian representation.
 */
export const formatDuration = (minutes: number): string => {
  return `${toPersianDigits(minutes)} دقیقه`;
};

/**
 * Returns initials from a Persian name for avatars.
 */
export const getInitials = (fullName: string): string => {
  if (!fullName) return '';
  const parts = fullName.trim().split(/\s+/);
  if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].charAt(0);
  return `${parts[0].charAt(0)}`;
};

/**
 * Formats canonical time (HH:mm) into Persian digits (e.g. "09:00" -> "۰۹:۰۰").
 */
export const formatTimeFa = (time: string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!time) return '';
  return toPersianDigits(time);
};

/**
 * Formats tracking code with Persian digits (e.g. "AR-8492" -> "AR-۸۴۹۲").
 */
export const formatTrackingCodeFa = (code: string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!code) return '';
  return toPersianDigits(code);
};

export { formatPhoneNumber } from './phone';


=================== ./src/lib/jalali.ts ===================
import { toPersianDigits } from './format';

/**
 * Jalali date helpers using the browser's native Intl API with 'fa-IR-u-ca-persian' calendar.
 */

export interface JalaliDay {
  dateString: string; // YYYY-MM-DD (ISO)
  dayName: string; // e.g. "شنبه"
  dayNumber: string; // e.g. "۲۶"
  monthName: string; // e.g. "مهر"
  formatted: string; // e.g. "شنبه ۲۶ مهر"
  isToday: boolean;
  isTomorrow: boolean;
  isWorkingDay: boolean;
}

const persianFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR-u-ca-persian', {
  year: 'numeric',
  month: 'numeric',
  day: 'numeric',
});

const persianFullFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR-u-ca-persian', {
  weekday: 'long',
  year: 'numeric',
  month: 'long',
  day: 'numeric',
});

const persianWeekdayFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR-u-ca-persian', {
  weekday: 'long',
});

const persianMonthFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR-u-ca-persian', {
  month: 'long',
});

const persianDayFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR-u-ca-persian', {
  day: 'numeric',
});

/**
 * Returns formatted Jalali date string for a given Date.
 */
export const formatJalaliDate = (date: Date = new Date()): string => {
  return persianFormatter.format(date);
};

/**
 * Returns long Persian date representation.
 * e.g. "یکشنبه ۲۶ مهر ۱۴۰۵"
 */
export const formatJalaliDateFull = (date: Date = new Date()): string => {
  return persianFullFormatter.format(date);
};

/**
 * Formats ISO date string (YYYY-MM-DD) to Jalali date string (e.g. "۱۴۰۵/۷/۲۶").
 */
export const formatIsoToJalali = (dateStr: string): string => {
  if (!dateStr) return '';
  if (dateStr.includes('-')) {
    const parts = dateStr.split('T')[0].split('-').map(Number);
    if (parts.length === 3 && !parts.some(isNaN)) {
      const date = new Date(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2]);
      return persianFormatter.format(date);
    }
  }
  return toPersianDigits(dateStr);
};

/**
 * Formats ISO date string (YYYY-MM-DD) to full Persian date string (e.g. "یکشنبه ۲۶ مهر ۱۴۰۵").
 */
export const formatIsoToJalaliFull = (dateStr: string): string => {
  if (!dateStr) return '';
  if (dateStr.includes('-')) {
    const parts = dateStr.split('T')[0].split('-').map(Number);
    if (parts.length === 3 && !parts.some(isNaN)) {
      const date = new Date(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2]);
      return persianFullFormatter.format(date);
    }
  }
  return toPersianDigits(dateStr);
};

/**
 * Generates the upcoming working days (Saturday through Thursday) for booking.
 * Friday (جمعه) is usually closed or skipped based on clinic hours.
 */
export const getUpcomingWorkingDays = (count: number = 14): JalaliDay[] => {
  const days: JalaliDay[] = [];
  const today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

  let generated = 0;

  // Search forward up to 30 days to get 'count' working days
  for (let i = 0; i < 30 && generated < count; i++) {
    const d = new Date(today.getTime());
    d.setDate(today.getDate() + i);

    const dayOfWeek = d.getDay(); // 0 is Sunday, 5 is Friday, 6 is Saturday
    const isFriday = dayOfWeek === 5;

    // Clinic is open Saturday to Thursday (skip Friday)
    if (!isFriday) {
      const isToday = i === 0;
      const isTomorrow = i === 1;
      const dayName = persianWeekdayFormatter.format(d);
      const dayNumber = toPersianDigits(persianDayFormatter.format(d));
      const monthName = persianMonthFormatter.format(d);

      const year = d.getFullYear();
      const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
      const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
      const dateString = `${year}-${month}-${day}`;

      days.push({
        dateString,
        dayName,
        dayNumber,
        monthName,
        formatted: `${dayName} ${dayNumber} ${monthName}`,
        isToday,
        isTomorrow,
        isWorkingDay: true,
      });

      generated++;
    }
  }

  return days;
};

/**
 * Returns ISO date string (YYYY-MM-DD) for today.
 */
export const getTodayDateString = (): string => {
  const d = new Date();
  const year = d.getFullYear();
  const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

/**
 * Returns ISO date string (YYYY-MM-DD) for tomorrow.
 */
export const getTomorrowDateString = (): string => {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
  const year = d.getFullYear();
  const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

=================== ./src/lib/phone.ts ===================
import { toEnglishDigits, toPersianDigits } from './format';

const IRANIAN_MOBILE_REGEX = /^09\d{9}$/;

/**
 * Normalizes an input phone number to standard Iranian mobile format (09XXXXXXXXX).
 * Handles:
 * - Persian and Arabic digits
 * - Spaces, dashes, parentheses
 * - Country code prefixes (+98, 0098, 98)
 */
export const normalizePhoneNumber = (raw: string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!raw) return '';
  let cleaned = toEnglishDigits(raw).replace(/[\s\-().]/g, '');

  if (cleaned.startsWith('+98')) {
    cleaned = '0' + cleaned.substring(3);
  } else if (cleaned.startsWith('0098')) {
    cleaned = '0' + cleaned.substring(4);
  } else if (cleaned.startsWith('98') && cleaned.length === 12) {
    cleaned = '0' + cleaned.substring(2);
  } else if (cleaned.startsWith('9') && cleaned.length === 10) {
    cleaned = '0' + cleaned;
  }

  return cleaned;
};

/**
 * Validates whether a phone number matches Iranian mobile format.
 */
export const isValidIranianMobile = (phone: string | null | undefined): boolean => {
  const normalized = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
  return IRANIAN_MOBILE_REGEX.test(normalized);
};

/**
 * Formats a phone number for user-friendly display in Persian digits.
 * e.g. "09124567890" -> "۰۹۱۲ ۴۵۶ ۷۸۹۰"
 */
export const formatPhoneNumber = (phone: string | null | undefined): string => {
  const normalized = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
  if (normalized.length === 11) {
    const formatted = `${normalized.slice(0, 4)} ${normalized.slice(4, 7)} ${normalized.slice(7)}`;
    return toPersianDigits(formatted);
  }
  return toPersianDigits(phone || '');
};

=================== ./src/lib/useClickOutside.ts ===================
import { useEffect, type RefObject } from 'react';

export function useClickOutside<T extends HTMLElement>(
  ref: RefObject<T>,
  handler: () => void,
  enabled: boolean = true
) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const listener = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      const el = ref.current;
      if (!el || el.contains(event.target as Node)) return;
      handler();
    };
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', listener);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', listener);
  }, [ref, handler, enabled]);
}

=================== ./src/lib/useDebounce.ts ===================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

/**
 * Custom hook to debounce any value (e.g. search input).
 * Delays updating the debounced value until after the specified delay has elapsed
 * since the last time the value changed.
 *
 * @param value The value to debounce
 * @param delay Delay in milliseconds (default: 300ms)
 * @returns The debounced value
 */
export function useDebounce<T>(value: T, delay: number = 300): T {
  const [debouncedValue, setDebouncedValue] = useState<T>(value);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = setTimeout(() => {
      setDebouncedValue(value);
    }, delay);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(handler);
    };
  }, [value, delay]);

  return debouncedValue;
}

/**
 * Calculates tomorrow and yesterday canonical dates (YYYY-MM-DD) from a base date.
 */
function getRelativeDate(baseDateStr: string, offsetDays: number): string {
  const parts = baseDateStr.split('-').map(Number);
  const d = new Date(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2]);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + offsetDays);
  const y = d.getFullYear();
  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
}

export interface SeedData {
  clinic: ClinicWorkspace;
  users: UserProfile[];
  services: Service[];
  doctors: Doctor[];
  appointments: Appointment[];
  clients: Client[];
  leads: Lead[];
  automations: Automation[];
  nextActions: DashboardNextAction[];
}

  const tomorrow = getRelativeDate(today, 1);

  const appointments: Appointment[] = [

    {
