=================== ./src/pages/ForbiddenPage.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { ShieldAlert, ArrowRight, Home } from 'lucide-react';
import { ButtonLink } from '../components/ui/ButtonLink';
import { useAuth } from '../app/contexts';

export const ForbiddenPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { role, user } = useAuth();

  const getHomeRoute = () => {
    if (role === 'doctor') return '/doctor';
    if (role === 'patient') return '/me';
    return '/';
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafafa] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="max-w-md w-full bg-white rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 shadow-sm p-8 text-center space-y-6">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-rose-600">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-8 h-8" />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-600 bg-rose-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
            خطای ۴۰۳ — عدم دسترسی
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-neutral-900 mt-2">
            دسترسی به این بخش مجاز نمی‌باشد
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed">
            کاربر گرامی {user?.name || ''}، نقش شما ({user?.roleTitle || role || 'مهمان'}) مجوزهای امنیتی لازم برای مشاهده یا ویرایش این بخش از سامانه مدفلو را ندارد.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-2 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-3">
          <ButtonLink to={getHomeRoute()} variant="primary" size="md" className="w-full sm:w-auto flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
            <Home className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>بازگشت به پنل اصلی</span>
          </ButtonLink>
          <ButtonLink to="/login" variant="outline" size="md" className="w-full sm:w-auto flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
            <span>ورود با حساب دیگر</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </ButtonLink>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/pages/NotFoundPage.tsx ===================
import React from 'react';
import { ButtonLink } from '../components/ui/ButtonLink';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { HelpCircle, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';

export const NotFoundPage: React.FC = () => {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafafa] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <Card className="max-w-md w-full text-center p-8 space-y-5">
        <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-600 rounded-full mx-auto flex items-center justify-center">
          <HelpCircle className="w-7 h-7" />
        </div>

        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-neutral-900">
            صفحه مورد نظر یافت نشد
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-2">
            آدرس وارد شده معتبر نمی‌باشد یا این صفحه حذف گردیده است.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-2">
          <ButtonLink to="/" variant="primary" className="gap-2 mx-auto">
            <span>بازگشت به داشبورد اصلی</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </ButtonLink>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
};

=================== ./src/services/adapters/http/HttpAuditRepository.ts ===================
import type { IAuditRepository } from '../../repositories/IAuditRepository';
import type { AuditLogEntry, AuditAction } from '../../../domain/audit';
import type { Role } from '../../../domain/session';
import { httpClient } from '../../api/httpClient';
import { toAuditLogDto } from './mappers';

export class HttpAuditRepository implements IAuditRepository {
  async getLogs(clinicId: string): Promise<AuditLogEntry[]> {
    return httpClient.get<AuditLogEntry[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/audit-logs`);
  }

  async recordLog(params: {
    clinicId: string;
    userId: string;
    userName: string;
    role: Role;
    action: AuditAction;
    details?: string;
  }): Promise<void> {
    const payload = toAuditLogDto(params);
    await httpClient.post(`/clinics/${params.clinicId}/audit-logs`, payload);
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/http/HttpAuthRepository.ts ===================
import type {
  IAuthRepository,
  RequestOtpResult,
  VerifyOtpResult,
  CreateUserInput,
} from '../../repositories/IAuthRepository';
import type { UserProfile, UserSession, Role, ClinicWorkspace } from '../../../domain/session';
import { httpClient, setAuthToken } from '../../api/httpClient';

interface UserProfileDto {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  role: Role;
  role_title: string;
  avatar_initials: string;
  email?: string;
  phone: string;
  doctor_id?: string;
  client_id?: string;
  clinic_id?: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

interface ClinicWorkspaceDto {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  tagline: string;
  phone: string;
  address: string;
  working_hours: string;
}

interface RequestOtpResponseDto {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  expires_in_seconds: number;
}

interface VerifyOtpResponseDto {
  success: boolean;
  access_token: string;
  user: UserProfileDto;
  active_clinic: ClinicWorkspaceDto;
  available_clinics: ClinicWorkspaceDto[];
}

function mapUserDtoToDomain(dto: UserProfileDto): UserProfile {
  return {
    id: dto.id,
    name: dto.name,
    role: dto.role,
    roleTitle: dto.role_title,
    avatarInitials: dto.avatar_initials,
    email: dto.email,
    phone: dto.phone,
    doctorId: dto.doctor_id,
    clientId: dto.client_id,
    clinicId: dto.clinic_id,
    isActive: dto.is_active,
    createdAt: dto.created_at,
  };
}

function mapClinicDtoToDomain(dto: ClinicWorkspaceDto): ClinicWorkspace {
  return {
    id: dto.id,
    slug: dto.slug,
    name: dto.name,
    tagline: dto.tagline,
    phone: dto.phone,
    address: dto.address,
    workingHours: dto.working_hours,
  };
}

export class HttpAuthRepository implements IAuthRepository {
  private inFlightRefreshPromise: Promise<UserSession | null> | null = null;

  async requestOtp(phone: string): Promise<RequestOtpResult> {
    const res = await httpClient.post<RequestOtpResponseDto>('/auth/otp/request', {
      phone,
    });
    return {
      success: res.success,
      message: res.message,
      expiresInSeconds: res.expires_in_seconds,
    };
  }

  async verifyOtp(phone: string, code: string): Promise<VerifyOtpResult> {
    const res = await httpClient.post<VerifyOtpResponseDto>('/auth/otp/verify', {
      phone,
      code,
    });

    if (res.access_token) {
      setAuthToken(res.access_token);
    }

    const session: UserSession = {
      user: mapUserDtoToDomain(res.user),
      activeClinic: mapClinicDtoToDomain(res.active_clinic),
      availableClinics: (res.available_clinics || []).map(mapClinicDtoToDomain),
      isAuthenticated: true,
      token: res.access_token,
    };

    return {
      success: res.success,
      session,
    };
  }

  async verifyPhoneOwnership(phone: string, code: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; verificationToken?: string; error?: string }> {
    try {
      const res = await httpClient.post<{ success: boolean; verification_token: string; message?: string }>('/auth/otp/verify-phone', {
        phone,
        code,
      });
      return {
        success: res.success,
        verificationToken: res.verification_token,
      };
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در تأیید مالکیت شماره تلفن.',
      };
    }
  }

  async getCurrentUser(): Promise<UserSession | null> {
    try {
      const res = await httpClient.get<{
        user: UserProfileDto;
        active_clinic: ClinicWorkspaceDto;
        available_clinics: ClinicWorkspaceDto[];
      }>('/auth/me');

      return {
        user: mapUserDtoToDomain(res.user),
        activeClinic: mapClinicDtoToDomain(res.active_clinic),
        availableClinics: (res.available_clinics || []).map(mapClinicDtoToDomain),
        isAuthenticated: true,
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async logout(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await httpClient.post('/auth/logout');
    } finally {
      setAuthToken(null);
    }
  }

  async refreshSession(): Promise<UserSession | null> {
    if (this.inFlightRefreshPromise) {
      return this.inFlightRefreshPromise;
    }

    this.inFlightRefreshPromise = (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await httpClient.post<{
          access_token: string;
          user: UserProfileDto;
        }>('/auth/refresh');

        if (res.access_token) {
          setAuthToken(res.access_token);
        }

        const currentUser = await this.getCurrentUser();
        if (!currentUser) return null;
        return {
          ...currentUser,
          token: res.access_token,
        };
      } catch {
        return null;
      } finally {
        this.inFlightRefreshPromise = null;
      }
    })();

    return this.inFlightRefreshPromise;
  }

  async updateProfile(userId: string, input: { name: string }): Promise<UserProfile> {
    const res = await httpClient.patch<UserProfileDto>(`/auth/profile/${userId}`, {
      name: input.name,
    });
    return mapUserDtoToDomain(res);
  }

  async getUsers(clinicId: string): Promise<UserProfile[]> {
    const res = await httpClient.get<UserProfileDto[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/users`);
    return res.map(mapUserDtoToDomain);
  }

  async createUser(clinicId: string, input: CreateUserInput): Promise<UserProfile> {
    const res = await httpClient.post<UserProfileDto>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/users`, {
      name: input.name,
      phone: input.phone,
      role: input.role,
      role_title: input.roleTitle,
      email: input.email,
      doctor_id: input.doctorId,
    });
    return mapUserDtoToDomain(res);
  }

  async updateUserRole(
    clinicId: string,
    userId: string,
    newRole: Role,
    _actor?: unknown,
    doctorId?: string
  ): Promise<UserProfile> {
    const res = await httpClient.patch<UserProfileDto>(
      `/clinics/${clinicId}/users/${userId}/role`,
      { role: newRole, doctor_id: doctorId }
    );
    return mapUserDtoToDomain(res);
  }

  async toggleUserStatus(clinicId: string, userId: string): Promise<UserProfile> {
    const res = await httpClient.patch<UserProfileDto>(
      `/clinics/${clinicId}/users/${userId}/status`
    );
    return mapUserDtoToDomain(res);
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/http/index.ts ===================
import { httpClient, ApiError } from '../../api/httpClient';
import type { IAppointmentRepository, AppointmentFilters } from '../../repositories/IAppointmentRepository';
import type { IClientRepository } from '../../repositories/IClientRepository';
import type { ILeadRepository } from '../../repositories/ILeadRepository';
import type { ICatalogRepository } from '../../repositories/ICatalogRepository';
import type { IAutomationRepository } from '../../repositories/IAutomationRepository';
import type { IDashboardRepository } from '../../repositories/IDashboardRepository';
import type { IBookingRepository, BookingSubmissionInput } from '../../repositories/IBookingRepository';
import type { IMessageRepository } from '../../repositories/IMessageRepository';
import type { Appointment, CreateAppointmentInput } from '../../../domain/appointment';
import type { AppointmentStatus } from '../../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import type { Client } from '../../../domain/client';
import type { Lead } from '../../../domain/lead';
import type { Service } from '../../../domain/service';
import type { Doctor } from '../../../domain/doctor';
import type { Automation, AutomationEvent, AutomationStats, OutboxMessage } from '../../../domain/automation';
import type { DashboardSummary, DashboardNextAction } from '../../../domain/dashboard';
import type { TimeSlot } from '../../../domain/slot';
import type { ClinicWorkspace, Actor } from '../../../domain/session';
import {
  mapAppointmentDtoToDomain,
  mapClientDtoToDomain,
  mapLeadDtoToDomain,
  mapServiceDtoToDomain,
  mapDoctorDtoToDomain,
  toAppointmentCreateDto,
  toCheckConflictDto,
  toBookingSubmissionDto,
  toOutboxMessageDto,
} from './mappers';
import type {
  AppointmentDto,
  ClientDto,
  LeadDto,
  ServiceDto,
  DoctorDto,
} from './mappers';

export class HttpAppointmentRepository implements IAppointmentRepository {
  async getAppointments(clinicId: string, filters?: AppointmentFilters): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const dtos = await httpClient.get<AppointmentDto[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/appointments`, {
      params: filters as Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined>,
    });
    return dtos.map(mapAppointmentDtoToDomain);
  }

  async getTodayQueue(clinicId: string, limit: number = 5): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const dtos = await httpClient.get<AppointmentDto[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/appointments/today`, {
      params: { limit },
    });
    return dtos.map(mapAppointmentDtoToDomain);
  }

  async getAppointmentById(clinicId: string, id: string): Promise<Appointment | null> {
    try {
      const dto = await httpClient.get<AppointmentDto>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/appointments/${id}`);
      return dto ? mapAppointmentDtoToDomain(dto) : null;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
        return null;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  async createAppointment(clinicId: string, input: CreateAppointmentInput, actor?: Actor): Promise<Appointment> {
    if (actor?.role === 'patient') {
      throw new Error('دسترسی غیرمجاز: مراجع مجاز به ثبت نوبت جدید نمیباشد.');
    }
    const payload = toAppointmentCreateDto(input);
    const dto = await httpClient.post<AppointmentDto>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/appointments`, payload);
    return mapAppointmentDtoToDomain(dto);
  }

  async updateStatus(clinicId: string, id: string, newStatus: AppointmentStatus, actor?: Actor): Promise<Appointment> {
    if (actor?.role === 'patient' && newStatus !== 'cancelled') {
      throw new Error('دسترسی غیرمجاز: مراجع مجاز به ثبت نوبت جدید نمیباشد.');
    }
    const dto = await httpClient.patch<AppointmentDto>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/appointments/${id}/status`, {
      status: newStatus,
    });
    return mapAppointmentDtoToDomain(dto);
  }

  async checkConflict(clinicId: string, doctorId: string, date: string, time: string, durationMinutes: number): Promise<boolean> {
    const payload = toCheckConflictDto({ doctorId, date, time, durationMinutes });
    const res = await httpClient.post<{ hasConflict: boolean }>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/appointments/check-conflict`, payload);
    return res.hasConflict;
  }

  async updateMedicalNotes(clinicId: string, id: string, notes: string): Promise<void> {
    await httpClient.patch(`/clinics/${clinicId}/appointments/${id}/medical-notes`, {
      medical_notes: notes,
    });
  }
}

export class HttpClientRepository implements IClientRepository {
  async getClients(clinicId: string, search?: string): Promise<Client[]> {
    const dtos = await httpClient.get<ClientDto[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/clients`, { params: { search } });
    return dtos.map(mapClientDtoToDomain);
  }

  async getAtRiskClients(clinicId: string): Promise<Client[]> {
    const dtos = await httpClient.get<ClientDto[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/clients/at-risk`);
    return dtos.map(mapClientDtoToDomain);
  }

  async getClientById(clinicId: string, id: string): Promise<Client | null> {
    try {
      const dto = await httpClient.get<ClientDto>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/clients/${id}`);
      return dto ? mapClientDtoToDomain(dto) : null;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
        return null;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }
}

export class HttpCatalogRepository implements ICatalogRepository {

    return dtos.map(mapServiceDtoToDomain);
  }

  async getDoctors(clinicId: string): Promise<Doctor[]> {

      return dto ? mapServiceDtoToDomain(dto) : null;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
        return null;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  async getDoctorById(clinicId: string, id: string): Promise<Doctor | null> {
    try {
      const dto = await httpClient.get<DoctorDto>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/catalog/doctors/${id}`);
      return dto ? mapDoctorDtoToDomain(dto) : null;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
        return null;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  async getClinicBySlug(slug: string): Promise<ClinicWorkspace | null> {
    try {
      const dto = await httpClient.get<ClinicWorkspace>(`/public/clinics/${slug}`);
      return dto || null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

export class HttpAutomationRepository implements IAutomationRepository {
  async getAutomations(clinicId: string): Promise<Automation[]> {
    return httpClient.get<Automation[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/automations`);
  }

  async getStats(clinicId: string): Promise<AutomationStats> {
    return httpClient.get<AutomationStats>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/automations/stats`);
  }

  async setEnabled(clinicId: string, id: string, enabled: boolean): Promise<void> {
    await httpClient.patch(`/clinics/${clinicId}/automations/${id}`, { enabled });
  }

  async triggerEvent(clinicId: string, event: AutomationEvent, payload: Record<string, any>): Promise<void> {
    await httpClient.post(`/clinics/${clinicId}/automations/events`, { event, payload });
  }
}

export class HttpDashboardRepository implements IDashboardRepository {
  async getSummary(clinicId: string): Promise<DashboardSummary> {
    return httpClient.get<DashboardSummary>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/dashboard/summary`);
  }

  async getNextActions(clinicId: string): Promise<DashboardNextAction[]> {
    return httpClient.get<DashboardNextAction[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/dashboard/next-actions`);
  }
}

export class HttpBookingRepository implements IBookingRepository {
  async getAvailableSlots(params: { clinicId: string; doctorId: string; serviceId: string; date: string }): Promise<TimeSlot[]> {
    return httpClient.get<TimeSlot[]>(`/public/clinics/${params.clinicId}/booking/slots`, { params });
  }

  async submitBooking(input: BookingSubmissionInput): Promise<{ appointment: Appointment; trackingCode: string }> {
    const payload = toBookingSubmissionDto(input);
    const res = await httpClient.post<{ appointment: AppointmentDto; tracking_code: string }>(
      `/public/clinics/${input.clinicId}/booking`,
      payload
    );
    return {
      appointment: mapAppointmentDtoToDomain(res.appointment),
      trackingCode: res.tracking_code,
    };
  }
}

export class HttpMessageRepository implements IMessageRepository {
  async getOutboxMessages(clinicId: string): Promise<OutboxMessage[]> {
    return httpClient.get<OutboxMessage[]>(`/clinics/${clinicId}/messages/outbox`);
  }

  async saveMessage(message: Omit<OutboxMessage, 'id' | 'createdAt'>): Promise<OutboxMessage> {
    const payload = toOutboxMessageDto(message);
    return httpClient.post<OutboxMessage>(`/clinics/${message.clinicId}/messages/outbox`, payload);
  }
}

export { HttpAuthRepository } from './HttpAuthRepository';
export { HttpAuditRepository } from './HttpAuditRepository';


=================== ./src/services/adapters/http/mappers.ts ===================
import type { Appointment } from '../../../domain/appointment';
import type { AppointmentStatus } from '../../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import type { Client } from '../../../domain/client';
import type { Lead } from '../../../domain/lead';
import type { Service } from '../../../domain/service';
import type { Doctor } from '../../../domain/doctor';

export interface AppointmentDto {
  id: string;
  clinic_id: string;
  tracking_code: string;
  appointment_date: string;
  appointment_time: string;
  client_id?: string;
  client_name: string;
  client_phone: string;
  service_id: string;
  service_name: string;
  doctor_id: string;
  doctor_name: string;
  duration_minutes: number;
  price: number;
  status: AppointmentStatus;
  source: 'online_booking' | 'reception_manual' | 'followup';
  notes?: string;
  medical_notes?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface ClientDto {
  id: string;
  clinic_id: string;
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  email?: string;
  status: 'active' | 'at_risk' | 'churned' | 'new';
  total_visits: number;
  total_spent: number;
  last_visit_date?: string;
  recovery_value?: number;
  notes?: string;
  created_at: string;
}

  phone: string;
  interested_service: string;
  status: 'new' | 'hot' | 'contacted' | 'converted' | 'dropped';
  is_decision_ready: boolean;
  score: number;
  source: string;
  estimated_value: number;
  created_at: string;
}

  id: string;

  duration_minutes: number;

  avatar_url?: string;
  is_active: boolean;
}

  serviceName: dto.service_name,
  doctorId: dto.doctor_id,
  doctorName: dto.doctor_name,
  durationMinutes: dto.duration_minutes,
  price: dto.price,
  status: dto.status,
  source: dto.source,
  notes: dto.notes,
  medicalNotes: dto.medical_notes,
  createdAt: dto.created_at,
  updatedAt: dto.updated_at,
});

  clinicId: dto.clinic_id,
  name: dto.name,
  phone: dto.phone,
  email: dto.email,
  status: dto.status,

});

  interestedService: dto.interested_service,

  createdAt: dto.created_at,
});

  name: dto.name,
  duration: dto.duration_minutes,
  price: dto.price,
  category: dto.category,
  description: dto.description,
  isActive: dto.is_active,
});

  specialty: dto.specialty,
  startHour: dto.start_hour,

/* =========================================================================
   Request DTOs & Mappers (JSON on the wire — snake_case)
   ========================================================================= */

export interface CreateAppointmentRequestDto {
  clinic_id: string;
  appointment_date: string;
  appointment_time: string;
  client_name: string;
  client_phone: string;
  service_id: string;
  doctor_id: string;
  source: string;
  notes?: string;
  medical_notes?: string;
}

export const toAppointmentCreateDto = (
  input: import('../../../domain/appointment').CreateAppointmentInput
): CreateAppointmentRequestDto => ({
  clinic_id: input.clinicId,
  appointment_date: input.date,
  appointment_time: input.time,
  client_name: input.clientName,
  client_phone: input.clientPhone,
  service_id: input.serviceId,
  doctor_id: input.doctorId,
  source: input.source,
  notes: input.notes,
  medical_notes: input.medicalNotes,
});

export interface CheckConflictRequestDto {
  doctor_id: string;
  date: string;
  time: string;
  duration_minutes: number;
}

export const toCheckConflictDto = (params: {
  doctorId: string;
  date: string;
  time: string;
  durationMinutes: number;
}): CheckConflictRequestDto => ({
  doctor_id: params.doctorId,
  date: params.date,
  time: params.time,
  duration_minutes: params.durationMinutes,
});

export interface BookingSubmissionRequestDto {
  clinic_id: string;
  service_id: string;
  doctor_id: string;
  date: string;
  time: string;
  client_name: string;
  client_phone: string;
  medical_notes?: string;
  notes?: string;
  captcha_token: string;
  verification_token: string;
}

  input: import('../../repositories/IBookingRepository').BookingSubmissionInput

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository.ts ===================
import type {
  IAppointmentRepository,
  AppointmentFilters,
} from '../../repositories/IAppointmentRepository';
import type { Appointment, CreateAppointmentInput } from '../../../domain/appointment';
import type { AppointmentStatus } from '../../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import type { Actor } from '../../../domain/session';
import { canTransition, canTransitionByRole } from '../../../domain/appointmentStatus';
import { can } from '../../../domain/permissions';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';
import { getTodayDateString } from '../../../lib/jalali';
import { toEnglishDigits, toPersianDigits } from '../../../lib/format';
import { MockAutomationRepository } from './MockAutomationRepository';
import { MockAuditRepository } from './MockAuditRepository';
import { normalizePhoneNumber } from '../../../lib/phone';

export class MockAppointmentRepository implements IAppointmentRepository {
  private auditRepo = new MockAuditRepository();

  private enrichAndFilterMedicalNotes(apt: Appointment, actor?: Actor): Appointment {
    const clone = { ...apt };
    if (!actor) {
      delete clone.medicalNotes;
      return clone;
    }

    const hasPermission = can(actor.role, 'medical_notes:read');
    const isDoctorOfThisApt = actor.role === 'doctor' && apt.doctorId === actor.doctorId;
    const isOwner = actor.role === 'owner';

    if (hasPermission && (isOwner || isDoctorOfThisApt)) {
      const storedNote = mockStorage.getMedicalNote(apt.id);
      clone.medicalNotes = storedNote || apt.medicalNotes || undefined;
    } else {
      delete clone.medicalNotes;
    }

    return clone;
  }

  async getAppointments(
    clinicId: string,
    filters?: AppointmentFilters,
    actor?: Actor
  ): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    await simulateDelay();
    let list = mockStorage.getAppointments().filter((apt) => apt.clinicId === clinicId);

    // Apply role-based scoping (Fail-closed: if doctor has no doctorId, return empty)
    if (actor?.role === 'doctor') {
      if (!actor.doctorId) {
        return [];
      }
      list = list.filter((apt) => apt.doctorId === actor.doctorId);
    } else if (actor?.role === 'patient') {
      const actorPhone = actor.phone ? normalizePhoneNumber(actor.phone) : '';
      list = list.filter((apt) => {
        const aptPhone = normalizePhoneNumber(apt.clientPhone);
        return (actorPhone && aptPhone === actorPhone) || (actor.clientId && apt.clientId === actor.clientId);
      });
    }

    // Apply explicit filters
    if (filters?.date) {
      list = list.filter((apt) => apt.date === filters.date);
    }
    if (filters?.status) {
      list = list.filter((apt) => apt.status === filters.status);
    }
    if (filters?.doctorId) {
      list = list.filter((apt) => apt.doctorId === filters.doctorId);
    }
    if (filters?.clientId) {
      list = list.filter((apt) => apt.clientId === filters.clientId);
    }
    if (filters?.search) {
      const qOriginal = filters.search.trim().toLowerCase();
      const qNorm = toEnglishDigits(filters.search).trim().toLowerCase();
      list = list.filter(
        (apt) =>
          apt.clientName.toLowerCase().includes(qOriginal) ||
          apt.clientPhone.includes(qNorm) ||
          toEnglishDigits(apt.clientPhone).includes(qNorm) ||
          apt.trackingCode.toLowerCase().includes(qNorm) ||
          apt.serviceName.toLowerCase().includes(qOriginal)
      );
    }

    return list.map((apt) => this.enrichAndFilterMedicalNotes(apt, actor));
  }

  async getTodayQueue(
    clinicId: string,
    limit: number = 5,
    actor?: Actor
  ): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    await simulateDelay();
    const today = getTodayDateString();
    let all = mockStorage.getAppointments().filter((apt) => apt.clinicId === clinicId && apt.date === today);

    // Fail-closed for doctor without doctorId
    if (actor?.role === 'doctor') {
      if (!actor.doctorId) {
        return [];
      }
      all = all.filter((apt) => apt.doctorId === actor.doctorId);
    }

    // Exclude cancelled and completed appointments
    all = all.filter((apt) => apt.status !== 'cancelled' && apt.status !== 'completed');

    // Sort ascending by time (canonical HH:mm)
    all.sort((a, b) => {
      const aTime = toEnglishDigits(a.time);
      const bTime = toEnglishDigits(b.time);
      return aTime.localeCompare(bTime);
    });

    const sliced = all.slice(0, limit);
    return sliced.map((apt) => this.enrichAndFilterMedicalNotes(apt, actor));
  }

  async getAppointmentById(
    clinicId: string,
    id: string,
    actor?: Actor
  ): Promise<Appointment | null> {
    await simulateDelay();
    const item = mockStorage
      .getAppointments()
      .find((apt) => apt.clinicId === clinicId && apt.id === id);

    if (!item) return null;

    // Fail-closed for doctor
    if (actor?.role === 'doctor') {
      if (!actor.doctorId || item.doctorId !== actor.doctorId) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    if (actor?.role === 'patient') {
      const actorPhone = actor.phone ? normalizePhoneNumber(actor.phone) : '';
      const aptPhone = normalizePhoneNumber(item.clientPhone);
      if (actorPhone !== aptPhone && actor.clientId !== item.clientId) {
        return null;
      }
    }

    const enriched = this.enrichAndFilterMedicalNotes(item, actor);
    if (enriched.medicalNotes && actor) {
      void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
        clinicId,
        userId: actor.userId,
        userName: actor.userName || actor.userId || actor.role,
        role: actor.role,
        action: 'medical_notes.view',
        details: `مشاهده یادداشت پزشکی نوبت ${item.trackingCode}`,
      });
    }

    return enriched;
  }

  async createAppointment(
    clinicId: string,
    input: CreateAppointmentInput,
    actor?: Actor
  ): Promise<Appointment> {
    if (actor?.role === 'patient') {
      throw new Error('دسترسی غیرمجاز: مراجع مجاز به ثبت نوبت جدید نمیباشد.');
    }
    await simulateDelay();
    const services = mockStorage.getServices();
    const doctors = mockStorage.getDoctors();

    const service = services.find((s) => s.id === input.serviceId);
    const doctor = doctors.find((d) => d.id === input.doctorId);

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const trackingNum = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
    const trackingCode = `AR-${toPersianDigits(trackingNum)}`;
    // Upsert client record based on phone AND name
    const normalizedPhone = normalizePhoneNumber(input.clientPhone);
    const normalizedInputName = input.clientName.trim();
    const allClients = mockStorage.getClients();
    
    // Look for exact match by phone AND name
    let client = allClients.find(
      (c) =>
        c.clinicId === clinicId &&
        normalizePhoneNumber(c.phone) === normalizedPhone &&
        c.name.trim() === normalizedInputName
    );

    // If no exact match, check if any existing client shares this phone (family member)
    const familyPrimaryClient = allClients.find(
      (c) => c.clinicId === clinicId && normalizePhoneNumber(c.phone) === normalizedPhone
    );

    if (!client) {
      const newClient = {
        id: `client-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`,
        clinicId,
        name: normalizedInputName,
        phone: normalizedPhone,
        totalVisits: 1,
        totalSpent: service?.price || 0,
        lastVisitDate: input.date,
        status: 'new' as const,
        parentClientId: familyPrimaryClient ? (familyPrimaryClient.parentClientId || familyPrimaryClient.id) : undefined,
        createdAt: now,
      };
      client = newClient;
      mockStorage.saveClients([newClient, ...allClients]);
    } else {
      client.totalVisits = (client.totalVisits || 0) + 1;
      client.totalSpent = (client.totalSpent || 0) + (service?.price || 0);
      client.lastVisitDate = input.date;
      mockStorage.saveClients([...allClients]);
    }
    const id = `apt-${Date.now()}`;

    // Store medicalNotes in isolated storage
    if (input.medicalNotes) {
      mockStorage.saveMedicalNote(id, input.medicalNotes);
    }

    const newApt: Appointment = {
      id,
      clinicId,
      clientId: client.id,
      trackingCode,
      date: input.date,
      time: input.time,
      clientName: input.clientName,
      clientPhone: input.clientPhone,
      serviceId: input.serviceId,
      serviceName: service?.name || 'خدمت کلینیک',
      doctorId: input.doctorId,
      doctorName: doctor?.name || 'پزشک معالج',
      durationMinutes: service?.duration || 30,
      price: service?.price || 0,
      status: 'pending_confirmation',
      source: input.source,
      notes: input.notes,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    };

    const current = mockStorage.getAppointments();
    mockStorage.saveAppointments([newApt, ...current]);

    return newApt;
  }

  async updateStatus(
    clinicId: string,
    id: string,
    newStatus: AppointmentStatus,
    actor?: Actor
  ): Promise<Appointment> {
    await simulateDelay();
    const appointments = mockStorage.getAppointments();
    const aptIndex = appointments.findIndex(
      (a) => a.clinicId === clinicId && a.id === id
    );

    if (aptIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('نوبت مورد نظر یافت نشد.');
    }

    const apt = appointments[aptIndex];

    // Ownership check: Doctor can only update their own appointments
    if (actor?.role === 'doctor') {
      if (!actor.doctorId || apt.doctorId !== actor.doctorId) {
        throw new Error('دسترسی غیرمجاز: پزشک تنها مجاز به مدیریت نوبت‌های خود می‌باشد.');
      }
    }

    // Ownership check: Patient can only update their own appointments
    if (actor?.role === 'patient') {
      if (newStatus !== 'cancelled') {
        throw new Error('دسترسی غیرمجاز: مراجع مجاز به ثبت نوبت جدید نمیباشد.');
      }
      const actorPhone = actor.phone ? normalizePhoneNumber(actor.phone) : '';
      const aptPhone = normalizePhoneNumber(apt.clientPhone);
      const matchPhone = Boolean(actorPhone && aptPhone === actorPhone);
      const matchClient = Boolean(actor.clientId && apt.clientId === actor.clientId);
      if (!matchPhone && !matchClient) {
        throw new Error('دسترسی غیرمجاز: بیمار تنها مجاز به مدیریت نوبت خود می‌باشد.');
      }
    }

    // State transition validation (with role check if actor provided)
    const isAllowed = actor
      ? canTransitionByRole(apt.status, newStatus, actor.role)
      : canTransition(apt.status, newStatus);

    if (!isAllowed) {
      throw new Error(`نقش ${actor?.role || 'جاری'} مجاز به تغییر وضعیت از «${apt.status}» به «${newStatus}» نمی‌باشد.`);
    }

    const updated: Appointment = {
      ...apt,
      status: newStatus,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    appointments[aptIndex] = updated;
    mockStorage.saveAppointments([...appointments]);

    // Record audit log
    if (actor) {
      void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
        clinicId,
        userId: actor.userId,
        userName: actor.userName || actor.userId || actor.role,
        role: actor.role,
        action: 'appointment.status_change',
        details: `تغییر وضعیت نوبت ${apt.trackingCode} از ${apt.status} به ${newStatus}`,
      });
    }

    // Trigger automation event on confirmation
    if (newStatus === 'confirmed') {
      const automationRepo = new MockAutomationRepository();
      await automationRepo.triggerEvent(clinicId, 'appointment.confirmed', {
        name: apt.clientName,
        clientName: apt.clientName,
        phone: apt.clientPhone,
        service: apt.serviceName,
        time: apt.time,
      });
    }

    // Trigger automation event on visit completed
    if (newStatus === 'completed') {
      const automationRepo = new MockAutomationRepository();
      await automationRepo.triggerEvent(clinicId, 'visit.completed', {
        name: apt.clientName,
        clientName: apt.clientName,
        phone: apt.clientPhone,
        service: apt.serviceName,
        time: apt.time,
      });
    }

    return updated;
  }

  async checkConflict(
    clinicId: string,
    doctorId: string,
    date: string,
    time: string,
    durationMinutes: number
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    await simulateDelay(40);
    const appointments = mockStorage.getAppointments();
    const cleanTime = toEnglishDigits(time).trim();
    const [h, m] = cleanTime.split(':').map(Number);
    const targetStart = h * 60 + m;
    const targetEnd = targetStart + durationMinutes;

    return appointments.some((a) => {
      if (a.clinicId !== clinicId || a.doctorId !== doctorId || a.date !== date || a.status === 'cancelled') {
        return false;
      }
      const [ah, am] = toEnglishDigits(a.time).trim().split(':').map(Number);
      const aStart = ah * 60 + am;
      const aEnd = aStart + (a.durationMinutes || 30);
      return targetStart < aEnd && targetEnd > aStart;
    });
  }

  async updateMedicalNotes(
    clinicId: string,
    id: string,
    notes: string,
    actor?: Actor
  ): Promise<void> {
    await simulateDelay(40);
    if (actor && !can(actor.role, 'medical_notes:write')) {
      throw new Error('شما دسترسی لازم برای ویرایش یادداشت پزشکی را ندارید.');
    }

    const appointments = mockStorage.getAppointments();
    const apt = appointments.find((a) => a.clinicId === clinicId && a.id === id);
    if (!apt) {
      throw new Error('نوبت مورد نظر یافت نشد.');
    }

    // Ownership check: doctor can only update notes for their own appointments
    if (actor?.role === 'doctor') {
      if (!actor.doctorId || apt.doctorId !== actor.doctorId) {
        throw new Error('دسترسی غیرمجاز: پزشک تنها مجاز به ثبت یادداشت در نوبت‌های خود می‌باشد.');
      }
    }

    mockStorage.saveMedicalNote(id, notes);

    if (actor) {
      void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
        clinicId,
        userId: actor.userId,
        userName: actor.userName || actor.userId || actor.role,
        role: actor.role,
        action: 'medical_notes.update',
        details: `ثبت یا ویرایش یادداشت پزشکی برای نوبت ${apt.trackingCode || id}`,
      });
    }
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockAuditRepository.ts ===================
import type { IAuditRepository } from '../../repositories/IAuditRepository';
import type { AuditLogEntry, AuditAction } from '../../../domain/audit';
import { AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS } from '../../../domain/audit';
import type { Role } from '../../../domain/session';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';

export class MockAuditRepository implements IAuditRepository {
  async getLogs(clinicId: string): Promise<AuditLogEntry[]> {
    await simulateDelay(60);
    const logs = mockStorage.getAuditLogs().filter((l) => l.clinicId === clinicId);
    return logs.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt).getTime());
  }

  async recordLog(params: {
    clinicId: string;
    userId: string;
    userName: string;
    role: Role;
    action: AuditAction;
    details?: string;
  }): Promise<void> {
    const entry: AuditLogEntry = {
      id: `audit-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`,
      clinicId: params.clinicId,
      userId: params.userId,
      userName: params.userName,
      role: params.role,
      action: params.action,
      actionLabel: AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS[params.action] || params.action,
      details: params.details,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const current = mockStorage.getAuditLogs();
    mockStorage.saveAuditLogs([entry, ...current]);
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockAuthRepository.ts ===================
import type {
  IAuthRepository,
  RequestOtpResult,
  VerifyOtpResult,
  VerifyPhoneOwnershipResult,
  CreateUserInput,
} from '../../repositories/IAuthRepository';
import type { UserProfile, UserSession, Role, Actor } from '../../../domain/session';
import { normalizePhoneNumber, isValidIranianMobile } from '../../../lib/phone';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../../../mocks/seed';
import { MockAuditRepository } from './MockAuditRepository';

const SESSION_STORAGE_KEY = 'medflow_auth_session';
const FIXED_MOCK_OTP = '123456';
const OTP_EXPIRY_SECONDS = 120; // 2 minutes
const LOCKOUT_MINUTES = 5;
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;

export class MockAuthRepository implements IAuthRepository {
  private auditRepo = new MockAuditRepository();

  async requestOtp(phone: string): Promise<RequestOtpResult> {
    await simulateDelay(60);
    const normalized = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);

    if (!isValidIranianMobile(normalized)) {
      throw new Error('شماره تلفن همراه معتبر نمی‌باشد (الگوی ۰۹۱۲...).');
    }

    const state = mockStorage.getOtpState(normalized);
    const now = Date.now();

    // Check lockout
    if (state?.lockedUntil && state.lockedUntil > now) {
      const remainingMinutes = Math.ceil((state.lockedUntil - now) / (60 * 1000));
      throw new Error(
        `تعداد تلاش‌های ناموفق بیش از حد مجاز بوده است. لطفاً ${remainingMinutes} دقیقه دیگر مجدداً تلاش فرمایید.`
      );
    }

    // Rate limit check: max 3 requests in 10 minutes
    const recentRequests = mockStorage.getOtpRequestCount(normalized, 10);
    if (recentRequests >= 3) {
      throw new Error(
        'تعداد درخواست‌های ارسال کد بیش از حد مجاز است (حداکثر ۳ بار در ۱۰ دقیقه). لطفاً بعداً تلاش فرمایید.'
      );
    }
    mockStorage.recordOtpRequest(normalized);

    // Check if user exists in clinic registry or is a patient
    const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
    const user = users.find((u) => normalizePhoneNumber(u.phone) === normalized);

    if (user && !user.isActive) {
      throw new Error('حساب کاربری شما توسط مدیر سیستم غیرفعال شده است.');
    }

    // Retain failed attempts count within window:
    const retainedAttempts =
      state?.lockedUntil && now > state.lockedUntil ? 0 : state?.attempts || 0;

    // Save new OTP state
    mockStorage.saveOtpState(normalized, {
      code: FIXED_MOCK_OTP,
      sentAt: now,
      expiresAt: now + OTP_EXPIRY_SECONDS * 1000,
      attempts: retainedAttempts,
      lockedUntil: state?.lockedUntil && state.lockedUntil > now ? state.lockedUntil : undefined,
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'کد تأیید ۶ رقمی از طریق پیامک ارسال شد.',
      expiresInSeconds: OTP_EXPIRY_SECONDS,
    };
  }

  async verifyPhoneOwnership(phone: string, code: string): Promise<VerifyPhoneOwnershipResult> {
    await simulateDelay(80);
    const normalized = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const state = mockStorage.getOtpState(normalized);
    const now = Date.now();

    if (!state) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'کد تأیید منقضی شده یا درخواست نشده است. لطفاً مجدداً درخواست ارسال کد دهید.',
      };
    }

    // Check lockout
    if (state.lockedUntil && state.lockedUntil > now) {
      const remainingMinutes = Math.ceil((state.lockedUntil - now) / (60 * 1000));
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `به دلیل تلاش‌های ناموفق مکرر، امکان اعتبارسنجی تا ${remainingMinutes} دقیقه دیگر وجود ندارد.`,
      };
    }

    // Check expiry
    if (now > state.expiresAt) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'کد تأیید منقضی شده است. لطفاً کد جدید دریافت کنید.',
      };
    }

    // Check code match
    const cleanCode = code.trim();
    if (cleanCode !== state.code) {
      const nextAttempts = state.attempts + 1;
      const willLock = nextAttempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS;
      const lockedUntil = willLock ? now + LOCKOUT_MINUTES * 60 * 1000 : undefined;

      mockStorage.saveOtpState(normalized, {
        ...state,
        attempts: nextAttempts,
        lockedUntil,
      });

      if (willLock) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: `۳ بار کد اشتباه وارد شد. به دلایل امنیتی تا ${LOCKOUT_MINUTES} دقیقه امکان اعتبارسنجی وجود ندارد.`,
        };
      }

      const remainingAttempts = MAX_ATTEMPTS - nextAttempts;
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `کد وارد شده صحیح نمی‌باشد. (${remainingAttempts} بار فرصت باقی‌مانده)`,
      };
    }

    // Success - consume OTP state and issue single-use phone verification token
    mockStorage.clearOtpState(normalized);
    const verificationToken = `vtok_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10)}_${Date.now()}`;
    mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(normalized, verificationToken, now + 15 * 60 * 1000);

    return {
      success: true,
      verificationToken,
    };
  }

  async verifyOtp(phone: string, code: string): Promise<VerifyOtpResult> {
    await simulateDelay(80);
    const normalized = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const state = mockStorage.getOtpState(normalized);
    const now = Date.now();

    if (!state) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'کد تأیید منقضی شده یا درخواست نشده است. لطفاً مجدداً درخواست ارسال کد دهید.',
      };
    }

    // Check lockout
    if (state.lockedUntil && state.lockedUntil > now) {
      const remainingMinutes = Math.ceil((state.lockedUntil - now) / (60 * 1000));
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `حساب شما به مدت ${remainingMinutes} دقیقه به دلیل تلاش‌های ناموفق قفل شده است.`,
      };
    }

    // Check expiry
    if (now > state.expiresAt) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'کد تأیید منقضی شده است. لطفاً کد جدید دریافت کنید.',
      };
    }

    // Check code match
    const cleanCode = code.trim();
    if (cleanCode !== state.code) {
      const nextAttempts = state.attempts + 1;
      const willLock = nextAttempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS;
      const lockedUntil = willLock ? now + LOCKOUT_MINUTES * 60 * 1000 : undefined;

      mockStorage.saveOtpState(normalized, {
        ...state,
        attempts: nextAttempts,
        lockedUntil,
      });

      // Record failed audit log
      void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        userId: 'unknown',
        userName: normalized,
        role: 'patient',
        action: 'auth.login_failed',
        details: `کد اشتباه برای شماره ${normalized}. تلاش شماره ${nextAttempts} از ${MAX_ATTEMPTS}`,
      });

      if (willLock) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: `۳ بار کد اشتباه وارد شد. به دلایل امنیتی تا ${LOCKOUT_MINUTES} دقیقه امکان ورود وجود ندارد.`,
        };
      }

      const remainingAttempts = MAX_ATTEMPTS - nextAttempts;
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `کد وارد شده صحیح نمی‌باشد. (${remainingAttempts} بار فرصت باقی‌مانده)`,
      };
    }

    // Success - resolve or auto-provision patient user
    mockStorage.clearOtpState(normalized);

    const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
    let user = users.find((u) => normalizePhoneNumber(u.phone) === normalized);

    if (!user) {
      // Auto-create patient profile
      const newPatient: UserProfile = {
        id: `usr-p-${Date.now()}`,
        name: 'مراجع گرامی',
        role: 'patient',
        roleTitle: 'مراجع کلینیک',
        avatarInitials: 'م',
        phone: normalized,
        clientId: `cl-${Date.now()}`,
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        isActive: true,
        createdAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fa-IR'),
      };
      user = newPatient;
      mockStorage.saveUsers([...users, newPatient]);
    }

    if (!user.isActive) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'حساب کاربری شما مسدود می‌باشد.',
      };
    }

    const session: UserSession = {
      user,
      activeClinic: SEED_CLINIC,
      availableClinics: [SEED_CLINIC],
      isAuthenticated: true,
      token: `mock-jwt-token-${user.id}-${Date.now()}`,
    };

    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(session));
    }

    // Record login audit log
    void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
      clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
      userId: user.id,
      userName: user.name,
      role: user.role,
      action: 'auth.login_success',
      details: `ورود با شماره ${normalized} و نقش ${user.roleTitle}`,
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      session,
    };
  }

  async getCurrentUser(): Promise<UserSession | null> {
    await simulateDelay(30);
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;

    try {
      const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (!stored) return null;
      const parsed: UserSession = JSON.parse(stored);

      // Re-verify against users storage in case user was deactivated or role changed
      const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
      const freshUser = users.find((u) => u.id === parsed.user.id);
      if (!freshUser || !freshUser.isActive) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
        return null;
      }

      parsed.user = freshUser;
      return parsed;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async logout(): Promise<void> {
    await simulateDelay(30);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (stored) {
        try {
          const parsed: UserSession = JSON.parse(stored);
          void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
            clinicId: parsed.activeClinic.id,
            userId: parsed.user.id,
            userName: parsed.user.name,
            role: parsed.user.role,
            action: 'auth.logout',
            details: 'خروج کاربر از سیستم',
          });
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }
      }
      sessionStorage.removeItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
    }
  }

  async refreshSession(): Promise<UserSession | null> {
    return this.getCurrentUser();
  }

  async updateProfile(userId: string, input: { name: string }): Promise<UserProfile> {
    await simulateDelay(50);
    const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
    const index = users.findIndex((u) => u.id === userId);
    if (index === -1) {
      throw new Error('کاربر مورد نظر یافت نشد.');
    }

    const current = users[index];
    const newName = input.name.trim();
    const updated: UserProfile = {
      ...current,
      name: newName,
      avatarInitials: newName.charAt(0) || current.avatarInitials,
    };

    users[index] = updated;
    mockStorage.saveUsers(users);

    // Update in sessionStorage if current user
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (stored) {
          const parsed: UserSession = JSON.parse(stored);
          if (parsed.user.id === userId) {
            parsed.user = updated;
            sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(parsed));
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }

    return updated;
  }

  // --- User Management ---
  async getUsers(clinicId: string): Promise<UserProfile[]> {
    await simulateDelay(50);
    return mockStorage.getUsers().filter((u) => !u.clinicId || u.clinicId === clinicId);
  }

  async createUser(clinicId: string, input: CreateUserInput, actor?: Actor): Promise<UserProfile> {
    await simulateDelay(60);
    const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
    const normalized = normalizePhoneNumber(input.phone);

    if (actor) {
      if (actor.role === 'admin' && (input.role === 'owner' || input.role === 'admin')) {
        throw new Error('مدیر عملیات مجاز به ایجاد کاربر با نقش مدیر ارشد یا مدیر عملیات نمی‌باشد.');
      }
    }

    if (input.role === 'doctor' && !input.doctorId) {
      throw new Error('انتخاب پزشک معالج برای نقش پزشک متخصص الزامی است.');
    }

    if (users.some((u) => normalizePhoneNumber(u.phone) === normalized)) {
      throw new Error('کاربری با این شماره تلفن همراه قبلاً ثبت شده است.');
    }

    const initials = input.name.trim().charAt(0) || 'ک';
    const newUser: UserProfile = {
      id: `usr-${Date.now()}`,
      name: input.name.trim(),
      role: input.role,
      roleTitle: input.roleTitle,
      avatarInitials: initials,
      email: input.email,
      phone: normalized,
      doctorId: input.doctorId,
      clinicId,
      isActive: true,
      createdAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fa-IR'),
    };

    mockStorage.saveUsers([newUser, ...users]);

    void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
      clinicId,
      userId: actor?.userId || newUser.id,
      userName: actor?.userName || actor?.userId || newUser.name,
      role: actor?.role || newUser.role,
      action: 'user.created',
      details: `ایجاد کاربر ${newUser.name} با نقش ${newUser.roleTitle}`,
    });

    return newUser;
  }

  async updateUserRole(
    clinicId: string,
    userId: string,
    newRole: Role,
    actor?: Actor,
    doctorId?: string
  ): Promise<UserProfile> {
    await simulateDelay(50);
    const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
    const index = users.findIndex((u) => u.id === userId);
    if (index === -1) {
      throw new Error('کاربر مورد نظر یافت نشد.');
    }

    const current = users[index];

    if (actor) {
      if (actor.userId === userId) {
        throw new Error('امکان تغییر سطح دسترسی حساب کاربری خودتان وجود ندارد.');
      }
      if (actor.role === 'admin') {
        if (current.role === 'owner' || current.role === 'admin') {
          throw new Error('مدیر عملیات مجاز به ویرایش نقش مدیر ارشد یا سایر مدیران نمی‌باشد.');
        }
        if (newRole === 'owner' || newRole === 'admin') {
          throw new Error('مدیر عملیات مجاز به ارتقای کاربران به نقش مدیر ارشد یا مدیر عملیات نمی‌باشد.');
        }
      }
      if (current.role === 'owner' && newRole !== 'owner') {
        const activeOwners = users.filter((u) => u.role === 'owner' && u.isActive && u.id !== userId);
        if (activeOwners.length === 0) {
          throw new Error('امکان تنزل نقش آخرین مدیر ارشد (Owner) کلینیک وجود ندارد.');
        }
      }
    }

    const effectiveDoctorId = doctorId || current.doctorId;
    if (newRole === 'doctor' && !effectiveDoctorId) {
      throw new Error('تخصیص شناسه پزشک معالج برای نقش پزشک الزامی است.');
    }

    const updated: UserProfile = {
      ...current,
      role: newRole,
      doctorId: newRole === 'doctor' ? effectiveDoctorId : current.doctorId,
    };

    users[index] = updated;
    mockStorage.saveUsers([...users]);

    void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
      clinicId,
      userId: actor?.userId || current.id,
      userName: actor?.userName || actor?.userId || current.name,
      role: actor?.role || newRole,
      action: 'user.role_change',
      details: `تغییر نقش کاربر ${current.name} از ${current.role} به ${newRole}`,
    });

    return updated;
  }

  async toggleUserStatus(clinicId: string, userId: string, actor?: Actor): Promise<UserProfile> {
    await simulateDelay(50);
    const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
    const index = users.findIndex((u) => u.id === userId);
    if (index === -1) {
      throw new Error('کاربر مورد نظر یافت نشد.');
    }

    const current = users[index];

    if (actor) {
      if (actor.userId === userId) {
        throw new Error('امکان غیرفعال‌سازی حساب کاربری خودتان وجود ندارد.');
      }
      if (actor.role === 'admin' && (current.role === 'owner' || current.role === 'admin')) {
        throw new Error('مدیر عملیات مجاز به تغییر وضعیت حساب مدیر ارشد یا سایر مدیران نمی‌باشد.');
      }
      if (current.role === 'owner' && current.isActive) {
        const activeOwners = users.filter((u) => u.role === 'owner' && u.isActive && u.id !== userId);
        if (activeOwners.length === 0) {
          throw new Error('امکان غیرفعال‌سازی آخرین مدیر ارشد (Owner) کلینیک وجود ندارد.');
        }
      }
    }

    const updated: UserProfile = {
      ...current,
      isActive: !current.isActive,
    };

    users[index] = updated;
    mockStorage.saveUsers([...users]);

    void this.auditRepo.recordLog({
      clinicId,
      userId: actor?.userId || current.id,
      userName: actor?.userName || actor?.userId || current.name,
      role: actor?.role || current.role,
      action: updated.isActive ? 'user.activated' : 'user.deactivated',
      details: `وضعیت کاربر ${current.name} به ${updated.isActive ? 'فعال' : 'غیرفعال'} تغییر یافت.`,
    });

    return updated;
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockAutomationRepository.ts ===================
import type { IAutomationRepository } from '../../repositories/IAutomationRepository';
import type { Automation, AutomationEvent, AutomationStats, OutboxMessage } from '../../../domain/automation';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';
import { integrationRegistry } from '../../../integrations/registry';

export class MockAutomationRepository implements IAutomationRepository {
  async getAutomations(clinicId: string): Promise<Automation[]> {
    await simulateDelay();
    const isSmsConnected = integrationRegistry.isConnected('sms');
    const isWhatsappConnected = integrationRegistry.isConnected('whatsapp');

    return mockStorage
      .getAutomations()
      .filter((a) => a.clinicId === clinicId)
      .map((a) => {
        const requiresSms = a.channel.includes('پیامک');
        const requiresWhatsapp = a.channel.includes('واتساپ');

        let healthy = true;
        if (requiresSms && !isSmsConnected) {
          healthy = false;
        }
        if (requiresWhatsapp && !isWhatsappConnected && !requiresSms) {
          healthy = false;
        }

        return {
          ...a,
          healthy,
        };
      });
  }

  async getStats(clinicId: string): Promise<AutomationStats> {
    await simulateDelay(30);
    const automations = mockStorage.getAutomations().filter((a) => a.clinicId === clinicId);
    const totalActions = automations.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.totalTriggered || 0), 0);
    const hoursSaved = Math.round((totalActions * 4) / 60);

    return {
      totalActions,
      hoursSaved,
    };
  }

  async setEnabled(clinicId: string, id: string, enabled: boolean): Promise<void> {
    await simulateDelay(20);
    const automations = mockStorage.getAutomations();
    const item = automations.find((a) => a.clinicId === clinicId && a.id === id);
    if (item) {
      item.enabled = enabled;
      mockStorage.saveAutomations([...automations]);
    }
  }

  async triggerEvent(
    clinicId: string,
    event: AutomationEvent,
    payload: Record<string, unknown>
  ): Promise<void> {
    await simulateDelay(50);
    const automations = await this.getAutomations(clinicId);
    const matchingRule = automations.find(
      (a) => a.clinicId === clinicId && a.event === event && a.enabled && a.healthy
    );

    if (!matchingRule) return;

    // Compose personalized message
    let content = matchingRule.messageTemplate;
    Object.entries(payload).forEach(([k, v]) => {
      content = content.replace(new RegExp(`{${k}}`, 'g'), String(v));
    });

    const recipientPhone = String(payload.phone || payload.clientPhone || '۰۹۱۲۰۰۰۰۰۰۰');
    const recipientName = String(payload.name || payload.clientName || 'مراجع گرامی');

    // Route through providers
    const channel = matchingRule.channel.includes('واتساپ') ? 'whatsapp' : 'sms';
    let messageStatus: 'sent' | 'pending' | 'failed' = 'sent';
    let scheduledFor: string | undefined = undefined;

    // 24-Hour Reminder Scheduling
    const appointmentDateTime = (payload.appointmentDateTime || payload.dateTime) as string | undefined;
    if (appointmentDateTime) {
      const aptTime = new Date(appointmentDateTime).getTime();
      const diffMs = aptTime - Date.now();
      if (diffMs > 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
        messageStatus = 'pending';
        scheduledFor = new Date(aptTime - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
      }
    }

    if (messageStatus !== 'pending') {
      if (channel === 'whatsapp') {
        await integrationRegistry.whatsappProvider.send(recipientPhone, content);
      } else {
        const isSmsConnected = integrationRegistry.isConnected('sms');
        if (!isSmsConnected) {
          messageStatus = 'failed';
        }
        await integrationRegistry.smsProvider.send(recipientPhone, content);
      }
    }

    // Save to simulated outbox
    const outboxMessage: OutboxMessage = {
      id: `outbox-${Date.now()}`,
      clinicId,
      recipientPhone,
      recipientName,
      channel,
      content,
      status: messageStatus,
      triggerEvent: event,
      scheduledFor,
      createdAt: 'هم‌اکنون',
    };

    const currentOutbox = mockStorage.getOutbox();
    mockStorage.saveOutbox([outboxMessage, ...currentOutbox]);

    // Increment automation counter
    const allAutomations = mockStorage.getAutomations();
    const storedRule = allAutomations.find((a) => a.clinicId === clinicId && a.id === matchingRule.id);
    if (storedRule) {
      storedRule.totalTriggered += 1;
      mockStorage.saveAutomations([...allAutomations]);
    }
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockBookingRepository.ts ===================
import type {
  IBookingRepository,
  BookingSubmissionInput,
} from '../../repositories/IBookingRepository';
import type { TimeSlot } from '../../../domain/slot';
import type { Appointment } from '../../../domain/appointment';
import { calculateAvailableSlots } from '../../../domain/slot';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';
import { toEnglishDigits } from '../../../lib/format';
import { normalizePhoneNumber } from '../../../lib/phone';
import { MockAutomationRepository } from './MockAutomationRepository';

export class MockBookingRepository implements IBookingRepository {
  private automationRepo = new MockAutomationRepository();

  async getAvailableSlots(params: {
    clinicId: string;
    doctorId: string;
    serviceId: string;
    date: string;
  }): Promise<TimeSlot[]> {
    await simulateDelay(60);
    const { doctorId, serviceId, date, clinicId } = params;

    const doctor = mockStorage.getDoctors().find((d) => d.clinicId === clinicId && d.id === doctorId);
    const service = mockStorage.getServices().find((s) => s.clinicId === clinicId && s.id === serviceId);

    if (!doctor || !service) {
      return [];
    }

    const appointments = mockStorage.getAppointments().filter((a) => a.clinicId === clinicId);

    return calculateAvailableSlots({
      doctor,
      serviceDurationMinutes: service.duration,
      existingAppointments: appointments,
      date,
    });
  }

  async submitBooking(input: BookingSubmissionInput): Promise<{
    appointment: Appointment;
    trackingCode: string;
  }> {
    await simulateDelay(100);
    const {
      clinicId,
      serviceId,
      doctorId,
      date,
      time,
      clientName,
      clientPhone,
      notes,
      medicalNotes,
      verificationToken,
      captchaToken,
    } = input;

    const normalizedPhone = normalizePhoneNumber(clientPhone);

    // 1. Enforce atomic OTP phone verification token lock to protect against race condition / double-submit
    if (!verificationToken || !mockStorage.lockToken(normalizedPhone, verificationToken)) {
      throw new Error(
        'اعتبارسنجی شماره تلفن همراه نامعتبر است، منقضی شده، یا در حال پردازش می‌باشد. لطفاً مجدداً بررسی فرمایید.'
      );
    }

    try {
      // 2. Validate captcha
      if (!captchaToken || !captchaToken.trim()) {
        throw new Error('کپچا الزامی است یا اعتبارسنجی آن ناموفق بود.');
      }

      // 3. Validate service and doctor
      const service = mockStorage.getServices().find((s) => s.clinicId === clinicId && s.id === serviceId);
      const doctor = mockStorage.getDoctors().find((d) => d.clinicId === clinicId && d.id === doctorId);

      if (!service || !doctor) {
        throw new Error('خدمت یا پزشک انتخاب‌شده در سیستم معتبر نیست.');
      }

      // 4. Check rate limit (max 3 bookings per hour per normalized phone)
      const recentCount = mockStorage.getRecentBookingCount(normalizedPhone, 60);
      if (recentCount >= 3) {
        throw new Error(
          'تعداد درخواست‌های رزرو از این شماره بیش از حد مجاز در ساعت است (حداکثر ۳ نوبت). لطفاً بعداً تلاش فرمایید.'
        );
      }

      // 5. Double check conflict before committing
      const appointments = mockStorage.getAppointments();
      const canonicalTime = toEnglishDigits(time).trim();
      const [h, m] = canonicalTime.split(':').map(Number);
      const targetStart = h * 60 + m;
      const targetEnd = targetStart + service.duration;

      const conflict = appointments.some((a) => {
        if (a.clinicId !== clinicId || a.doctorId !== doctorId || a.date !== date || a.status === 'cancelled') {
          return false;
        }
        const [ah, am] = toEnglishDigits(a.time).trim().split(':').map(Number);
        const aStart = ah * 60 + am;
        const aEnd = aStart + (a.durationMinutes || 30);
        return targetStart < aEnd && targetEnd > aStart;
      });

      if (conflict) {
        throw new Error('متأسفانه در حین تکمیل فرم، این ساعت توسط کاربر دیگری رزرو شد. لطفاً ساعت دیگری را انتخاب فرمایید.');
      }

    // 6. Generate dynamic canonical tracking code (e.g. "AR-8492")
    const randomCode = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
    const trackingCode = `AR-${randomCode}`;
    const appointmentId = `apt-online-${Date.now()}`;

    // 7. Upsert client based on phone AND name
    const allClients = mockStorage.getClients();
    const normalizedInputName = clientName.trim();

    let client = allClients.find(
      (c) =>
        c.clinicId === clinicId &&
        normalizePhoneNumber(c.phone) === normalizedPhone &&
        c.name.trim() === normalizedInputName
    );

    const familyPrimaryClient = allClients.find(
      (c) => c.clinicId === clinicId && normalizePhoneNumber(c.phone) === normalizedPhone
    );

    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    if (!client) {
      const newClient = {
        id: `client-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`,
        clinicId,
        name: normalizedInputName,
        phone: normalizedPhone,
        totalVisits: 1,
        totalSpent: service.price,
        lastVisitDate: date,
        status: 'new' as const,
        parentClientId: familyPrimaryClient ? (familyPrimaryClient.parentClientId || familyPrimaryClient.id) : undefined,
        createdAt: now,
      };
      client = newClient;
      mockStorage.saveClients([newClient, ...allClients]);
    } else {
      client.totalVisits = (client.totalVisits || 0) + 1;
      client.totalSpent = (client.totalSpent || 0) + service.price;
      client.lastVisitDate = date;
      mockStorage.saveClients([...allClients]);
    }

    // Store medicalNotes in isolated storage
    if (medicalNotes) {
      mockStorage.saveMedicalNote(appointmentId, medicalNotes);
    }

    const newAppointment: Appointment = {
      id: appointmentId,
      clinicId,
      clientId: client.id,
      trackingCode,
      date,
      time: canonicalTime,
      clientName,
      clientPhone: normalizedPhone,
      serviceId,
      serviceName: service.name,
      doctorId,
      doctorName: doctor.name,
      durationMinutes: service.duration,
      price: service.price,
      status: 'pending_confirmation',
      source: 'online_booking',
      notes,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    };

    mockStorage.saveAppointments([newAppointment, ...appointments]);

    // 8. Consume token and record booking attempt ONLY after success
    mockStorage.consumeToken(verificationToken);
    mockStorage.recordBookingAttempt(normalizedPhone);

    // Emit domain event to automation engine
    await this.automationRepo.triggerEvent(clinicId, 'appointment.created', {
      name: clientName,
      clientName,
      phone: normalizedPhone,
      service: service.name,
      time: newAppointment.time,
    });

      return {
        appointment: newAppointment,
        trackingCode,
      };
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      // Re-issue / restore token if booking failed so user does not have to re-verify phone
      mockStorage.releaseToken(verificationToken);
      throw err;
    }
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockCatalogRepository.ts ===================
import type { ICatalogRepository } from '../../repositories/ICatalogRepository';
import type { Service } from '../../../domain/service';
import type { Doctor } from '../../../domain/doctor';
import type { ClinicWorkspace } from '../../../domain/session';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../../../mocks/seed';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';

export class MockCatalogRepository implements ICatalogRepository {
  async getServices(clinicId: string): Promise<Service[]> {
    await simulateDelay(50);
    return mockStorage.getServices().filter((s) => s.clinicId === clinicId && s.isActive);
  }

  async getDoctors(clinicId: string): Promise<Doctor[]> {
    await simulateDelay(50);
    return mockStorage.getDoctors().filter((d) => d.clinicId === clinicId && d.isActive);
  }

  async getServiceById(clinicId: string, id: string): Promise<Service | null> {
    await simulateDelay(30);
    const item = mockStorage
      .getServices()
      .find((s) => s.clinicId === clinicId && s.id === id);
    return item || null;
  }

  async getDoctorById(clinicId: string, id: string): Promise<Doctor | null> {
    await simulateDelay(30);
    const item = mockStorage
      .getDoctors()
      .find((d) => d.clinicId === clinicId && d.id === id);
    return item || null;
  }

  async getClinicBySlug(slug: string): Promise<ClinicWorkspace | null> {
    await simulateDelay(30);
    if (slug === SEED_CLINIC.slug || slug === 'ariana') {
      return SEED_CLINIC;
    }
    return null;
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockClientRepository.ts ===================
import type { IClientRepository } from '../../repositories/IClientRepository';
import type { Client } from '../../../domain/client';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';
import { toEnglishDigits } from '../../../lib/format';

export class MockClientRepository implements IClientRepository {
  async getClients(clinicId: string, search?: string): Promise<Client[]> {
    await simulateDelay();
    let clients = mockStorage.getClients().filter((c) => c.clinicId === clinicId);

    if (search) {
      const qOriginal = search.trim().toLowerCase();
      const qNorm = toEnglishDigits(search).trim().toLowerCase();
      clients = clients.filter(
        (c) =>
          c.name.toLowerCase().includes(qOriginal) ||
          c.phone.includes(qNorm) ||
          toEnglishDigits(c.phone).includes(qNorm)
      );
    }
    return clients;
  }

  async getAtRiskClients(clinicId: string): Promise<Client[]> {
    await simulateDelay();
    return mockStorage
      .getClients()
      .filter((c) => c.clinicId === clinicId && c.status === 'at_risk');
  }

  async getClientById(clinicId: string, id: string): Promise<Client | null> {
    await simulateDelay();
    const item = mockStorage
      .getClients()
      .find((c) => c.clinicId === clinicId && c.id === id);
    return item || null;
  }
}

=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/MockDashboardRepository.ts ===================
import type { IDashboardRepository } from '../../repositories/IDashboardRepository';
import type { DashboardSummary, DashboardNextAction } from '../../../domain/dashboard';
import { mockStorage, simulateDelay } from './mockStorage';
import { getTodayDateString } from '../../../lib/jalali';
import { toPersianDigits } from '../../../lib/format';

export class MockDashboardRepository implements IDashboardRepository {
  async getSummary(clinicId: string): Promise<DashboardSummary> {
    await simulateDelay();
    const today = getTodayDateString();

    const appointments = mockStorage
      .getAppointments()
      .filter((a) => a.clinicId === clinicId && a.date === today);

    const leads = mockStorage
      .getLeads()
      .filter((l) => l.clinicId === clinicId && l.status === 'hot');

    const decisionReady = leads.filter((l) => l.isDecisionReady);

    const atRiskClients = mockStorage
      .getClients()
      .filter((c) => c.clinicId === clinicId && c.status === 'at_risk');

    const totalRecoveryValue = atRiskClients.reduce(
      (sum, c) => sum + (c.recoveryValue || 0),
      0
    );

    const recoveryValueInMillion = (totalRecoveryValue / 1000000).toFixed(1);

    const todayCount = appointments.length; // 24 from seed
    const hotCount = leads.length; // 7 from seed
    const decisionCount = decisionReady.length; // 3 from seed
    const atRiskCount = atRiskClients.length; // 3 from seed

    return {
      todayAppointmentsCount: todayCount,
      todayDelta: '↑ ۴ نسبت به دیروز',
      hotLeadsCount: hotCount,
      decisionReadyCount: decisionCount,
      atRiskClientsCount: atRiskCount,
      recoveryValueText: `${toPersianDigits(recoveryValueInMillion)} میلیون ارزش بازیابی`,
      responseRateText: '۹۲٪',
      responseRateDelta: '↑ ۸٪ این هفته',
      metrics: [
        {
          id: 'today',
          label: 'نوبت امروز',
          value: toPersianDigits(todayCount),
          delta: '↑ ۴ نسبت به دیروز',
          iconName: 'calendar',
        },
        {
          id: 'hot',
          label: 'سرنخ داغ',
          value: toPersianDigits(hotCount),
          hint: `${toPersianDigits(decisionCount)} نفر آماده تصمیمگیری`,
          iconName: 'flame',
        },
        {
          id: 'at_risk',
          label: 'مشتری در خطر',
          value: toPersianDigits(atRiskCount),
          hint: `${toPersianDigits(recoveryValueInMillion)} میلیون ارزش بازیابی`,
          iconName: 'alert-triangle',
        },
        {
          id: 'response',
          label: 'نرخ پاسخگویی',
          value: '۹۲٪',
          delta: '↑ ۸٪ این هفته',
          iconName: 'message-circle',
        },
      ],
    };
  }

  async getNextActions(_clinicId: string): Promise<DashboardNextAction[]> {
    await simulateDelay();
    return mockStorage.getNextActions();
  }
}
