=================== ./src/services/adapters/mock/mockStorage.ts ===================
import {
  SEED_APPOINTMENTS,
  SEED_CLIENTS,
  SEED_LEADS,
  SEED_SERVICES,
  SEED_DOCTORS,
  SEED_AUTOMATIONS,
  SEED_NEXT_ACTIONS,
  SEED_CLINIC,
  SEED_USERS,
  buildSeed,
} from '../../../mocks/seed';
import type { Appointment } from '../../../domain/appointment';
import type { Client } from '../../../domain/client';
import type { Lead } from '../../../domain/lead';
import type { Service } from '../../../domain/service';
import type { Doctor } from '../../../domain/doctor';
import type { Automation, OutboxMessage } from '../../../domain/automation';
import type { DashboardNextAction } from '../../../domain/dashboard';
import type { UserProfile } from '../../../domain/session';
import type { AuditLogEntry } from '../../../domain/audit';

import { normalizePhoneNumber } from '../../../lib/phone';
import { getTodayDateString } from '../../../lib/jalali';

const STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX = 'medflow_mock_';

export interface OtpSessionState {
  code: string;
  sentAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
  attempts: number;
  lockedUntil?: number;
}

export interface PhoneVerificationToken {
  token: string;
  phone: string;
  expiresAt: number;
  status?: 'active' | 'pending_consumption' | 'consumed';
  lockedAt?: number;
}

class MockStorage {
  private get<T>(key: string, defaultValue: T): T {
    try {
      if (typeof window === 'undefined') return defaultValue;
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${key}`);
      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : defaultValue;
    } catch {
      return defaultValue;
    }
  }

  private set<T>(key: string, value: T): void {
    try {
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem(`${STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${key}`, JSON.stringify(value));
      }
    } catch {
      // Storage full or unavailable
    }
  }

  // --- Users ---
  public getUsers(): UserProfile[] {
    return this.get<UserProfile[]>('users', SEED_USERS);
  }

  public saveUsers(items: UserProfile[]): void {
    this.set('users', items);
  }

  // --- Audit Logs ---
  public getAuditLogs(): AuditLogEntry[] {
    const initialLogs: AuditLogEntry[] = [
      {
        id: 'log-init-1',
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        userId: 'usr-sara',
        userName: 'سارا حسینی',
        role: 'reception',
        action: 'auth.login_success',
        actionLabel: 'ورود موفق به سامانه',
        details: 'ورود موفق از طریق پیامک OTP',
        createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 3600000).toISOString(),
      },
    ];
    return this.get<AuditLogEntry[]>('audit_logs', initialLogs);
  }

  public saveAuditLogs(items: AuditLogEntry[]): void {
    this.set('audit_logs', items);
  }

  // --- Appointments ---
  public getAppointments(): Appointment[] {
    const today = getTodayDateString();
    const storedSeedDate = this.get<string | null>('seed_date', null);

    if (storedSeedDate !== today) {
      const currentStored = this.get<Appointment[]>('appointments', []);
      const userAppointments = currentStored.filter(
        (a) => !/^apt-(\d+|tom-\d+|past-\d+)$/.test(a.id)
      );
      const freshSeed = buildSeed(today);
      const combined = [...freshSeed.appointments, ...userAppointments];
      this.saveAppointments(combined);
      this.set('seed_date', today);
      return combined;
    }

    return this.get<Appointment[]>('appointments', SEED_APPOINTMENTS);
  }

  public saveAppointments(items: Appointment[]): void {
    // Medical notes separation: strip medicalNotes from regular appointment array
    // and store in a dedicated isolated store to prevent unauthorized exposure.
    const notesStore = this.getAllMedicalNotes();
    const sanitized = items.map((apt) => {
      if (apt.medicalNotes) {
        notesStore[apt.id] = apt.medicalNotes;
      }
      const clone = { ...apt };
      delete clone.medicalNotes;
      return clone;
    });

    this.set('appointments', sanitized);
    this.set('medical_notes_store', notesStore);
  }

  // --- Medical Notes Storage (Isolated & Privacy Protected) ---
  public getAllMedicalNotes(): Record<string, string> {
    const defaultNotes: Record<string, string> = {
      'apt-1': 'سابقه حساسیت به داروی لیدوکائین. نیاز به تست پوستی قبل از تزریق.',
      'apt-2': 'سابقه مصرف راکوتان تا ۳ ماه پیش.',
    };
    return this.get<Record<string, string>>('medical_notes_store', defaultNotes);
  }

  public getMedicalNote(appointmentId: string): string | undefined {
    const store = this.getAllMedicalNotes();
    return store[appointmentId];
  }

  public saveMedicalNote(appointmentId: string, note: string): void {
    const store = this.getAllMedicalNotes();
    store[appointmentId] = note;
    this.set('medical_notes_store', store);
  }

  // --- Clients ---
  public getClients(): Client[] {
    return this.get<Client[]>('clients', SEED_CLIENTS);
  }

  public saveClients(items: Client[]): void {
    this.set('clients', items);
  }

  // --- Leads ---
  public getLeads(): Lead[] {
    return this.get<Lead[]>('leads', SEED_LEADS);
  }

  public saveLeads(items: Lead[]): void {
    this.set('leads', items);
  }

  // --- Services & Doctors ---
  public getServices(): Service[] {
    return this.get<Service[]>('services', SEED_SERVICES);
  }

  public getDoctors(): Doctor[] {
    return this.get<Doctor[]>('doctors', SEED_DOCTORS);
  }

  // --- Automations ---
  public getAutomations(): Automation[] {
    return this.get<Automation[]>('automations', SEED_AUTOMATIONS);
  }

  public saveAutomations(items: Automation[]): void {
    this.set('automations', items);
  }

  // --- Next Actions ---
  public getNextActions(): DashboardNextAction[] {
    return this.get<DashboardNextAction[]>('next_actions', SEED_NEXT_ACTIONS);
  }

  // --- Outbox Messages ---
  public getOutbox(): OutboxMessage[] {
    const initialOutbox: OutboxMessage[] = [
      {
        id: 'msg-init-1',
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        recipientPhone: '۰۹۱۹۲۳۴۱۱۰۸',
        recipientName: 'نرگس موسوی',
        channel: 'whatsapp',
        content: 'نوبت شما برای فیلر لب امروز ساعت ۱۰:۳۰ در کلینیک آریانا با موفقیت تأیید گردید.',
        status: 'sent',
        triggerEvent: 'appointment.confirmed',
        createdAt: 'امروز ۱۰:۳۰',
      },
      {
        id: 'msg-init-2',
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        recipientPhone: '۰۹۱۲۴۵۶۷۸۹۰',
        recipientName: 'مریم احمدی',
        channel: 'sms',
        content: 'درخواست نوبت بوتاکس شما دریافت شد و در دست بررسی پذیرش کلینیک آریانا قرار دارد.',
        status: 'failed',
        triggerEvent: 'appointment.created',
        createdAt: 'امروز ۰۹:۰۰',
      },
    ];
    return this.get<OutboxMessage[]>('outbox', initialOutbox);
  }

  public saveOutbox(items: OutboxMessage[]): void {
    this.set('outbox', items);
  }

  public clearOutbox(): void {
    this.set('outbox', []);
  }

  public getOutboxMessages(): OutboxMessage[] {
    return this.getOutbox();
  }

  // --- OTP Verification & Rate Limiting ---
  public getOtpState(phone: string): OtpSessionState | null {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const all = this.get<Record<string, OtpSessionState>>('otp_sessions', {});
    return all[norm] || null;
  }

  public saveOtpState(phone: string, state: OtpSessionState): void {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const all = this.get<Record<string, OtpSessionState>>('otp_sessions', {});
    all[norm] = state;
    this.set('otp_sessions', all);
  }

  public clearOtpState(phone: string): void {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const all = this.get<Record<string, OtpSessionState>>('otp_sessions', {});
    delete all[norm];
    this.set('otp_sessions', all);
  }

  // --- OTP Request Rate Limiting (max 3 per 10 minutes) ---
  public getOtpRequestCount(phone: string, withinMinutes: number = 10): number {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const key = `otp_request_rate_${norm}`;
    const timestamps = this.get<number[]>(key, []);
    const threshold = Date.now() - withinMinutes * 60 * 1000;
    return timestamps.filter((t) => t > threshold).length;
  }

  public recordOtpRequest(phone: string): void {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const key = `otp_request_rate_${norm}`;
    const timestamps = this.get<number[]>(key, []);
    const now = Date.now();
    const threshold = now - 10 * 60 * 1000;
    const updated = [...timestamps.filter((t) => t > threshold), now];
    this.set(key, updated);
  }

  // --- Phone Verification Tokens for Booking (Single-use, short-lived) ---
  public saveVerificationToken(phone: string, token: string, expiresAt?: number): void {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const all = this.get<Record<string, PhoneVerificationToken>>('phone_verification_tokens', {});
    const exp = expiresAt !== undefined ? expiresAt : Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000;
    all[token] = { token, phone: norm, expiresAt: exp, status: 'active' };
    this.set('phone_verification_tokens', all);
  }

  public isTokenValid(phone: string, token: string): boolean {
    if (!token) return false;
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const all = this.get<Record<string, PhoneVerificationToken>>('phone_verification_tokens', {});
    const record = all[token];
    if (!record) return false;
    
    // Auto-release stale lock after 30 seconds
    const isLocked = record.status === 'pending_consumption' && record.lockedAt && (Date.now() - record.lockedAt < 30000);
    if (isLocked) return false;

    return record.phone === norm && Date.now() <= record.expiresAt && record.status !== 'consumed';
  }

  /**
   * Atomically locks the token for pending consumption during booking submission.
   * Returns true if successfully acquired lock, false if token is invalid or already locked.
   */
  public lockToken(phone: string, token: string): boolean {
    if (!this.isTokenValid(phone, token)) return false;
    const all = this.get<Record<string, PhoneVerificationToken>>('phone_verification_tokens', {});
    const record = all[token];
    if (!record) return false;

    record.status = 'pending_consumption';
    record.lockedAt = Date.now();
    this.set('phone_verification_tokens', all);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Re-activates or releases a token if a booking fails (e.g. slot collision)
   */
  public releaseToken(token: string): void {
    const all = this.get<Record<string, PhoneVerificationToken>>('phone_verification_tokens', {});
    const record = all[token];
    if (record && record.status === 'pending_consumption') {
      record.status = 'active';
      delete record.lockedAt;
      this.set('phone_verification_tokens', all);
    }
  }

  public consumeToken(token: string): void {
    const all = this.get<Record<string, PhoneVerificationToken>>('phone_verification_tokens', {});
    delete all[token];
    this.set('phone_verification_tokens', all);
  }

  public verifyAndConsumeToken(phone: string, token: string): boolean {
    if (!this.isTokenValid(phone, token)) return false;
    this.consumeToken(token);
    return true;
  }

  // --- Booking Rate Limiting ---
  public getRecentBookingCount(phone: string, withinMinutes: number = 60): number {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const key = `booking_rate_${norm}`;
    const timestamps = this.get<number[]>(key, []);
    const threshold = Date.now() - withinMinutes * 60 * 1000;
    const recent = timestamps.filter((t) => t > threshold);
    return recent.length;
  }

  public recordBookingAttempt(phone: string): void {
    const norm = normalizePhoneNumber(phone);
    const key = `booking_rate_${norm}`;
    const timestamps = this.get<number[]>(key, []);
    const now = Date.now();
    const threshold = now - 60 * 60 * 1000;
    const updated = [...timestamps.filter((t) => t > threshold), now];
    this.set(key, updated);
  }

  public resetToSeed(): void {
    const today = getTodayDateString();
    const freshSeed = buildSeed(today);
    this.set('seed_date', today);
    this.saveAppointments(freshSeed.appointments);
    this.saveClients(freshSeed.clients);
    this.saveLeads(freshSeed.leads);
    this.saveAutomations(freshSeed.automations);
    this.saveUsers(freshSeed.users);
    this.set('outbox', []);
  }

  public getSeedDate(): string | null {
    return this.get<string | null>('seed_date', null);
  }

  public setSeedDate(date: string): void {
    this.set('seed_date', date);
  }
}

export const mockStorage = new MockStorage();

export const simulateDelay = (ms: number = 80): Promise<void> => {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
};

=================== ./src/services/api/httpClient.ts ===================
import { env } from '../../config/env';

export interface RequestOptions extends RequestInit {
  params?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined>;
  _isRetry?: boolean;
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  errors?: Record<string, string[]>;

  constructor(
    status: number,
    message: string,
    errors?: Record<string, string[]>
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.errors = errors;
    this.name = 'ApiError';
  }
}

let authToken: string | null = null;
let refreshHandler: (() => Promise<string | null>) | null = null;
let logoutHandler: (() => void) | null = null;
let refreshPromise: Promise<string | null> | null = null;

function isAuthBypassEndpoint(endpoint: string): boolean {
  return /\/auth\/(otp|refresh|logout)/.test(endpoint);
}

export const setAuthToken = (token: string | null) => {
  authToken = token;
};

export const setAuthHandlers = (
  onRefresh: () => Promise<string | null>,
  onLogout: () => void
) => {
  refreshHandler = onRefresh;
  logoutHandler = onLogout;
};

export const httpClient = {
  async request<T>(endpoint: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
    const { params, headers: customHeaders, _isRetry, ...fetchOptions } = options;

    let url = endpoint.startsWith('http') ? endpoint : `${env.apiBaseUrl}${endpoint}`;

    if (params) {
      const searchParams = new URLSearchParams();
      Object.entries(params).forEach(([key, val]) => {
        if (val !== undefined) searchParams.append(key, String(val));
      });
      const queryString = searchParams.toString();
      if (queryString) {
        url += (url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?') + queryString;
      }
    }

    const headers: HeadersInit = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Accept: 'application/json',
      ...(authToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${authToken}` } : {}),
      ...customHeaders,
    };

    try {
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        credentials: 'include',
        ...fetchOptions,
        headers,
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        // Handle 401 Unauthorized: bypass for /auth/*, single-flight refresh, and trigger logout only if user had session
        if (
          response.status === 401 &&
          !_isRetry &&
          !isAuthBypassEndpoint(endpoint) &&
          refreshHandler
        ) {
          const hadSession = Boolean(authToken);
          try {
            if (!refreshPromise) {
              refreshPromise = refreshHandler().finally(() => {
                refreshPromise = null;
              });
            }
            const newToken = await refreshPromise;
            if (newToken) {
              setAuthToken(newToken);
              return this.request<T>(endpoint, { ...options, _isRetry: true });
            }
          } catch {
            // refresh failed
          }
          if (hadSession && logoutHandler) {
            setAuthToken(null);
            logoutHandler();
          }
        }

        let errorData: Record<string, unknown> = {};
        try {
          errorData = (await response.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;
        } catch {
          // Response body was not JSON
        }

        const message =
          typeof errorData.message === 'string'
            ? errorData.message
            : `خطای ارتباط با سرور: ${response.statusText}`;

        const errors =
          errorData.errors && typeof errorData.errors === 'object'
            ? (errorData.errors as Record<string, string[]>)
            : undefined;

        throw new ApiError(response.status, message, errors);
      }

      return (await response.json()) as T;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError) throw err;
      throw new ApiError(0, 'خطای ارتباط با شبکه. لطفاً اتصال اینترنت خود را بررسی کنید.');
    }
  },

  get<T>(endpoint: string, options?: RequestOptions): Promise<T> {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, { ...options, method: 'GET' });
  },

  post<T>(endpoint: string, data?: unknown, options?: RequestOptions): Promise<T> {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, {
      ...options,
      method: 'POST',
      body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    });
  },

  put<T>(endpoint: string, data?: unknown, options?: RequestOptions): Promise<T> {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, {
      ...options,
      method: 'PUT',
      body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    });
  },

  patch<T>(endpoint: string, data?: unknown, options?: RequestOptions): Promise<T> {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, {
      ...options,
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    });
  },

  delete<T>(endpoint: string, options?: RequestOptions): Promise<T> {
    return this.request<T>(endpoint, { ...options, method: 'DELETE' });
  },
};

=================== ./src/services/index.ts ===================
import { env } from '../config/env';
import type { IAppointmentRepository } from './repositories/IAppointmentRepository';
import type { IClientRepository } from './repositories/IClientRepository';
import type { ILeadRepository } from './repositories/ILeadRepository';
import type { ICatalogRepository } from './repositories/ICatalogRepository';
import type { IAutomationRepository } from './repositories/IAutomationRepository';
import type { IDashboardRepository } from './repositories/IDashboardRepository';
import type { IBookingRepository } from './repositories/IBookingRepository';
import type { IMessageRepository } from './repositories/IMessageRepository';
import type { IAuthRepository } from './repositories/IAuthRepository';
import type { IAuditRepository } from './repositories/IAuditRepository';

import { MockAppointmentRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository';
import { MockClientRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockClientRepository';
import { MockLeadRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockLeadRepository';
import { MockCatalogRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockCatalogRepository';
import { MockAutomationRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockAutomationRepository';
import { MockDashboardRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockDashboardRepository';
import { MockBookingRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockBookingRepository';
import { MockMessageRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockMessageRepository';
import { MockAuthRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockAuthRepository';
import { MockAuditRepository } from './adapters/mock/MockAuditRepository';

import {
  HttpAppointmentRepository,
  HttpClientRepository,
  HttpLeadRepository,
  HttpCatalogRepository,
  HttpAutomationRepository,
  HttpDashboardRepository,
  HttpBookingRepository,
  HttpMessageRepository,
  HttpAuthRepository,
  HttpAuditRepository,
} from './adapters/http';

export interface ServiceContainer {
  appointmentRepo: IAppointmentRepository;
  clientRepo: IClientRepository;
  leadRepo: ILeadRepository;
  catalogRepo: ICatalogRepository;
  automationRepo: IAutomationRepository;
  dashboardRepo: IDashboardRepository;
  bookingRepo: IBookingRepository;
  messageRepo: IMessageRepository;
  authRepo: IAuthRepository;
  auditRepo: IAuditRepository;
}

const createServiceContainer = (): ServiceContainer => {
  const isHttp = env.dataSource === 'http';

  if (isHttp) {
    return {
      appointmentRepo: new HttpAppointmentRepository(),
      clientRepo: new HttpClientRepository(),
      leadRepo: new HttpLeadRepository(),
      catalogRepo: new HttpCatalogRepository(),
      automationRepo: new HttpAutomationRepository(),
      dashboardRepo: new HttpDashboardRepository(),
      bookingRepo: new HttpBookingRepository(),
      messageRepo: new HttpMessageRepository(),
      authRepo: new HttpAuthRepository(),
      auditRepo: new HttpAuditRepository(),
    };
  }

  return {
    appointmentRepo: new MockAppointmentRepository(),
    clientRepo: new MockClientRepository(),
    leadRepo: new MockLeadRepository(),
    catalogRepo: new MockCatalogRepository(),
    automationRepo: new MockAutomationRepository(),
    dashboardRepo: new MockDashboardRepository(),
    bookingRepo: new MockBookingRepository(),
    messageRepo: new MockMessageRepository(),
    authRepo: new MockAuthRepository(),
    auditRepo: new MockAuditRepository(),
  };
};

export const services = createServiceContainer();

      ['appointments', clinicId, 'list', filters] as const,

      ['appointments', clinicId, 'todayQueue', actorId] as const,
    detail: (clinicId: string, id: string) =>
      ['appointments', clinicId, 'detail', id] as const,
    myAppointments: (clinicId: string, phoneOrId: string) =>
      ['appointments', clinicId, 'myAppointments', phoneOrId] as const,
  },

    summary: (clinicId: string) => ['dashboard', clinicId, 'summary'] as const,
    nextActions: (clinicId: string) => ['dashboard', clinicId, 'nextActions'] as const,
  },
  clients: {
    all: (clinicId: string, search?: string) =>
      ['clients', clinicId, { search }] as const,
    atRisk: (clinicId: string) => ['clients', clinicId, 'atRisk'] as const,
    detail: (clinicId: string, id: string) => ['clients', clinicId, id] as const,
  },
  leads: {
    all: (clinicId: string) => ['leads', clinicId] as const,
    hot: (clinicId: string) => ['leads', clinicId, 'hot'] as const,
  },
  catalog: {
    services: (clinicId: string) => ['catalog', clinicId, 'services'] as const,
    doctors: (clinicId: string) => ['catalog', clinicId, 'doctors'] as const,
    clinicBySlug: (slug: string) => ['catalog', 'clinic', slug] as const,
  },
  automations: {
    all: (clinicId: string) => ['automations', clinicId] as const,
    stats: (clinicId: string) => ['automations', clinicId, 'stats'] as const,
  },
  booking: {
    slots: (clinicId: string, doctorId: string, serviceId: string, date: string) =>

  },
  messages: {
    outbox: (clinicId: string) => ['messages', clinicId, 'outbox'] as const,
  },

    all: () => ['integrations', 'all'] as const,
    sidebar: () => ['integrations', 'sidebar'] as const,
  },
  auth: {
    currentUser: () => ['auth', 'currentUser'] as const,
    users: (clinicId: string) => ['auth', clinicId, 'users'] as const,
  },

  search?: string;

=================== ./src/test/appointmentSecurity.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { MockAppointmentRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import type { Actor } from '../domain/session';

describe('Appointment Repository Security & Ownership Rules', () => {
  let aptRepo: MockAppointmentRepository;
  const clinicId = SEED_CLINIC.id; // 'clinic-ariana'

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    aptRepo = new MockAppointmentRepository();
  });

  describe('Doctor Fail-Closed Policy', () => {
    it('returns empty list for doctor without doctorId in getAppointments', async () => {
      const doctorWithoutId: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-doc-ghost',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
      };

      const res = await aptRepo.getAppointments(clinicId, {}, doctorWithoutId);
      expect(res).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('returns empty list for doctor without doctorId in getTodayQueue', async () => {
      const doctorWithoutId: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-doc-ghost',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
      };

      const queue = await aptRepo.getTodayQueue(clinicId, undefined, doctorWithoutId);
      expect(queue).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('returns null for doctor without doctorId in getAppointmentById', async () => {
      const doctorWithoutId: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-doc-ghost',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
      };

      const apt = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', doctorWithoutId);
      expect(apt).toBeNull();
    });

    it('returns only treating doctor appointments when doctorId is provided', async () => {
      const doctorActor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-naderi',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
        doctorId: 'd1',
      };

      const res = await aptRepo.getAppointments(clinicId, {}, doctorActor);
      expect(res.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(res.every((a) => a.doctorId === 'd1')).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('Ownership Rules for Status & Medical Notes Updates', () => {
    it('allows doctor to update status of their own appointment', async () => {
      const doctor1: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-naderi',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
        doctorId: 'd1', // apt-1 has doctorId 'd1'
      };

      // Set apt-1 to confirmed first so doctor can move to in_clinic
      const apts = mockStorage.getAppointments();
      const apt1 = apts.find((a) => a.id === 'apt-1');
      if (apt1) {
        apt1.status = 'confirmed';
        mockStorage.saveAppointments(apts);
      }

      await expect(
        aptRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'in_clinic', doctor1)
      ).resolves.not.toThrow();

      const updated = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', doctor1);
      expect(updated?.status).toBe('in_clinic');
    });

    it('prevents doctor from updating status of another doctor appointment', async () => {
      const doctor2: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-bahrami',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
        doctorId: 'd2', // apt-1 is d1
      };

      await expect(
        aptRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'in_clinic', doctor2)
      ).rejects.toThrow('دسترسی غیرمجاز: پزشک تنها مجاز به مدیریت نوبت‌های خود می‌باشد.');
    });

    it('allows doctor to update medical notes of their own appointment', async () => {
      const doctor1: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-naderi',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
        doctorId: 'd1',
      };

      await expect(
        aptRepo.updateMedicalNotes(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'وضعیت پوست پس از درمان بهبود یافته است.', doctor1)
      ).resolves.not.toThrow();
    });

    it('prevents doctor from updating medical notes of another doctor appointment', async () => {
      const doctor2: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-bahrami',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
        doctorId: 'd2',
      };

      await expect(
        aptRepo.updateMedicalNotes(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'یادداشت غیرمجاز', doctor2)
      ).rejects.toThrow('دسترسی غیرمجاز: پزشک تنها مجاز به ثبت یادداشت در نوبت‌های خود می‌باشد.');
    });

    it('allows patient to cancel their own appointment', async () => {
      const patientActor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-patient',
        role: 'patient',
        clinicId,
        phone: '09121112233', // Matches apt-1
      };

      await expect(
        aptRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'cancelled', patientActor)
      ).resolves.not.toThrow();
    });

    it('prevents patient from cancelling another patient appointment', async () => {
      const otherPatient: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-patient-other',
        role: 'patient',
        clinicId,
        phone: '09129999999', // Does NOT match apt-1
      };

      await expect(
        aptRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'cancelled', otherPatient)
      ).rejects.toThrow('دسترسی غیرمجاز: بیمار تنها مجاز به مدیریت نوبت خود می‌باشد.');
    });
  });

  describe('Medical Notes Visibility Redaction', () => {
    it('strips medicalNotes for reception role across all read queries', async () => {
      const receptionActor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-sara',
        role: 'reception',
        clinicId,
      };

      const listRes = await aptRepo.getAppointments(clinicId, {}, receptionActor);
      expect(listRes.every((a) => a.medicalNotes === undefined)).toBe(true);

      const single = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', receptionActor);
      expect(single?.medicalNotes).toBeUndefined();

      const queue = await aptRepo.getTodayQueue(clinicId, undefined, receptionActor);
      expect(queue.every((a) => a.medicalNotes === undefined)).toBe(true);
    });

    it('strips medicalNotes for admin and patient roles', async () => {
      const adminActor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-admin',
        role: 'admin',
        clinicId,
      };

      const singleAdmin = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', adminActor);
      expect(singleAdmin?.medicalNotes).toBeUndefined();

      const patientActor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-patient',
        role: 'patient',
        clinicId,
        phone: '09121112233',
      };

      const singlePatient = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', patientActor);
      expect(singlePatient?.medicalNotes).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('allows owner to see exact medicalNotes text', async () => {
      const exactNote = 'سابقه حساسیت به لیدوکائین و مصرف آسپرین';
      mockStorage.saveMedicalNote('apt-1', exactNote);

      const ownerActor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-owner',
        role: 'owner',
        clinicId,
      };

      const single = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', ownerActor);
      expect(single).not.toBeNull();
      expect(single?.medicalNotes).toBe(exactNote);
    });

    it('allows treating doctor of appointment to see exact medicalNotes text', async () => {
      const exactNote = 'سابقه حساسیت به لیدوکائین و مصرف آسپرین';
      mockStorage.saveMedicalNote('apt-1', exactNote);

      const treatingDoctor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-naderi',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
        doctorId: 'd1', // apt-1 treating doctor
      };

      const single = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', treatingDoctor);
      expect(single).not.toBeNull();
      expect(single?.medicalNotes).toBe(exactNote);
    });

    it('denies other doctor from seeing appointment or updating medical notes', async () => {
      const exactNote = 'سابقه حساسیت به لیدوکائین و مصرف آسپرین';
      mockStorage.saveMedicalNote('apt-1', exactNote);

      const otherDoctor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-bahrami',
        role: 'doctor',
        clinicId,
        doctorId: 'd2', // apt-1 has doctorId d1
      };

      const single = await aptRepo.getAppointmentById(clinicId, 'apt-1', otherDoctor);
      expect(single).toBeNull();

      await expect(
        aptRepo.updateMedicalNotes(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'تلاش نفوذ دیگر پزشک', otherDoctor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('دسترسی غیرمجاز: پزشک تنها مجاز به ثبت یادداشت در نوبت‌های خود می‌باشد.');
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/appointmentStatus.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  canTransition,
  canTransitionByRole,
  getAvailableTransitions,
  getAvailableTransitionsByRole,
} from '../domain/appointmentStatus';

describe('Appointment Status State Machine', () => {
  it('allows valid transition from pending_confirmation to confirmed or cancelled', () => {
    expect(canTransition('pending_confirmation', 'confirmed')).toBe(true);
    expect(canTransition('pending_confirmation', 'cancelled')).toBe(true);
    expect(canTransition('pending_confirmation', 'completed')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('allows valid transition from confirmed to in_clinic or pending_payment or cancelled', () => {
    expect(canTransition('confirmed', 'in_clinic')).toBe(true);
    expect(canTransition('confirmed', 'pending_payment')).toBe(true);
    expect(canTransition('confirmed', 'cancelled')).toBe(true);
    expect(canTransition('confirmed', 'completed')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('allows valid transition from in_clinic to completed', () => {
    expect(canTransition('in_clinic', 'completed')).toBe(true);
    expect(canTransition('in_clinic', 'pending_confirmation')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('terminal states have no outgoing transitions', () => {
    expect(getAvailableTransitions('completed')).toEqual([]);
    expect(getAvailableTransitions('cancelled')).toEqual([]);
    expect(canTransition('completed', 'confirmed')).toBe(false);
    expect(canTransition('cancelled', 'confirmed')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('does not allow transitioning to the identical state', () => {
    expect(canTransition('confirmed', 'confirmed')).toBe(false);
  });

  describe('Role-Aware State Transitions', () => {
    it('patient can only cancel pending_confirmation or confirmed appointments', () => {
      expect(canTransitionByRole('pending_confirmation', 'cancelled', 'patient')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('confirmed', 'cancelled', 'patient')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('pending_confirmation', 'confirmed', 'patient')).toBe(false);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('in_clinic', 'completed', 'patient')).toBe(false);
      expect(getAvailableTransitionsByRole('confirmed', 'patient')).toEqual(['cancelled']);
    });

    it('doctor can transition in_clinic to completed, but cannot cancel', () => {
      expect(canTransitionByRole('in_clinic', 'completed', 'doctor')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('confirmed', 'in_clinic', 'doctor')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('confirmed', 'cancelled', 'doctor')).toBe(false);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('pending_confirmation', 'cancelled', 'doctor')).toBe(false);
    });

    it('reception can confirm, cancel, and settle or complete in_clinic visits', () => {
      expect(canTransitionByRole('pending_confirmation', 'confirmed', 'reception')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('pending_confirmation', 'cancelled', 'reception')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('confirmed', 'in_clinic', 'reception')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('in_clinic', 'completed', 'reception')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('in_clinic', 'pending_payment', 'reception')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('pending_payment', 'completed', 'reception')).toBe(true);
    });

    it('owner and admin have unrestricted transitions', () => {
      expect(canTransitionByRole('pending_confirmation', 'confirmed', 'owner')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('in_clinic', 'completed', 'owner')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransitionByRole('in_clinic', 'completed', 'admin')).toBe(true);
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/authGuards.test.tsx ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { MemoryRouter, Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthContext, type AuthContextValue } from '../app/contexts';
import { RequireRole } from '../components/auth/RequireRole';
import { RequirePermission } from '../components/auth/RequirePermission';
import { Can } from '../components/auth/Can';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import type { Role, UserProfile, UserSession, Actor } from '../domain/session';

function createMockAuthContext(role: Role | null, isLoading = false): AuthContextValue {
  const user: UserProfile | null = role
    ? {
        id: 'usr-test',
        name: 'کاربر تست',
        phone: '09121110000',
        role,
        roleTitle: 'نقش آزمایشی',
        avatarInitials: 'ت',
        isActive: true,
        createdAt: '1403/01/01',
      }
    : null;

  const session: UserSession | null = user
    ? {
        token: 'test-token',
        user,
        activeClinic: SEED_CLINIC,
        availableClinics: [SEED_CLINIC],
        isAuthenticated: true,
      }
    : null;

  const actor: Actor | null = user
    ? {
        userId: user.id,
        userName: user.name,
        role: user.role,
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
      }
    : null;

  return {
    user,
    role,
    session,
    isAuthenticated: !!user,
    isLoading,
    actor,
    requestOtp: async () => ({ success: true, message: 'ارسال شد', expiresInSeconds: 120 }),
    verifyOtp: async () => ({ success: true, session: session ?? undefined }),
    logout: async () => {},
    updateProfileName: async () => {},
  };
}

describe('Auth Guards & Access Control Components', () => {
  describe('RequireRole', () => {
    it('renders child content when user has the required role', () => {
      const authValue = createMockAuthContext('patient');

      render(
        <AuthContext.Provider value={authValue}>
          <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/me']}>
            <Routes>
              <Route
                path="/me"
                element={
                  <RequireRole roles={['patient']}>
                    <div>پورتال اختصاصی بیمار</div>
                  </RequireRole>
                }
              />
              <Route path="/403" element={<div>عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳</div>} />
            </Routes>
          </MemoryRouter>
        </AuthContext.Provider>
      );

      expect(screen.getByText('پورتال اختصاصی بیمار')).toBeTruthy();
      expect(screen.queryByText('عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳')).toBeNull();
    });

    it('redirects to /403 when user does not have the required role', () => {
      const authValue = createMockAuthContext('reception');

      render(
        <AuthContext.Provider value={authValue}>
          <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/me']}>
            <Routes>
              <Route
                path="/me"
                element={
                  <RequireRole roles={['patient']}>
                    <div>پورتال اختصاصی بیمار</div>
                  </RequireRole>
                }
              />
              <Route path="/403" element={<div>عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳</div>} />
            </Routes>
          </MemoryRouter>
        </AuthContext.Provider>
      );

      expect(screen.queryByText('پورتال اختصاصی بیمار')).toBeNull();
      expect(screen.getByText('عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳')).toBeTruthy();
    });

    it('doctor portal only allows doctor role', () => {
      const patientAuth = createMockAuthContext('patient');

      render(
        <AuthContext.Provider value={patientAuth}>
          <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/doctor']}>
            <Routes>
              <Route
                path="/doctor"
                element={
                  <RequireRole roles={['doctor']}>
                    <div>میز کار پزشک</div>
                  </RequireRole>
                }
              />
              <Route path="/403" element={<div>عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳</div>} />
            </Routes>
          </MemoryRouter>
        </AuthContext.Provider>
      );

      expect(screen.queryByText('میز کار پزشک')).toBeNull();
      expect(screen.getByText('عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳')).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  describe('RequirePermission', () => {
    it('renders child content when user role has permission', () => {
      const authValue = createMockAuthContext('owner');

      render(
        <AuthContext.Provider value={authValue}>
          <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/settings/audit-logs']}>
            <Routes>
              <Route
                path="/settings/audit-logs"
                element={
                  <RequirePermission permission="audit:read">
                    <div>لاگ‌های امنیتی سیستم</div>
                  </RequirePermission>
                }
              />
              <Route path="/403" element={<div>عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳</div>} />
            </Routes>
          </MemoryRouter>
        </AuthContext.Provider>
      );

      expect(screen.getByText('لاگ‌های امنیتی سیستم')).toBeTruthy();
    });

    it('redirects to /403 when user lacks permission', () => {
      const authValue = createMockAuthContext('reception');

      render(
        <AuthContext.Provider value={authValue}>
          <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/settings/audit-logs']}>
            <Routes>
              <Route
                path="/settings/audit-logs"
                element={
                  <RequirePermission permission="audit:read">
                    <div>لاگ‌های امنیتی سیستم</div>
                  </RequirePermission>
                }
              />
              <Route path="/403" element={<div>عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳</div>} />
            </Routes>
          </MemoryRouter>
        </AuthContext.Provider>
      );

      expect(screen.queryByText('لاگ‌های امنیتی سیستم')).toBeNull();
      expect(screen.getByText('عدم دسترسی ۴۰۳')).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  describe('Can component', () => {
    it('renders children if user has permission', () => {
      const authValue = createMockAuthContext('doctor');

      render(
        <AuthContext.Provider value={authValue}>
          <Can permission="medical_notes:write">
            <button>ویرایش پرونده پزشکی</button>
          </Can>
        </AuthContext.Provider>
      );

      expect(screen.getByText('ویرایش پرونده پزشکی')).toBeTruthy();
    });

    it('does not render children if user lacks permission', () => {
      const authValue = createMockAuthContext('reception');

      render(
        <AuthContext.Provider value={authValue}>
          <Can permission="medical_notes:write" fallback={<span>دسترسی محدود</span>}>
            <button>ویرایش پرونده پزشکی</button>
          </Can>
        </AuthContext.Provider>
      );

      expect(screen.queryByText('ویرایش پرونده پزشکی')).toBeNull();
      expect(screen.getByText('دسترسی محدود')).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/authOtp.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { MockAuthRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAuthRepository';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';

describe('Auth Repository & OTP Logic', () => {
  let authRepo: MockAuthRepository;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    localStorage.clear();
    sessionStorage.clear();
    authRepo = new MockAuthRepository();
  });

  it('generates OTP successfully for valid Iranian phone number', async () => {
    const res = await authRepo.requestOtp('09121110003');
    expect(res.success).toBe(true);
    expect(res.expiresInSeconds).toBe(120);
  });

  it('rejects invalid Iranian phone numbers', async () => {
    await expect(authRepo.requestOtp('12345')).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(authRepo.requestOtp('08123456789')).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('prevents inactive users from requesting OTP', async () => {
    // Create an inactive user in storage
    const users = mockStorage.getUsers();
    users.push({
      id: 'usr-inactive',
      name: 'کاربر مسدود',
      phone: '09129990000',
      role: 'reception',
      roleTitle: 'پذیرش',
      avatarInitials: 'ک',
      isActive: false,
      createdAt: '1403/01/01',
    });
    mockStorage.saveUsers(users);

    await expect(authRepo.requestOtp('09129990000')).rejects.toThrow(
      'حساب کاربری شما توسط مدیر سیستم غیرفعال شده است.'
    );
  });

  it('verifies correct OTP code (123456) and provisions session', async () => {
    await authRepo.requestOtp('09121110003');
    const verifyRes = await authRepo.verifyOtp('09121110003', '123456');

    expect(verifyRes.success).toBe(true);
    expect(verifyRes.session).toBeDefined();
    expect(verifyRes.session?.user.name).toBe('سارا حسینی');
    expect(verifyRes.session?.user.role).toBe('reception');
    expect(verifyRes.session?.isAuthenticated).toBe(true);
  });

  it('fails with incorrect code and locks out after 3 failed attempts', async () => {
    const phone = '09121110001';
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone);

    // 1st wrong attempt
    const attempt1 = await authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, '999999');
    expect(attempt1.success).toBe(false);
    expect(attempt1.error).toContain('کد وارد شده صحیح نمی‌باشد');

    // 2nd wrong attempt
    const attempt2 = await authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, '888888');
    expect(attempt2.success).toBe(false);

    // 3rd wrong attempt -> lockout
    const attempt3 = await authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, '777777');
    expect(attempt3.success).toBe(false);
    expect(attempt3.error).toContain('۳ بار کد اشتباه وارد شد');

    // Subsequent requestOtp is also locked
    await expect(authRepo.requestOtp(phone)).rejects.toThrow('تعداد تلاش‌های ناموفق');
  });

  it('fails verification if OTP is expired', async () => {
    const phone = '09121110002';
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone);

    // Manually expire the OTP in storage
    const state = mockStorage.getOtpState(phone);
    if (state) {
      state.expiresAt = Date.now() - 1000;
      mockStorage.saveOtpState(phone, state);
    }

    const res = await authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, '123456');
    expect(res.success).toBe(false);
    expect(res.error).toContain('منقضی شده است');
  });

  it('logs out and clears session', async () => {
    await authRepo.requestOtp('09121110003');
    await authRepo.verifyOtp('09121110003', '123456');

    const currentUserBefore = await authRepo.getCurrentUser();
    expect(currentUserBefore).not.toBeNull();

    await authRepo.logout();
    const currentUserAfter = await authRepo.getCurrentUser();
    expect(currentUserAfter).toBeNull();
  });

  it('requestOtp preserves failed attempts counter and does not reset attempts', async () => {
    const phone = '09121110001';
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone);

    // 2 failed attempts
    await authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, '000000');
    await authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, '111111');

    // Request new OTP
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone);

    // 3rd failed attempt should lock out immediately
    const res = await authRepo.verifyOtp(phone, '222222');
    expect(res.success).toBe(false);
    expect(res.error).toContain('۳ بار کد اشتباه وارد شد');

    // Account locked
    await expect(authRepo.requestOtp(phone)).rejects.toThrow('تعداد تلاش‌های ناموفق');
  });

  it('enforces rate limit of maximum 3 OTP requests within 10 minutes', async () => {
    const phone = '09125556677';
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone); // 1
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone); // 2
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone); // 3

    // 4th request must throw rate limit error
    await expect(authRepo.requestOtp(phone)).rejects.toThrow('بیش از حد مجاز است');
  });

  it('verifyPhoneOwnership validates code and returns single-use token without logging in user', async () => {
    const phone = '09123334455';
    // Initially not logged in
    expect(await authRepo.getCurrentUser()).toBeNull();

    // Request OTP first
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone);

    const result = await authRepo.verifyPhoneOwnership(phone, '123456');
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.verificationToken).toBeDefined();
    expect(result.verificationToken?.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);

    // Still not logged in (no session created)
    expect(await authRepo.getCurrentUser()).toBeNull();

    // Rejects invalid code
    const invalidResult = await authRepo.verifyPhoneOwnership(phone, '000000');
    expect(invalidResult.success).toBe(false);
    expect(invalidResult.error).toBeDefined();
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/bookingToken.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { MockBookingRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockBookingRepository';
import { MockAuthRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAuthRepository';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import type { BookingSubmissionInput } from '../services/repositories/IBookingRepository';

describe('Booking Repository & Verification Token Protection', () => {
  let bookingRepo: MockBookingRepository;
  let authRepo: MockAuthRepository;
  const clinicId = SEED_CLINIC.id;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    bookingRepo = new MockBookingRepository();
    authRepo = new MockAuthRepository();
  });

  const createValidBookingInput = (phone: string, token: string): BookingSubmissionInput => ({
    clinicId,
    serviceId: 's1',
    doctorId: 'd1',
    date: '1403/07/20',
    time: '14:00',
    clientName: 'مریم حسینی',
    clientPhone: phone,
    verificationToken: token,
    captchaToken: 'valid-captcha-mock',
    notes: 'توضیحات تستی',
  });

  it('rejects booking submission if verificationToken is missing or invalid', async () => {
    const input = createValidBookingInput('09121114455', 'fake-token-12345');
    await expect(bookingRepo.submitBooking(input)).rejects.toThrow(
      'اعتبارسنجی شماره تلفن همراه نامعتبر است'
    );
  });

  it('successfully books appointment with verified token and consumes it', async () => {
    const phone = '09121114455';
    // 1. Request OTP & verify phone to get valid token
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phone);
    const verifyRes = await authRepo.verifyPhoneOwnership(phone, '123456');
    expect(verifyRes.success).toBe(true);
    const token = verifyRes.verificationToken!;
    expect(token).toBeDefined();

    // 2. Submit booking using the token
    const input = createValidBookingInput(phone, token);
    const bookingResult = await bookingRepo.submitBooking(input);
    expect(bookingResult.appointment.id).toBeDefined();
    expect(bookingResult.trackingCode).toBeDefined();

    // 3. Token cannot be reused (single-use)
    const secondInput = createValidBookingInput(phone, token);
    await expect(bookingRepo.submitBooking(secondInput)).rejects.toThrow(
      'اعتبارسنجی شماره تلفن همراه نامعتبر است'
    );
  });

  it('rejects booking if token does not match the client phone', async () => {
    const phoneA = '09121114455';
    const phoneB = '09129998877';

    // Verify phoneA
    await authRepo.requestOtp(phoneA);
    const verifyRes = await authRepo.verifyPhoneOwnership(phoneA, '123456');
    const tokenA = verifyRes.verificationToken!;

    // Attempt to book for phoneB using token for phoneA
    const input = createValidBookingInput(phoneB, tokenA);
    await expect(bookingRepo.submitBooking(input)).rejects.toThrow(
      'اعتبارسنجی شماره تلفن همراه نامعتبر است'
    );
  });

  it('enforces booking rate limit (max 3 bookings per hour per phone)', async () => {
    const phone = '09123332211';
    const times = ['10:00', '11:00', '12:00', '13:00'];

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      mockStorage.clearOtpState(phone);
      await authRepo.requestOtp(phone);
      const v = await authRepo.verifyPhoneOwnership(phone, '123456');
      const input = {
        ...createValidBookingInput(phone, v.verificationToken!),
        time: times[i],
      };
      const res = await bookingRepo.submitBooking(input);
      expect(res.appointment.id).toBeDefined();
    }

    // 4th booking within an hour must be rejected by booking repo
    const token4 = 'vtok-test-rate-limit-4';
    mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(phone, token4, Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);
    const input4 = {
      ...createValidBookingInput(phone, token4),
      time: times[3],
    };
    await expect(bookingRepo.submitBooking(input4)).rejects.toThrow('بیش از حد مجاز در ساعت است');
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/format.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  toPersianDigits,
  toEnglishDigits,
  formatCurrency,
  formatDuration,
  getInitials,
} from '../lib/format';

describe('Format and Digit Conversion Helpers', () => {
  it('converts English digits to Persian digits', () => {
    expect(toPersianDigits('0123456789')).toBe('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹');
    expect(toPersianDigits(1405)).toBe('۱۴۰۵');
  });

  it('converts Arabic digits to Persian digits', () => {
    expect(toPersianDigits('٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩')).toBe('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹');
  });

  it('converts Persian digits back to English digits', () => {
    expect(toEnglishDigits('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹')).toBe('0123456789');
    expect(toEnglishDigits('۰۹:۰۰')).toBe('09:00');
  });

  it('formats monetary currency in Tomans with Persian thousands separator', () => {
    expect(formatCurrency(12800000)).toBe('۱۲٬۸۰۰٬۰۰۰ تومان');
    expect(formatCurrency(4500000)).toBe('۴٬۵۰۰٬۰۰۰ تومان');
  });

  it('formats duration in minutes', () => {
    expect(formatDuration(30)).toBe('۳۰ دقیقه');
    expect(formatDuration(90)).toBe('۹۰ دقیقه');
  });

  it('extracts initials from Persian names', () => {
    expect(getInitials('سارا حسینی')).toBe('س');
    expect(getInitials('مریم احمدی')).toBe('م');
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/httpAdapters404AndSingleFlight.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError, setAuthToken } from '../services/api/httpClient';
import {
  HttpAppointmentRepository,
  HttpClientRepository,
  HttpCatalogRepository,
  HttpAuthRepository,
} from '../services/adapters/http';

describe('HTTP Adapters: 404 Null Handling & Single-Flight Refresh', () => {
  const originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;

  beforeEach(() => {
    setAuthToken(null);
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  describe('1. 404 Null Return for getById methods', () => {
    const appointmentRepo = new HttpAppointmentRepository();
    const clientRepo = new HttpClientRepository();
    const catalogRepo = new HttpCatalogRepository();

    it('HttpAppointmentRepository.getAppointmentById returns null on 404 and throws on other errors', async () => {
      // 404 response
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
        statusText: 'Not Found',
        json: async () => ({ code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'نوبت یافت نشد' }),
      });

      const result = await appointmentRepo.getAppointmentById('ariana', 'non-existent-id');
      expect(result).toBeNull();

      // 500 response -> must throw
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 500,
        statusText: 'Internal Server Error',
        json: async () => ({ message: 'خطای سرور' }),
      });

      await expect(appointmentRepo.getAppointmentById('ariana', 'err-id')).rejects.toThrow(ApiError);
    });

    it('HttpClientRepository.getClientById returns null on 404 and throws on other errors', async () => {
      // 404 response
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
        statusText: 'Not Found',
        json: async () => ({ code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'پرونده مراجع یافت نشد' }),
      });

      const result = await clientRepo.getClientById('ariana', 'non-existent-client');
      expect(result).toBeNull();

      // 403 response -> must throw
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 403,
        statusText: 'Forbidden',
        json: async () => ({ message: 'عدم دسترسی' }),
      });

      await expect(clientRepo.getClientById('ariana', 'forbidden-client')).rejects.toThrow(ApiError);
    });

    it('HttpCatalogRepository.getServiceById returns null on 404 and throws on other errors', async () => {
      // 404 response
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
        statusText: 'Not Found',
        json: async () => ({ code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'خدمت یافت نشد' }),
      });

      const result = await catalogRepo.getServiceById('ariana', 'non-existent-srv');
      expect(result).toBeNull();

      // 500 response -> must throw
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 500,
        statusText: 'Internal Server Error',
        json: async () => ({ message: 'خطا' }),
      });

      await expect(catalogRepo.getServiceById('ariana', 'err-srv')).rejects.toThrow(ApiError);
    });

    it('HttpCatalogRepository.getDoctorById returns null on 404 and throws on other errors', async () => {
      // 404 response
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
        statusText: 'Not Found',
        json: async () => ({ code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'پزشک یافت نشد' }),
      });

      const result = await catalogRepo.getDoctorById('ariana', 'non-existent-doc');
      expect(result).toBeNull();

      // 500 response -> must throw
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 500,
        statusText: 'Internal Server Error',
        json: async () => ({ message: 'خطا' }),
      });

      await expect(catalogRepo.getDoctorById('ariana', 'err-doc')).rejects.toThrow(ApiError);
    });
  });

  describe('2. Single-Flight Refresh in HttpAuthRepository', () => {
    it('concurrent refreshSession calls share one in-flight promise and send only one HTTP request', async () => {
      const authRepo = new HttpAuthRepository();

      let refreshNetworkCalls = 0;
      let meNetworkCalls = 0;

      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (url: string) => {
        const urlStr = String(url);

        if (urlStr.includes('/auth/refresh')) {
          refreshNetworkCalls++;
          // Simulate network latency
          await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
          return {
            ok: true,
            status: 200,
            json: async () => ({
              access_token: 'new-access-token',
              user: {
                id: 'usr-1',
                name: 'دکتر کاوه',
                role: 'owner',
                role_title: 'صاحب کلینیک',
                avatar_initials: 'د',
                phone: '09121110001',
                is_active: true,
                created_at: '2026-10-20T00:00:00Z',
              },
            }),
          };
        }

        if (urlStr.includes('/auth/me')) {
          meNetworkCalls++;
          return {
            ok: true,
            status: 200,
            json: async () => ({
              user: {
                id: 'usr-1',
                name: 'دکتر کاوه',
                role: 'owner',
                role_title: 'صاحب کلینیک',
                avatar_initials: 'د',
                phone: '09121110001',
                is_active: true,
                created_at: '2026-10-20T00:00:00Z',
              },
              active_clinic: {
                id: 'ariana',
                slug: 'ariana',
                name: 'کلینیک آریانا',
                tagline: '',
                phone: '02188888888',
                address: '',
                working_hours: '۹ تا ۲۰',
              },
              available_clinics: [],
            }),
          };
        }

        return {
          ok: false,
          status: 404,
          json: async () => ({ message: 'Not found' }),
        };
      });

      // Fire 3 concurrent refresh calls (simulating React StrictMode & multiple components at boot)
      const [res1, res2, res3] = await Promise.all([
        authRepo.refreshSession(),
        authRepo.refreshSession(),
        authRepo.refreshSession(),
      ]);

      // Exactly ONE request to /auth/refresh must have been made
      expect(refreshNetworkCalls).toBe(1);

      // All 3 callers must receive the exact same valid session
      expect(res1).not.toBeNull();
      expect(res1?.token).toBe('new-access-token');
      expect(res1?.user.name).toBe('دکتر کاوه');
      expect(res2).toEqual(res1);
      expect(res3).toEqual(res1);

      // After in-flight promise completes, a new call must initiate a new request
      const subsequentCall = await authRepo.refreshSession();
      expect(refreshNetworkCalls).toBe(2);
      expect(subsequentCall?.token).toBe('new-access-token');
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/httpClientAuth.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { httpClient, setAuthToken, setAuthHandlers } from '../services/api/httpClient';
import { HttpAuthRepository } from '../services/adapters/http/HttpAuthRepository';

describe('HTTP Client & Auth Refresh Loop Protection (Phase 1)', () => {
  const originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;

  beforeEach(() => {
    setAuthToken(null);
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it('does NOT trigger refresh loop or logout when /auth/* returns 401', async () => {
    const refreshFn = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue('new-token');
    const logoutFn = vi.fn();
    setAuthHandlers(refreshFn, logoutFn);

    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: false,
      status: 401,
      statusText: 'Unauthorized',
      json: async () => ({ message: 'Refresh token expired' }),
    });

    // Calling an /auth/* endpoint like /auth/refresh
    await expect(httpClient.post('/auth/refresh')).rejects.toThrow();

    // refreshFn and logoutFn must NOT have been called
    expect(refreshFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(logoutFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('deduplicates concurrent 401s into a single refresh request (single-flight)', async () => {
    let refreshCallCount = 0;
    const refreshFn = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async () => {
      refreshCallCount++;
      // Simulate delay in refresh
      await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, 50));
      return 'refreshed-jwt-token';
    });
    const logoutFn = vi.fn();
    setAuthHandlers(refreshFn, logoutFn);
    setAuthToken('expired-jwt');

    let requestCount = 0;
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (_url: string, options: RequestInit) => {
      requestCount++;
      const authHeader = (options.headers as Record<string, string>)?.['Authorization'];
      if (authHeader === 'Bearer refreshed-jwt-token') {
        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({ success: true, data: 'protected-data' }),
        };
      }
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: 401,
        statusText: 'Unauthorized',
        json: async () => ({ message: 'Token expired' }),
      };
    });

    // Launch two concurrent protected requests
    const [res1, res2] = await Promise.all([
      httpClient.get<{ success: boolean; data: string }>('/protected-resource-1'),
      httpClient.get<{ success: boolean; data: string }>('/protected-resource-2'),
    ]);

    expect(res1.data).toBe('protected-data');
    expect(res2.data).toBe('protected-data');
    // Crucial: Single-flight must ensure refreshHandler was called exactly ONCE!
    expect(refreshCallCount).toBe(1);
    expect(refreshFn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(requestCount).toBe(4);
  });

  it('refreshSession returns newly issued token inside session object', async () => {
    const httpAuthRepo = new HttpAuthRepository();

    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (url: string) => {
      const urlStr = String(url);
      if (urlStr.includes('/auth/refresh')) {
        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            access_token: 'newly-minted-token-xyz',
            user: {
              id: 'usr-1',
              name: 'علی محمدی',
              role: 'doctor',
              role_title: 'پزشک معالج',
              avatar_initials: 'ع',
              phone: '09121110004',
              is_active: true,
              created_at: '1403/01/01',
            },
          }),
        };
      }
      if (urlStr.includes('/auth/me')) {
        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            user: {
              id: 'usr-1',
              name: 'علی محمدی',
              role: 'doctor',
              role_title: 'پزشک معالج',
              avatar_initials: 'ع',
              phone: '09121110004',
              is_active: true,
              created_at: '1403/01/01',
            },
            active_clinic: {
              id: 'c1',
              slug: 'ariana',
              name: 'کلینیک آریانا',
              tagline: 'کلینیک تخصصی',
              phone: '02188888888',
              address: 'تهران',
              working_hours: '9-20',
            },
            available_clinics: [],
          }),
        };
      }
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
        json: async () => ({}),
      };
    });

    const refreshed = await httpAuthRepo.refreshSession();
    expect(refreshed).not.toBeNull();
    expect(refreshed?.token).toBe('newly-minted-token-xyz');
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/manualAppointmentTime.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { toEnglishDigits } from '../lib/format';

describe('Manual Appointment Time Validation Regex', () => {
  const timeRegex = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;

  const isValidTime = (time: string): boolean => {
    const normalized = toEnglishDigits(time).trim();
    return timeRegex.test(normalized);
  };

  it('accepts valid 24-hour format times with English digits', () => {
    expect(isValidTime('00:00')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('09:15')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('12:00')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('16:30')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('23:59')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('accepts valid 24-hour format times with Persian digits', () => {
    expect(isValidTime('۰۰:۰۰')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('۰۹:۱۵')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('۱۲:۰۰')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('۱۶:۰۰')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTime('۲۳:۵۹')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects invalid hour or minute values', () => {
    expect(isValidTime('24:00')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('25:10')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('14:60')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('14:99')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects improperly formatted strings or single digit hours without leading zero', () => {
    expect(isValidTime('9:30')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('16')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('16:0')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('abcd')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidTime('16:00:00')).toBe(false);
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/patientReadOnly.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { MockAppointmentRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import { can } from '../domain/permissions';
import type { Actor } from '../domain/session';

describe('Patient read-only enforcement', () => {
  let repo: MockAppointmentRepository;
  const clinicId = SEED_CLINIC.id;
  const patient: Actor = {
    userId: 'usr-patient',
    role: 'patient',
    clinicId,
    phone: '09121112233',
    clientId: 'c1',
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    repo = new MockAppointmentRepository();
  });

  it('patient permissions', () => {
    expect(can('patient', 'appointments:create')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('patient', 'appointments:update_status')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('patient', 'appointments:read')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('patient', 'appointments:read_own')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('patient cannot create appointment via repository', async () => {
    await expect(
      repo.createAppointment(
        clinicId,
        {
          clinicId,
          date: '2026-11-01',
          time: '10:00',
          clientName: 'x',
          clientPhone: '09121112233',
          serviceId: 's1',
          doctorId: 'd1',
          source: 'online_booking',
        },
        patient
      )
    ).rejects.toThrow('دسترسی غیرمجاز');
  });

  it('patient cannot update status via repository', async () => {
    await expect(
      repo.updateStatus(clinicId, 'apt-1', 'confirmed', patient)
    ).rejects.toThrow('دسترسی غیرمجاز');
  });

  it('patient only sees their own appointments', async () => {
    const list = await repo.getAppointments(clinicId, undefined, patient);
    expect(list.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const apt of list) {
      const matchesPhone = apt.clientPhone === '09121112233';
      const matchesClient = apt.clientId === 'c1';
      expect(matchesPhone || matchesClient).toBe(true);
    }
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/permissions.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { can, canAny, canAll } from '../domain/permissions';

describe('Permissions & RBAC Matrix', () => {
  it('verifies owner has full permissions including financial and audit', () => {
    expect(can('owner', 'reports:financial')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('owner', 'audit:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('owner', 'medical_notes:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('owner', 'users:manage')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('owner', 'settings:manage')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('verifies admin has user and settings management, but no sensitive financial reports or audit log', () => {
    expect(can('admin', 'users:manage')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('admin', 'settings:manage')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('admin', 'reports:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('admin', 'reports:financial')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('admin', 'audit:read')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('verifies reception has NO medical_notes:read, NO settings:manage, NO reports:read, NO users:manage', () => {
    expect(can('reception', 'medical_notes:read')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('reception', 'medical_notes:write')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('reception', 'settings:manage')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('reception', 'reports:read')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('reception', 'reports:financial')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('reception', 'users:manage')).toBe(false);

    // Reception HAS appointments, leads, clients, messages
    expect(can('reception', 'appointments:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('reception', 'appointments:create')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('reception', 'appointments:update_status')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('reception', 'clients:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('reception', 'leads:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('reception', 'messages:read')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('verifies doctor has NO leads:read, NO marketing:manage, NO settings:manage, NO reports:read', () => {
    expect(can('doctor', 'leads:read')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('doctor', 'leads:manage')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('doctor', 'marketing:manage')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('doctor', 'settings:manage')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('doctor', 'reports:read')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('doctor', 'reports:financial')).toBe(false);

    // Doctor HAS medical notes and own appointments
    expect(can('doctor', 'medical_notes:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('doctor', 'medical_notes:write')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('doctor', 'appointments:read_own')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('doctor', 'appointments:update_status')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('verifies patient only has access to own appointments and client profile', () => {
    expect(can('patient', 'appointments:read_own')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('patient', 'appointments:update_status')).toBe(true);
    expect(can('patient', 'appointments:read')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('patient', 'leads:read')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('patient', 'settings:manage')).toBe(false);
    expect(can('patient', 'medical_notes:read')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('verifies helper functions canAny and canAll', () => {
    expect(canAny('reception', ['settings:manage', 'leads:read'])).toBe(true);
    expect(canAny('patient', ['settings:manage', 'leads:read'])).toBe(false);

    expect(canAll('owner', ['appointments:read', 'medical_notes:read', 'reports:financial'])).toBe(true);
    expect(canAll('reception', ['appointments:read', 'medical_notes:read'])).toBe(false);
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/phase2NormalizationAndSeed.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { buildSeed } from '../mocks/seed';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { MockAppointmentRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository';
import { MockClientRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockClientRepository';
import { formatTimeFa, formatTrackingCodeFa, toEnglishDigits } from '../lib/format';
import { formatPhoneNumber } from '../lib/phone';

describe('Phase 2: Data Normalization, Seed & Formatting Rules', () => {
  const testDate = '2026-10-18';
  let aptRepo: MockAppointmentRepository;
  let clientRepo: MockClientRepository;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    aptRepo = new MockAppointmentRepository();
    clientRepo = new MockClientRepository();
  });

  describe('buildSeed Structure & Validation', () => {
    it('generates exactly 24 appointments for today', () => {
      const seed = buildSeed(testDate);
      const todayApts = seed.appointments.filter((a) => a.date === testDate);
      expect(todayApts).toHaveLength(24);
    });

    it('has all valid HH:mm times with valid minutes (< 60) within working hours 9:00 - 20:00', () => {
      const seed = buildSeed(testDate);
      const timeRegex = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;

      for (const apt of seed.appointments) {
        // Must be canonical Latin digits
        expect(apt.time).toMatch(timeRegex);

        const [h, m] = apt.time.split(':').map(Number);
        const startMinutes = h * 60 + m;
        const endMinutes = startMinutes + apt.durationMinutes;

        // Must be within doctor working hours: 09:00 (540) to 20:00 (1200)
        expect(startMinutes).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(9 * 60);
        expect(endMinutes).toBeLessThanOrEqual(20 * 60);

        // Canonical trackingCode format AR-xxxx with Latin digits
        expect(apt.trackingCode).toMatch(/^AR-\d+$/);

        // Canonical clientPhone starting with 09 and Latin digits
        expect(apt.clientPhone).toMatch(/^09\d{9}$/);
      }
    });

    it('has zero overlapping appointments for the same doctor on the same day', () => {
      const seed = buildSeed(testDate);
      const aptsByDoctorAndDate = new Map<string, typeof seed.appointments>();

      for (const apt of seed.appointments) {
        const key = `${apt.doctorId}_${apt.date}`;
        const list = aptsByDoctorAndDate.get(key) || [];
        list.push(apt);
        aptsByDoctorAndDate.set(key, list);
      }

      for (const [, list] of aptsByDoctorAndDate.entries()) {
        const intervals = list.map((a) => {
          const [h, m] = a.time.split(':').map(Number);
          const start = h * 60 + m;
          const end = start + a.durationMinutes;
          return { id: a.id, start, end };
        });

        // Check each pair for overlap: startA < endB && endA > startB
        for (let i = 0; i < intervals.length; i++) {
          for (let j = i + 1; j < intervals.length; j++) {
            const a = intervals[i];
            const b = intervals[j];
            const hasOverlap = a.start < b.end && a.end > b.start;
            expect(hasOverlap, `Conflict detected between ${a.id} and ${b.id}`).toBe(false);
          }
        }
      }
    });

    it('contains between 5 to 8 appointments for tomorrow', () => {
      const seed = buildSeed(testDate);
      const tomorrowApts = seed.appointments.filter((a) => a.date > testDate);
      expect(tomorrowApts.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5);
      expect(tomorrowApts.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);
    });

    it('includes patient user client ID c1 inside SEED_CLIENTS with Latin phone', () => {
      const seed = buildSeed(testDate);
      const clientC1 = seed.clients.find((c) => c.id === 'c1');
      expect(clientC1).toBeDefined();
      expect(clientC1?.phone).toMatch(/^09\d{9}$/);
    });
  });

  describe('Storage Date Rollover & Fresh Seed Generation', () => {
    it('refreshes seed appointments when date changes while preserving custom user appointments', () => {
      // Save an appointment for yesterday under a previous seed_date
      mockStorage.setSeedDate('2026-10-17');
      const customUserApt = {
        id: 'apt-custom-user-1',
        clinicId: 'clinic-ariana',
        trackingCode: 'AR-9999',
        date: testDate,
        time: '18:00',
        clientName: 'کاربر تستی جدید',
        clientPhone: '09129990000',
        serviceId: 's1',
        serviceName: 'بوتاکس',
        doctorId: 'd1',
        doctorName: 'دکتر نادری',
        durationMinutes: 30,
        price: 4500000,
        status: 'confirmed' as const,
        source: 'online_booking' as const,
        createdAt: '2026-10-18T10:00:00Z',
        updatedAt: '2026-10-18T10:00:00Z',
      };
      mockStorage.saveAppointments([customUserApt]);

      // Calling getAppointments triggers rollover to today's seed
      const apts = mockStorage.getAppointments();
      expect(apts.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(24);
      // User created appointment must be preserved
      expect(apts.some((a) => a.id === 'apt-custom-user-1')).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('Search with Persian & English digits', () => {
    it('finds appointments when search query contains Persian digits ۰۹۱۲', async () => {
      const results = await aptRepo.getAppointments('clinic-ariana', { search: '۰۹۱۲' });
      expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(results.every((a) => a.clientPhone.includes('0912') || toEnglishDigits(a.clientPhone).includes('0912'))).toBe(true);
    });

    it('finds clients when searching with Persian digits', async () => {
      const clients = await clientRepo.getClients('clinic-ariana', '۰۹۱۲');
      expect(clients.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });
  });

  describe('Formatting Helpers in lib/format.ts', () => {
    it('formats time to Persian digits with formatTimeFa', () => {
      expect(formatTimeFa('09:00')).toBe('۰۹:۰۰');
      expect(formatTimeFa('14:30')).toBe('۱۴:۳۰');
    });

    it('formats tracking code to Persian digits with formatTrackingCodeFa', () => {
      expect(formatTrackingCodeFa('AR-8492')).toBe('AR-۸۴۹۲');
    });

    it('formats phone numbers using formatPhoneNumber', () => {
      expect(formatPhoneNumber('09121112233')).toBe('۰۹۱۲ ۱۱۱ ۲۲۳۳');
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/phase3BookingFlow.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { MockBookingRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockBookingRepository';
import { MockCatalogRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockCatalogRepository';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import type { BookingSubmissionInput } from '../services/repositories/IBookingRepository';

import { getTodayDateString } from '../lib/jalali';

describe('Phase 3: Booking Flow & Hardening', () => {
  let bookingRepo: MockBookingRepository;
  let catalogRepo: MockCatalogRepository;
  const clinicId = SEED_CLINIC.id;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    bookingRepo = new MockBookingRepository();
    catalogRepo = new MockCatalogRepository();
  });

  const createInput = (overrides: Partial<BookingSubmissionInput> = {}): BookingSubmissionInput => ({
    clinicId,
    serviceId: 's1',
    doctorId: 'd1',
    date: getTodayDateString(),
    time: '18:30',
    clientName: 'نیلوفر راد',
    clientPhone: '09124445566',
    verificationToken: 'valid-vtok-test',
    captchaToken: 'mock-captcha-token-valid',
    ...overrides,
  });

  describe('1. Phone Verification Token Preservation on Failures', () => {
    it('does not consume verification token when a booking conflict occurs', async () => {
      const phone = '09124445566';
