      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(phone, 'tok-conflict-test', Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

      // Book an initial appointment at 10:30 with doctor d1
      const initialInput = createInput({
        time: '10:30',
        verificationToken: 'tok-conflict-test',
        clientPhone: phone,
      });

      // Cause a conflict by attempting to book the exact same slot that is already booked in seed
      // (seed has apt-1 at 09:00, apt-2 at 10:30 with d1)
      await expect(bookingRepo.submitBooking(initialInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'این ساعت توسط کاربر دیگری رزرو شد'
      );

      // Verify the token is STILL valid and NOT consumed because the booking failed!
      expect(mockStorage.isTokenValid(phone, 'tok-conflict-test')).toBe(true);

      // The user can now use the SAME token to book a non-conflicting time (18:30)
      const nonConflictingInput = createInput({
        time: '18:30',
        verificationToken: 'tok-conflict-test',
        clientPhone: phone,
      });

      const result = await bookingRepo.submitBooking(nonConflictingInput);
      expect(result.appointment.id).toBeDefined();

      // After a SUCCESSFUL booking, the token IS consumed
      expect(mockStorage.isTokenValid(phone, 'tok-conflict-test')).toBe(false);
    });

    it('does not consume rate limit on failed booking', async () => {
      const phone = '09128889900';
      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(phone, 'tok-rate-limit-test', Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

      const beforeCount = mockStorage.getRecentBookingCount(phone, 60);

      // Attempt conflicting booking
      const conflictingInput = createInput({
        time: '09:00', // d1 already has appointment at 09:00
        verificationToken: 'tok-rate-limit-test',
        clientPhone: phone,
      });

      await expect(bookingRepo.submitBooking(conflictingInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'این ساعت توسط کاربر دیگری رزرو شد'
      );

      const afterCount = mockStorage.getRecentBookingCount(phone, 60);
      expect(afterCount).toBe(beforeCount);
    });
  });

  describe('2. Captcha Token Validation', () => {
    it('rejects booking submission when captchaToken is missing or empty', async () => {
      const phone = '09121112244';
      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(phone, 'tok-captcha-test', Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

      const inputWithoutCaptcha = createInput({
        verificationToken: 'tok-captcha-test',
        clientPhone: phone,
        captchaToken: '',
      });

      await expect(bookingRepo.submitBooking(inputWithoutCaptcha)).rejects.toThrow(
        'کپچا'
      );
    });

    it('HttpBookingRepository includes captcha_token in request payload', async () => {
      const { HttpBookingRepository } = await import('../services/adapters/http');
      let sentBody: any = null;
      const originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;
      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (_url: string, options: RequestInit) => {
        sentBody = JSON.parse(options.body as string);
        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            appointment: {
              id: 'apt-http-1',
              clinic_id: clinicId,
              tracking_code: 'AR-7777',
              date: '2026-10-18',
              time: '18:00',
              client_name: 'آرزو',
              client_phone: '09121112233',
              service_id: 's1',
              service_name: 'بوتاکس',
              doctor_id: 'd1',
              doctor_name: 'دکتر نادری',
              duration_minutes: 30,
              price: 4500000,
              status: 'pending_confirmation',
              source: 'online_booking',
              created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
              updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            },
            tracking_code: 'AR-7777',
          }),
        };
      });

      try {
        const httpBookingRepo = new HttpBookingRepository();
        const res = await httpBookingRepo.submitBooking(createInput({ captchaToken: 'turnstile-token-xyz' }));
        expect(res.trackingCode).toBe('AR-7777');
        expect(sentBody?.captcha_token).toBe('turnstile-token-xyz');
      } finally {
        globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
      }
    });
  });

  describe('3. Clinic Slug Resolution via Catalog Repository', () => {
    it('returns clinic for valid slug "ariana" and null for invalid slug', async () => {
      const validClinic = await catalogRepo.getClinicBySlug('ariana');
      expect(validClinic).not.toBeNull();
      expect(validClinic?.slug).toBe('ariana');

      const invalidClinic = await catalogRepo.getClinicBySlug('non-existent-clinic-slug');
      expect(invalidClinic).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('4. Client Upserting on Booking', () => {
    it('upserts a new client with status new and assigns clientId to the appointment', async () => {
      const newPhone = '09127776655';
      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(newPhone, 'tok-upsert-client', Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);

      const input = createInput({
        clientName: 'سارا کاظمی',
        clientPhone: newPhone,
        verificationToken: 'tok-upsert-client',
        time: '19:00',
      });

      const res = await bookingRepo.submitBooking(input);
      expect(res.appointment.clientId).toBeDefined();

      const clients = mockStorage.getClients();
      const client = clients.find((c) => c.phone === newPhone);
      expect(client).toBeDefined();
      expect(client?.name).toBe('سارا کاظمی');
      expect(client?.status).toBe('new');
      expect(res.appointment.clientId).toBe(client?.id);
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/phase4Automation.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { MockAutomationRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAutomationRepository';
import { MockAppointmentRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import { integrationRegistry } from '../integrations/registry';

describe('Phase 4: Automations and Integrations', () => {
  let automationRepo: MockAutomationRepository;
  let appointmentRepo: MockAppointmentRepository;
  const clinicId = SEED_CLINIC.id;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    automationRepo = new MockAutomationRepository();
    appointmentRepo = new MockAppointmentRepository();
  });

  describe('1. Automation Health reflects channel provider status', () => {
    it('computes automation healthy as false when its channel provider is disconnected', async () => {
      // Disconnect SMS provider
      integrationRegistry.updateStatus('sms', 'disconnected');

      const automations = await automationRepo.getAutomations(clinicId);
      const smsAutomations = automations.filter((a) => a.channel.includes('پیامک'));

      // If SMS provider is disconnected, SMS-based automations must NOT be healthy
      for (const a of smsAutomations) {
        expect(a.healthy).toBe(false);
      }

      // Reconnect SMS provider
      integrationRegistry.updateStatus('sms', 'connected');
      const automationsAfter = await automationRepo.getAutomations(clinicId);
      const smsAutomationsAfter = automationsAfter.filter((a) => a.channel.includes('پیامک'));
      for (const a of smsAutomationsAfter) {
        expect(a.healthy).toBe(true);
      }
    });
  });

  describe('2. appointment.created event triggers outbox confirmation message', () => {
    it('creates an outbox message when appointment.created event is emitted', async () => {
      mockStorage.clearOutbox();

      await automationRepo.triggerEvent(clinicId, 'appointment.created', {
        name: 'شیدا فراهانی',
        phone: '09123334455',
        service: 'بوتاکس پیشانی',
        time: '18:30',
      });

      const outbox = mockStorage.getOutboxMessages();
      expect(outbox.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      const sentMessage = outbox.find((m) => m.recipientPhone === '09123334455');
      expect(sentMessage).toBeDefined();
      expect(sentMessage?.content).toContain('دریافت شد');
    });
  });

  describe('3. visit.completed event emission on appointment status completion', () => {
    it('emits visit.completed and creates survey message when appointment is marked completed', async () => {
      mockStorage.clearOutbox();
      const appointments = mockStorage.getAppointments();
      const testApt = appointments.find((a) => a.status === 'in_clinic');
      expect(testApt).toBeDefined();

      await appointmentRepo.updateStatus(clinicId, testApt!.id, 'completed', {
        userId: 'usr-admin',
        clinicId,
        role: 'admin',
        userName: 'مدیر',
        phone: '09121110002',
      });

      const outbox = mockStorage.getOutboxMessages();
      // Should have generated survey message for visit.completed
      const surveyMessage = outbox.find(
        (m) => m.recipientPhone === testApt!.clientPhone && m.content.includes('رضایت')
      );
      expect(surveyMessage).toBeDefined();
    });
  });

  describe('4. 24-Hour Reminder Scheduling for distant appointments', () => {
    it('schedules reminder with status pending and scheduledFor when appointment is > 24 hours away', async () => {
      mockStorage.clearOutbox();

      // Submit an appointment scheduled for 3 days from now
      const futureDate = new Date();
      futureDate.setDate(futureDate.getDate() + 3);
      const futureDateStr = futureDate.toISOString().split('T')[0];

      await automationRepo.triggerEvent(clinicId, 'appointment.reminder_24h', {
        name: 'مریم احمدی',
        phone: '09121112233',
        service: 'فیلر لب',
        time: '14:00',
        appointmentDateTime: `${futureDateStr}T14:00:00Z`,
      });

      const outbox = mockStorage.getOutboxMessages();
      const reminderMsg = outbox.find((m) => m.recipientPhone === '09121112233');
      expect(reminderMsg).toBeDefined();
      expect(reminderMsg?.scheduledFor).toBeDefined();
      expect(reminderMsg?.status).toBe('pending');
    });
  });

  describe('5. Enable/Disable Automation Toggle', () => {
    it('prevents sending messages when automation rule is disabled', async () => {
      mockStorage.clearOutbox();

      // Disable appointment.created rule
      await automationRepo.setEnabled(clinicId, 'auto-booking-created', false);

      const automations = await automationRepo.getAutomations(clinicId);
      const rule = automations.find((a) => a.id === 'auto-booking-created');
      expect(rule?.enabled).toBe(false);

      // Trigger event while disabled
      await automationRepo.triggerEvent(clinicId, 'appointment.created', {
        name: 'ناهید نادری',
        phone: '09128881122',
        service: 'هایفوتراپی',
        time: '16:00',
      });

      const outbox = mockStorage.getOutboxMessages();
      const message = outbox.find((m) => m.recipientPhone === '09128881122');
      expect(message).toBeUndefined(); // Did not send because rule was disabled
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/phase5UxAndDeadButtons.test.tsx ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { MockAppointmentRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import { ErrorState } from '../components/common/ErrorState';
import { ButtonLink } from '../components/ui/ButtonLink';
import { DemoBanner } from '../components/layout/DemoBanner';
import { NextActionsList } from '../features/dashboard/components/NextActionsList';
import { LeadsView } from '../features/leads/LeadsView';
import { AppointmentsView } from '../features/appointments/AppointmentsView';
import { AppProviders } from '../app/providers';
import type { DashboardNextAction } from '../domain/dashboard';

describe('Phase 5: Dead Buttons, UX Polish & Error States', () => {
  const clinicId = SEED_CLINIC.id;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
  });

  describe('1. ErrorState component', () => {
    it('renders Persian error message and triggers retry', () => {
      let retried = false;
      render(
        <ErrorState
          title="خطا در ارتباط"
          message="سرور در دسترس نیست"
          onRetry={() => {
            retried = true;
          }}
        />
      );

      expect(screen.getByText('خطا در ارتباط')).toBeTruthy();
      expect(screen.getByText('سرور در دسترس نیست')).toBeTruthy();
      const retryBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /تلاش مجدد/ });
      fireEvent.click(retryBtn);
      expect(retried).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('2. ButtonLink component', () => {
    it('renders a valid link without nested button element', () => {
      const { container } = render(
        <MemoryRouter>
          <ButtonLink to="/test" variant="primary">
            تست دکمه لینک
          </ButtonLink>
        </MemoryRouter>
      );

      const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'تست دکمه لینک' });
      expect(link).toBeTruthy();
      expect(link.getAttribute('href')).toBe('/test');
      expect(container.querySelector('button')).toBeNull(); // No invalid nested <button>
    });
  });

  describe('3. NextActionsList navigation routes', () => {
    it('links to /clients?status=at_risk, /leads, and /appointments with date/status', () => {
      const actions: DashboardNextAction[] = [
        {
          id: 'a1',
          priority: 'high',
          title: 'پیگیری مشتریان در خطر',
          description: 'توضیحات',
          value: '120000',
          valueLabel: 'ارزش',
          actionType: 'call_clients',
        },
        {
          id: 'a2',
          priority: 'medium',
          title: 'پاسخ به سرنخ‌ها',
          description: 'توضیحات',
          value: '50%',
          valueLabel: 'نرخ',
          actionType: 'reply_leads',
        },
        {
          id: 'a3',
          priority: 'low',
          title: 'تأیید نوبت‌های فردا',
          description: 'توضیحات',
          value: '30%',
          valueLabel: 'کاهش',
          actionType: 'confirm_tomorrow',
        },
      ];

      render(
        <MemoryRouter>
          <NextActionsList actions={actions} />
        </MemoryRouter>
      );

      const links = screen.getAllByRole('link', { name: /شروع اقدام/ });
      expect(links[0].getAttribute('href')).toBe('/clients?status=at_risk');
      expect(links[1].getAttribute('href')).toBe('/leads');
      expect(links[2].getAttribute('href')).toBe(
        '/appointments?date=tomorrow&status=pending_confirmation'
      );
    });
  });

  describe('4. LeadsView dynamic status badge', () => {
    it('displays status badge corresponding to lead.status', async () => {
      // Modify one lead to 'contacted'
      const leads = mockStorage.getLeads();
      leads[0].status = 'contacted';
      mockStorage.saveLeads(leads);

      const queryClient = new QueryClient({
        defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } },
      });

      render(
        <MemoryRouter>
          <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
            <AppProviders>
              <LeadsView />
            </AppProviders>
          </QueryClientProvider>
        </MemoryRouter>
      );

      // Should show 'تماس گرفته‌شده' instead of all 'سرنخ داغ'
      const contactedBadge = await screen.findByText('تماس گرفته‌شده');
      expect(contactedBadge).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  describe('5. Reception Manual Appointment Client Upsert', () => {
    it('upserts a client record when creating an appointment manually', async () => {
      const appointmentRepo = new MockAppointmentRepository();
      const services = mockStorage.getServices();
      const doctors = mockStorage.getDoctors();

      const newPhone = '09129998877';
      const newName = 'کیانا راد';

      const initialClients = mockStorage.getClients();
      expect(initialClients.find((c) => c.phone.includes('9998877'))).toBeUndefined();

      const apt = await appointmentRepo.createAppointment(clinicId, {
        clinicId,
        date: '2026-10-25',
        time: '11:00',
        clientName: newName,
        clientPhone: newPhone,
        serviceId: services[0].id,
        doctorId: doctors[0].id,
        source: 'reception_manual',
      });

      expect(apt.clientId).toBeDefined();

      const updatedClients = mockStorage.getClients();
      const createdClient = updatedClients.find((c) => c.phone === newPhone || c.id === apt.clientId);
      expect(createdClient).toBeDefined();
      expect(createdClient?.name).toBe(newName);
    });
  });

  describe('6. DemoBanner for mock mode', () => {
    it('renders demo notice when env.dataSource is mock', () => {
      render(<DemoBanner />);
      expect(
        screen.getByText('نسخه‌ی نمایشی — داده‌ها فقط در مرورگر شما ذخیره می‌شوند.')
      ).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  describe('7. AppointmentsView Date Filtering & Jalali Formatting', () => {
    it('renders Jalali formatted date and supports today/tomorrow/all filters', async () => {
      const queryClient = new QueryClient({
        defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } },
      });

      render(
        <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/appointments?date=today']}>
          <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
            <AppProviders>
              <AppointmentsView />
            </AppProviders>
          </QueryClientProvider>
        </MemoryRouter>
      );

      // Should have filter buttons
      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'امروز' })).toBeTruthy();
      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'فردا' })).toBeTruthy();
      expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'همه' })).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });
});


=================== ./src/test/phase6HttpContract.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  toAppointmentCreateDto,
  toCheckConflictDto,
  toBookingSubmissionDto,
  toOutboxMessageDto,
  toAuditLogDto,
  mapAppointmentDtoToDomain,
  mapClientDtoToDomain,
  mapLeadDtoToDomain,
  mapServiceDtoToDomain,
  mapDoctorDtoToDomain,
} from '../services/adapters/http/mappers';
import type {
  AppointmentDto,
  ClientDto,
  LeadDto,
  ServiceDto,
  DoctorDto,
} from '../services/adapters/http/mappers';
import type { CreateAppointmentInput } from '../domain/appointment';
import type { BookingSubmissionInput } from '../services/repositories/IBookingRepository';

describe('Phase 6: HTTP Wire Contract & Snake_Case DTO Mappers', () => {
  describe('1. Request Mappers (Domain -> Snake_Case DTO on the Wire)', () => {
    it('toAppointmentCreateDto maps camelCase input to snake_case wire payload', () => {
      const input: CreateAppointmentInput = {
        clinicId: 'ariana',
        date: '2026-10-25',
        time: '14:30',
        clientName: 'مریم سپهری',
        clientPhone: '09121112233',
        serviceId: 'srv-1',
        doctorId: 'doc-1',
        source: 'reception_manual',
        notes: 'مراجعه اول',
      };

      const dto = toAppointmentCreateDto(input);

      expect(dto.clinic_id).toBe('ariana');
      expect(dto.appointment_date).toBe('2026-10-25');
      expect(dto.appointment_time).toBe('14:30');
      expect(dto.client_name).toBe('مریم سپهری');
      expect(dto.client_phone).toBe('09121112233');
      expect(dto.service_id).toBe('srv-1');
      expect(dto.doctor_id).toBe('doc-1');
      expect(dto.source).toBe('reception_manual');
      expect(dto.notes).toBe('مراجعه اول');
    });

    it('toCheckConflictDto converts camelCase parameters to snake_case', () => {
      const dto = toCheckConflictDto({
        doctorId: 'doc-42',
        date: '2026-10-26',
        time: '10:00',
        durationMinutes: 45,
      });

      expect(dto.doctor_id).toBe('doc-42');
      expect(dto.date).toBe('2026-10-26');
      expect(dto.time).toBe('10:00');
      expect(dto.duration_minutes).toBe(45);
    });

    it('toBookingSubmissionDto maps booking inputs including captcha and phone token', () => {
      const input: BookingSubmissionInput = {
        clinicId: 'ariana',
        serviceId: 'srv-botox',
        doctorId: 'doc-niloufar',
        date: '2026-10-28',
        time: '11:00',
        clientName: 'نیما فلاح',
        clientPhone: '09129998877',
        notes: 'توضیحات تست',
        captchaToken: 'captcha-token-123',
        verificationToken: 'phone-token-456',
      };

      const dto = toBookingSubmissionDto(input);

      expect(dto.clinic_id).toBe('ariana');
      expect(dto.service_id).toBe('srv-botox');
      expect(dto.doctor_id).toBe('doc-niloufar');
      expect(dto.date).toBe('2026-10-28');
      expect(dto.time).toBe('11:00');
      expect(dto.client_name).toBe('نیما فلاح');
      expect(dto.client_phone).toBe('09129998877');
      expect(dto.notes).toBe('توضیحات تست');
      expect(dto.captcha_token).toBe('captcha-token-123');
      expect(dto.verification_token).toBe('phone-token-456');
    });

    it('toOutboxMessageDto maps outgoing message fields to snake_case', () => {
      const dto = toOutboxMessageDto({
        clinicId: 'ariana',
        recipientPhone: '09121112233',
        recipientName: 'علی رضایی',
        channel: 'sms',
        content: 'یادآوری نوبت شما فردا ساعت ۱۰',
        status: 'pending',
        triggerEvent: 'appointment.confirmed',
        scheduledFor: '2026-10-27T08:00:00Z',
      });

      expect(dto.clinic_id).toBe('ariana');
      expect(dto.recipient_phone).toBe('09121112233');
      expect(dto.recipient_name).toBe('علی رضایی');
      expect(dto.channel).toBe('sms');
      expect(dto.content).toBe('یادآوری نوبت شما فردا ساعت ۱۰');
      expect(dto.status).toBe('pending');
      expect(dto.trigger_event).toBe('appointment.confirmed');
      expect(dto.scheduled_for).toBe('2026-10-27T08:00:00Z');
    });

    it('toAuditLogDto maps audit log entry params to snake_case', () => {
      const dto = toAuditLogDto({
        clinicId: 'ariana',
        userId: 'u-1',
        userName: 'مدیر سیستم',
        role: 'owner',
        action: 'appointment.status_change',
        details: 'تغییر نوبت به تأیید شده',
      });

      expect(dto.clinic_id).toBe('ariana');
      expect(dto.user_id).toBe('u-1');
      expect(dto.user_name).toBe('مدیر سیستم');
      expect(dto.role).toBe('owner');
      expect(dto.action).toBe('appointment.status_change');
      expect(dto.details).toBe('تغییر نوبت به تأیید شده');
    });
  });

  describe('2. Response Mappers (Snake_Case DTO from Wire -> Domain)', () => {
    it('mapAppointmentDtoToDomain correctly translates snake_case DTO to camelCase domain', () => {
      const dto: AppointmentDto = {
        id: 'apt-1',
        clinic_id: 'ariana',
        tracking_code: 'MF-1001',
        appointment_date: '2026-10-20',
        appointment_time: '16:00',
        client_name: 'رویا کمالی',
        client_phone: '09125556677',
        service_id: 'srv-1',
        service_name: 'فیلر لب',
        doctor_id: 'doc-1',
        doctor_name: 'دکتر سپهری',
        duration_minutes: 30,
        price: 2500000,
        status: 'confirmed',
        source: 'online_booking',
        medical_notes: 'سابقه تبخال ندارد',
        created_at: '2026-10-18T10:00:00Z',
        updated_at: '2026-10-18T10:00:00Z',
      };

      const domain = mapAppointmentDtoToDomain(dto);

      expect(domain.id).toBe('apt-1');
      expect(domain.clinicId).toBe('ariana');
      expect(domain.trackingCode).toBe('MF-1001');
      expect(domain.date).toBe('2026-10-20');
      expect(domain.time).toBe('16:00');
      expect(domain.durationMinutes).toBe(30);
      expect(domain.medicalNotes).toBe('سابقه تبخال ندارد');
      expect(domain.status).toBe('confirmed');
    });

    it('mapClientDtoToDomain and mapLeadDtoToDomain map correctly', () => {
      const clientDto: ClientDto = {
        id: 'c-1',
        clinic_id: 'ariana',
        name: 'فرزانه نوری',
        phone: '09128889900',
        status: 'at_risk',
        total_visits: 3,
        total_spent: 4500000,
        last_visit_date: '2026-07-01',
        recovery_value: 3000000,
        created_at: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',
      };
      const client = mapClientDtoToDomain(clientDto);
      expect(client.totalVisits).toBe(3);
      expect(client.totalSpent).toBe(4500000);
      expect(client.recoveryValue).toBe(3000000);
      expect(client.lastVisitDate).toBe('2026-07-01');

      const leadDto: LeadDto = {
        id: 'l-1',
        clinic_id: 'ariana',
        name: 'هدی حسینی',
        phone: '09127778899',
        interested_service: 'لیزر کندلا',
        status: 'hot',
        is_decision_ready: true,
        score: 95,
        source: 'اینستاگرام',
        estimated_value: 1200000,
        created_at: '2026-10-18T12:00:00Z',
      };
      const lead = mapLeadDtoToDomain(leadDto);
      expect(lead.interestedService).toBe('لیزر کندلا');
      expect(lead.isDecisionReady).toBe(true);
      expect(lead.estimatedValue).toBe(1200000);
    });

    it('mapServiceDtoToDomain and mapDoctorDtoToDomain map catalog entities', () => {
      const srvDto: ServiceDto = {
        id: 's-1',
        clinic_id: 'ariana',
        name: 'پاکسازی پوست',
        duration_minutes: 60,
        price: 800000,
        category: 'مراقبت پوست',
        is_active: true,
      };
      const srv = mapServiceDtoToDomain(srvDto);
      expect(srv.duration).toBe(60);
      expect(srv.isActive).toBe(true);

      const docDto: DoctorDto = {
        id: 'd-1',
        clinic_id: 'ariana',
        name: 'دکتر احمدی',
        specialty: 'پوست و مو',
        start_hour: 10,
        end_hour: 18,
        slot_interval_minutes: 30,
        avatar_url: '/avatars/doc.jpg',
        is_active: true,
      };
      const doc = mapDoctorDtoToDomain(docDto);
      expect(doc.startHour).toBe(10);
      expect(doc.endHour).toBe(18);
      expect(doc.slotIntervalMinutes).toBe(30);
      expect(doc.avatar).toBe('/avatars/doc.jpg');
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/phone.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { normalizePhoneNumber, isValidIranianMobile, formatPhoneNumber } from '../lib/phone';

describe('Iranian Mobile Normalization and Validation', () => {
  it('normalizes Persian digits to standard English ASCII mobile format', () => {
    expect(normalizePhoneNumber('۰۹۱۲۴۵۶۷۸۹۰')).toBe('09124567890');
    expect(normalizePhoneNumber('۰۹۱۹۲۳۴۱۱۰۸')).toBe('09192341108');
  });

  it('normalizes international prefixes +98, 0098, 98 and removes spaces/dashes', () => {
    expect(normalizePhoneNumber('+98 912 456 7890')).toBe('09124567890');
    expect(normalizePhoneNumber('0098-912-456-7890')).toBe('09124567890');
    expect(normalizePhoneNumber('989124567890')).toBe('09124567890');
  });

  it('validates Iranian mobile numbers accurately', () => {
    expect(isValidIranianMobile('09124567890')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidIranianMobile('۰۹۳۵۷۷۱۰۳۴۵')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidIranianMobile('+989101126773')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidIranianMobile('02188884321')).toBe(false); // Landline
    expect(isValidIranianMobile('0912345')).toBe(false); // Incomplete
    expect(isValidIranianMobile('abc')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('formats phone numbers for Persian UI display with spaced groups', () => {
    expect(formatPhoneNumber('09124567890')).toBe('۰۹۱۲ ۴۵۶ ۷۸۹۰');
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/refactorLogicFixes.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { MockBookingRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockBookingRepository';
import { MockAppointmentRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAppointmentRepository';
import { calculateAvailableSlots } from '../domain/slot';
import { canTransition } from '../domain/appointmentStatus';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import type { Doctor } from '../domain/doctor';
import type { Actor } from '../domain/session';

describe('Logical Bug Fixes & Refactoring Verification', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
  });

  // Bug 1: State Machine Deadlock for Reception & Clinical Exit Flow
  describe('1. Reception Exit & Settlement Transitions', () => {
    it('allows reception to transition from in_clinic to completed and pending_payment', () => {
      expect(canTransition('in_clinic', 'completed', 'reception')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransition('in_clinic', 'pending_payment', 'reception')).toBe(true);
      expect(canTransition('in_clinic', 'cancelled', 'reception')).toBe(true);
    });

    it('allows reception to settle pending_payment to completed', () => {
      expect(canTransition('pending_payment', 'completed', 'reception')).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  // Bug 2: Variable-Duration Time Slot Grid & Interval Collision Logic
  describe('2. Variable-Duration Time Slot Grid & Collision Logic', () => {
    const mockDoctor: Doctor = {
      id: 'd-test-var',
      clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
      name: 'دکتر جراحی',
      specialty: 'پوست و مو',
      startHour: 10,
      endHour: 13, // 10:00 to 13:00 (180 mins)
      slotIntervalMinutes: 30,
      isActive: true,
    };

    it('rigorously checks collision for multi-slot treatments (e.g. 90 mins) against active bookings', () => {
      // Existing 45-minute appointment at 10:30 to 11:15
      const existing = [
        {
          id: 'apt-overlap',
          clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
          trackingCode: 'AR-OVERLAP',
          date: '2026-10-25',
          time: '۱۰:۳۰', // Persian digits!
          clientName: 'بیمار همپوشان',
          clientPhone: '09121111111',
          serviceId: 's1',
          serviceName: 'بوتاکس',
          doctorId: 'd-test-var',
          doctorName: 'دکتر جراحی',
          durationMinutes: 45,
          price: 1500000,
          status: 'confirmed' as const,
          source: 'online_booking' as const,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
      ];

      // A 90-minute treatment starting at 10:00 will run until 11:30 -> overlaps with 10:30-11:15!
      const slotsFor90MinService = calculateAvailableSlots({
        doctor: mockDoctor,
        serviceDurationMinutes: 90,
        existingAppointments: existing,
        date: '2026-10-25',
      });

      // 10:00 slot (10:00 - 11:30) overlaps with 10:30 - 11:15 -> MUST be unavailable
      const slot1000 = slotsFor90MinService.find((s) => s.rawTime === '10:00');
      expect(slot1000?.available).toBe(false);
      expect(slot1000?.reason).toBe('رزرو شده');

      // 10:30 slot (10:30 - 12:00) overlaps with 10:30 - 11:15 -> MUST be unavailable
      const slot1030 = slotsFor90MinService.find((s) => s.rawTime === '10:30');
      expect(slot1030?.available).toBe(false);

      // 11:00 slot (11:00 - 12:30) overlaps with 10:30 - 11:15 -> MUST be unavailable
      const slot1100 = slotsFor90MinService.find((s) => s.rawTime === '11:00');
      expect(slot1100?.available).toBe(false);

      // 11:30 slot (11:30 - 13:00) does NOT overlap (11:15 <= 11:30) -> MUST be available
      const slot1130 = slotsFor90MinService.find((s) => s.rawTime === '11:30');
      expect(slot1130?.available).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  // Bug 3: Patient Identity Collision (Shared Family Mobile Numbers)
  describe('3. Patient Identity Modeling (Shared Family Numbers)', () => {
    it('creates distinct client profiles when family members share a mobile phone with different names', async () => {
      const repo = new MockBookingRepository();
      const sharedPhone = '09129998877';

      // 1. Mother books appointment
      const motherToken = 'token-mother-123';
      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(sharedPhone, motherToken);

      const booking1 = await repo.submitBooking({
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        doctorId: 'd1',
        serviceId: 's1',
        date: '2026-11-01',
        time: '18:00',
        clientName: 'مریم رضایی',
        clientPhone: sharedPhone,
        verificationToken: motherToken,
        captchaToken: 'mock-captcha-token',
      });

      // 2. Daughter books appointment with same phone number
      const daughterToken = 'token-daughter-456';
      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(sharedPhone, daughterToken);

      const booking2 = await repo.submitBooking({
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        doctorId: 'd1',
        serviceId: 's1',
        date: '2026-11-01',
        time: '19:00',
        clientName: 'سارا رضایی',
        clientPhone: sharedPhone,
        verificationToken: daughterToken,
        captchaToken: 'mock-captcha-token',
      });

      // Verify that appointments have distinct client IDs
      expect(booking1.appointment.clientId).not.toBe(booking2.appointment.clientId);
      expect(booking1.appointment.clientName).toBe('مریم رضایی');
      expect(booking2.appointment.clientName).toBe('سارا رضایی');

      // Verify clients storage contains both records and links daughter to mother's profile
      const allClients = mockStorage.getClients();
      const motherClient = allClients.find((c) => c.name === 'مریم رضایی');
      const daughterClient = allClients.find((c) => c.name === 'سارا رضایی');

      expect(motherClient).toBeDefined();
      expect(daughterClient).toBeDefined();
      expect(motherClient?.id).not.toBe(daughterClient?.id);
      expect(daughterClient?.parentClientId).toBe(motherClient?.id);
    });
  });

  // Bug 4: OTP Verification Token Race Condition & Double-Submit Protection
  describe('4. OTP Verification Token Race Condition Protection', () => {
    it('locks token atomically so concurrent submissions with the same token fail with race protection', async () => {
      const phone = '09125554433';
      const token = 'token-race-test';
      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(phone, token);

      // Verify lockToken locks atomically
      const lockedFirst = mockStorage.lockToken(phone, token);
      expect(lockedFirst).toBe(true);

      // Second attempt to lock concurrently must fail
      const lockedSecond = mockStorage.lockToken(phone, token);
      expect(lockedSecond).toBe(false);

      // Release lock
      mockStorage.releaseToken(token);
      const lockedAfterRelease = mockStorage.lockToken(phone, token);
      expect(lockedAfterRelease).toBe(true);
    });

    it('rejects double submission with already consumed or locked token', async () => {
      const repo = new MockBookingRepository();
      const phone = '09124443322';
      const token = 'token-double-submit';
      mockStorage.saveVerificationToken(phone, token);

      // First submission succeeds
      await repo.submitBooking({
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
        doctorId: 'd1',
        serviceId: 's1',
        date: '2026-11-05',
        time: '14:00',
        clientName: 'کاربر تستی',
        clientPhone: phone,
        verificationToken: token,
        captchaToken: 'mock-captcha-token',
      });

      // Second submission with same token fails immediately
      await expect(
        repo.submitBooking({
          clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
          doctorId: 'd1',
          serviceId: 's1',
          date: '2026-11-05',
          time: '15:00',
          clientName: 'کاربر تستی',
          clientPhone: phone,
          verificationToken: token,
          captchaToken: 'mock-captcha-token',
        })
      ).rejects.toThrow('اعتبارسنجی شماره تلفن همراه نامعتبر است، منقضی شده، یا در حال پردازش می‌باشد');
    });
  });

  // Bug 5: Audit Log Privacy Leak Prevention
  describe('5. Audit Log Privacy Leak Prevention', () => {
    it('records only sanitized metadata in audit log details and never exposes medical notes payload', async () => {
      const repo = new MockAppointmentRepository();
      const doctorActor: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-naderi',
        userName: 'دکتر نادری',
        role: 'doctor',
        doctorId: 'd1',
        clinicId: SEED_CLINIC.id,
      };

      const sensitiveNote = 'بیمار دارای آلرژی شدید به لیدوکائین و سابقه جراحی بینی است.';

      // Get an appointment for d1 in SEED_CLINIC
      const apts = await repo.getAppointments(SEED_CLINIC.id, undefined, doctorActor);
      const targetApt = apts.find((a) => a.doctorId === 'd1');
      expect(targetApt).toBeDefined();

      // Update medical notes
      await repo.updateMedicalNotes(SEED_CLINIC.id, targetApt!.id, sensitiveNote, doctorActor);

      // Check audit logs
      const auditLogs = mockStorage.getAuditLogs().filter((l) => l.clinicId === SEED_CLINIC.id);
      const noteUpdateLog = auditLogs.find((l) => l.action === 'medical_notes.update');

      expect(noteUpdateLog).toBeDefined();
      expect(noteUpdateLog?.details).toContain(targetApt!.trackingCode || targetApt!.id);
      // STRICT ASSERTION: Sensitive note text MUST NOT exist in audit log details
      expect(noteUpdateLog?.details).not.toContain('آلرژی');
      expect(noteUpdateLog?.details).not.toContain('لیدوکائین');
      expect(noteUpdateLog?.details).not.toContain(sensitiveNote);
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/roleHierarchy.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { MockAuthRepository } from '../services/adapters/mock/MockAuthRepository';
import { mockStorage } from '../services/adapters/mock/mockStorage';
import { SEED_CLINIC } from '../mocks/seed';
import type { Actor } from '../domain/session';

describe('Role Hierarchy & Admin Enforcement Rules', () => {
  let authRepo: MockAuthRepository;
  const clinicId = SEED_CLINIC.id;

  const ownerActor: Actor = {
    userId: 'usr-owner',
    userName: 'دکتر کاوه آریا',
    role: 'owner',
    clinicId,
  };

  const adminActor: Actor = {
    userId: 'usr-admin',
    userName: 'مریم کمالی',
    role: 'admin',
    clinicId,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockStorage.resetToSeed();
    authRepo = new MockAuthRepository();
  });

  describe('Admin Restrictions', () => {
    it('prevents admin from creating a user with owner role', async () => {
      await expect(
        authRepo.createUser(
          clinicId,
          {
            name: 'مالک جدید',
            phone: '09128881111',
            role: 'owner',
            roleTitle: 'مالک',
          },
          adminActor
        )
      ).rejects.toThrow('مدیر عملیات مجاز به ایجاد کاربر با نقش مدیر ارشد یا مدیر عملیات نمی‌باشد.');
    });

    it('prevents admin from creating a user with admin role', async () => {
      await expect(
        authRepo.createUser(
          clinicId,
          {
            name: 'ادمین جدید',
            phone: '09128882222',
            role: 'admin',
            roleTitle: 'مدیر سیستم',
          },
          adminActor
        )
      ).rejects.toThrow('مدیر عملیات مجاز به ایجاد کاربر با نقش مدیر ارشد یا مدیر عملیات نمی‌باشد.');
    });

    it('allows admin to create a reception user', async () => {
      const newUser = await authRepo.createUser(
        clinicId,
        {
          name: 'پذیرش جدید',
          phone: '09128883333',
          role: 'reception',
          roleTitle: 'پذیرش کلینیک',
        },
        adminActor
      );
      expect(newUser.id).toBeDefined();
      expect(newUser.role).toBe('reception');
    });

    it('prevents admin from modifying role of an existing owner or admin', async () => {
      // Trying to change owner's role
      await expect(
        authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, 'usr-owner', 'reception', adminActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('مدیر عملیات مجاز به ویرایش نقش مدیر ارشد یا سایر مدیران نمی‌باشد.');
    });

    it('prevents admin from toggling active status of an owner or admin', async () => {
      // Trying to deactivate owner
      await expect(
        authRepo.toggleUserStatus(clinicId, 'usr-owner', adminActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('مدیر عملیات مجاز به تغییر وضعیت حساب مدیر ارشد یا سایر مدیران نمی‌باشد.');
    });

    it('prevents admin from promoting someone to owner or admin', async () => {
      await expect(
        authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, 'usr-sara', 'owner', adminActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('مدیر عملیات مجاز به ارتقای کاربران به نقش مدیر ارشد یا مدیر عملیات نمی‌باشد.');
    });
  });

  describe('Self-Action & Demotion Safeguards', () => {
    it('prevents any user from changing their own role', async () => {
      await expect(
        authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, 'usr-admin', 'reception', adminActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('امکان تغییر سطح دسترسی حساب کاربری خودتان وجود ندارد.');

      await expect(
        authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, 'usr-owner', 'doctor', ownerActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('امکان تغییر سطح دسترسی حساب کاربری خودتان وجود ندارد.');
    });

    it('prevents any user from deactivating their own account', async () => {
      await expect(
        authRepo.toggleUserStatus(clinicId, 'usr-admin', adminActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('امکان غیرفعال‌سازی حساب کاربری خودتان وجود ندارد.');

      await expect(
        authRepo.toggleUserStatus(clinicId, 'usr-owner', ownerActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('امکان غیرفعال‌سازی حساب کاربری خودتان وجود ندارد.');
    });

    it('prevents deactivating or demoting the last remaining owner', async () => {
      // Create a second owner first
      const owner2 = await authRepo.createUser(
        clinicId,
        {
          name: 'مالک دوم',
          phone: '09127778899',
          role: 'owner',
          roleTitle: 'مالک دوم',
        },
        ownerActor
      );

      // Now we have 2 owners. Demoting owner2 should work
      await expect(
        authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, owner2.id, 'reception', ownerActor)
      ).resolves.not.toThrow();

      // Now only 1 owner left (usr-owner). Deactivating the last owner should fail
      const adminOther: Actor = {
        userId: 'usr-admin-other',
        role: 'owner',
        clinicId,
      };
      await expect(
        authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, 'usr-owner', 'reception', adminOther)
      ).rejects.toThrow('امکان تنزل نقش آخرین مدیر ارشد (Owner) کلینیک وجود ندارد.');

      await expect(
        authRepo.toggleUserStatus(clinicId, 'usr-owner', adminOther)
      ).rejects.toThrow('امکان غیرفعال‌سازی آخرین مدیر ارشد (Owner) کلینیک وجود ندارد.');
    });
  });

  describe('Doctor Role DoctorId Requirement', () => {
    it('requires doctorId when creating user with role doctor', async () => {
      await expect(
        authRepo.createUser(
          clinicId,
          {
            name: 'پزشک جدید',
            phone: '09124445566',
            role: 'doctor',
            roleTitle: 'پزشک معالج',
          },
          ownerActor
        )
      ).rejects.toThrow('انتخاب پزشک معالج برای نقش پزشک متخصص الزامی است.');
    });

    it('successfully creates doctor user when doctorId is provided', async () => {
      const doc = await authRepo.createUser(
        clinicId,
        {
          name: 'پزشک جدید',
          phone: '09124445566',
          role: 'doctor',
          roleTitle: 'پزشک معالج',
          doctorId: 'd1',
        },
        ownerActor
      );
      expect(doc.role).toBe('doctor');
      expect(doc.doctorId).toBe('d1');
    });

    it('requires doctorId when changing existing user role to doctor', async () => {
      await expect(
        authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, 'usr-sara', 'doctor', ownerActor)
      ).rejects.toThrow('تخصیص شناسه پزشک معالج برای نقش پزشک الزامی است.');
    });

    it('successfully updates user to doctor role when doctorId is provided', async () => {
      const updated = await authRepo.updateUserRole(clinicId, 'usr-sara', 'doctor', ownerActor, 'd2');
      expect(updated.role).toBe('doctor');
      expect(updated.doctorId).toBe('d2');
    });
  });
});

=================== ./src/test/setup.ts ===================
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

=================== ./src/test/slot.test.ts ===================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { calculateAvailableSlots } from '../domain/slot';
import type { Doctor } from '../domain/doctor';
import type { Appointment } from '../domain/appointment';

describe('Available Time Slots Calculation', () => {
  const mockDoctor: Doctor = {
    id: 'd1',
    clinicId: 'c1',
    name: 'دکتر تست',
    specialty: 'پوست',
    startHour: 9,
    endHour: 12,
    slotIntervalMinutes: 60,
    isActive: true,
  };

  it('generates all slots as available when no existing appointments exist', () => {
    const slots = calculateAvailableSlots({
      doctor: mockDoctor,
      serviceDurationMinutes: 60,
      existingAppointments: [],
      date: '2026-10-20',
    });

    // 09:00, 09:30, 10:00, 10:30, 11:00 (with step <= 30 min and 60 min service)
    expect(slots.length).toBe(5);
    expect(slots.every((s) => s.available)).toBe(true);
    expect(slots[0].rawTime).toBe('09:00');
  });

  it('marks a slot as unavailable if there is an overlapping active appointment', () => {
    const existing: Appointment[] = [
      {
        id: 'apt-test',
        clinicId: 'c1',
        trackingCode: 'AR-1000',
        date: '2026-10-20',
        time: '۰۹:۰۰',
        clientName: 'مراجع تست',
        clientPhone: '09120000000',
        serviceId: 's1',
        serviceName: 'بوتاکس',
        doctorId: 'd1',
        doctorName: 'دکتر تست',
        durationMinutes: 60,
        price: 1000,
        status: 'confirmed',
        source: 'online_booking',
        createdAt: '2026-10-20T08:00:00Z',
        updatedAt: '2026-10-20T08:00:00Z',
      },
    ];

    const slots = calculateAvailableSlots({
      doctor: mockDoctor,
      serviceDurationMinutes: 60,
      existingAppointments: existing,
      date: '2026-10-20',
    });

    // 09:00 (09:00-10:00) overlaps with apt (09:00-10:00) -> unavailable
    expect(slots[0].available).toBe(false);
    expect(slots[0].reason).toBe('رزرو شده');
    // 09:30 (09:30-10:30) overlaps with apt (09:00-10:00) -> unavailable
    expect(slots[1].available).toBe(false);
    // 10:00 (10:00-11:00) does not overlap -> available
    expect(slots[2].available).toBe(true);
  });

  it('ignores cancelled appointments when checking collision', () => {
    const existing: Appointment[] = [
      {
        id: 'apt-cancelled',
        clinicId: 'c1',
        trackingCode: 'AR-1000',
        date: '2026-10-20',
        time: '۰۹:۰۰',
        clientName: 'مراجع لغوشده',
        clientPhone: '09120000000',
        serviceId: 's1',
        serviceName: 'بوتاکس',
        doctorId: 'd1',
        doctorName: 'دکتر تست',
        durationMinutes: 60,
        price: 1000,
        status: 'cancelled',
        source: 'online_booking',
        createdAt: '2026-10-20T08:00:00Z',
        updatedAt: '2026-10-20T08:00:00Z',
      },
    ];

    const slots = calculateAvailableSlots({
      doctor: mockDoctor,
      serviceDurationMinutes: 60,
      existingAppointments: existing,
      date: '2026-10-20',
    });

    expect(slots[0].available).toBe(true);
  });
});

import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'

// https://vite.dev/config/

  plugins: [react()],
  test: {
    globals: true,
    environment: 'jsdom',
    setupFiles: './src/test/setup.ts',

      VITE_DATA_SOURCE: 'mock',
